# DECISIONS — Lumina POS

Append-only log. Oldest at top, newest at bottom.

---

2026-06-09 — Backend = Supabase (Postgres + Auth + Storage), not self-hosted Node/NestJS — solo dev, speed, Firebase background; the Pipeline's NestJS/Redis/Argon2id/BullMQ specs are superseded.

2026-06-09 — Auth = Supabase Auth owns credentials/sessions/JWT; public.users is a profile (id FK auth.users); NO password stored in public.users.

2026-06-09 — Signup provisioning via handle_new_user trigger: business_name → new tenant + ADMIN/CASHIER roles + Main branch + permission matrix + branch assignment; Demo Store (fixed UUIDs) = fallback.

2026-06-09 — Email verification via custom SMTP (Supabase gates template editing behind SMTP); OTP codes via `{{ .Token }}`; verifyOTP type signup/recovery.

2026-06-09 — go_router redirect is the SINGLE source of truth for auth navigation; never navigate manually after auth; RecoveryState forces /reset during recovery.

2026-06-09 — RLS = Supabase-style via auth_tenant_id()/auth_role_name(); NOT the schema doc's SET LOCAL app.current_tenant_id pattern.

2026-06-09 — UI = clean native iOS, light mode only, accent #007AFF; the claymorphic attempt was abandoned.

2026-06-09 — Schema version-controlled in supabase/migrations/ (0001 init, 0002 signup_provisioning) — previously lived only in the cloud.

2026-06-09 — Prefer dependency-free code for tiny visual effects (flutter_inset_box_shadow was abandoned, broke the build via removed hashValues()).

2026-06-09 — Desktop-only plugins (e.g. window_manager) MUST be platform-guarded in main() or mobile crashes at launch.

2026-06-09 — Flagged paid/server-side: SMS MFA (paid provider), remote session kill (Edge Function with service_role), suspicious-login/anomaly engine (later).

2026-06-09 — Removed dead code: AuthUser entity, EmailVO/PasswordVO, ResponsiveExtension+breakpoints. AppOtpField gained optional onChanged param; OTP _autoSubmitted flag resets on error and on field edit so retry works.

2026-06-09 — Phase 0.2 auth schema applied as migration 20260609000002_auth_full_schema.sql: extended tenants/roles/users; added branches, user_branch_assignments, permissions, devices, sessions, mfa_configs, audit_logs (audit_logs immutable — update/delete revoked from anon/authenticated); RLS helpers auth_tenant_id()/auth_role_name() + RLS policies on all new tables; seeded Demo Store permission matrix (ADMIN full = 36, CASHIER = 6) + Main Branch (BR01) + branch assignments for existing users.

2026-06-09 — handle_new_user fallback role corrected ADMIN→CASHIER: non-business signups provision into Demo Store as CASHIER (role …012), matching the locked provisioning rule; business_name signups still get their own tenant as ADMIN. (Runbook draft assigned Demo ADMIN in the fallback branch — fixed before applying.)

2026-06-09 — Dynamic RBAC permission layer: Permission entity + loadPermissions datasource/repo/use case; permissionMatrixProvider (AsyncValue<Set<String>> with "module:action" keys for granted==true); canProvider (Provider.family) + PermissionController.can(); permissionsReadyProvider; AuthProfile gained roleId from existing profile query; matrix loaded in HomePage bootstrap after profile resolves.

2026-06-09 — Forgot-password flow hardened: RecoveryState bool → RecoveryStage enum (none/awaitingCode/codeVerified); forgot page validates email + waits for success before nav + sets awaitingCode; OTP controller no longer clears recovery stage on failure (stays on /otp with banner); otp_page surfaces empty-code hint + resend success; reset page validates min 8 chars + confirm-match before API call, stays on /reset on error; reset controller only clears to none on success; new AuthFailures: InvalidOtpFailure, PasswordMismatchFailure, RecoverySessionExpiredFailure; _mapError refined to distinguish wrong-code vs expired-code; resend cooldown starts only after success.

2026-06-09 — PermissionGate widget (lib/core/widgets/permission_gate.dart): ConsumerWidget watching permissionMatrixProvider, renders child if "module:action" granted, fallback otherwise (default SizedBox.shrink).

2026-06-09 — Branch support: Branch entity (id/name/code/isMain/isDefault); loadUserBranches(userId) datasource/repo/use case; userBranchesProvider (AsyncValue<List<Branch>>) + currentBranchProvider (NotifierProvider<Branch?>); branch selection persisted in flutter_secure_storage; hydrated on app start after profile resolves in home page; branchesReadyProvider.

2026-06-09 — Branch select screen + routing: /branch-select route listing branches as tappable AppCards; BranchRouterState ChangeNotifier signals needsSelection to the router; single-branch users auto-select and skip to /home; multi-branch redirect to /branch-select after login; tapping a branch persists choice and navigates to /home; redirect priority: codeVerified > awaitingCode/otp > !loggedIn > needsSelection > loggedIn.

2026-06-09 — Entry screens: /env-check (EnvironmentCheckScreen) runs internet + session checks, shows pending/ok/fail rows, auto-continues on pass; /workspace-init (WorkspaceInitScreen) triggers profile + permission + branch loading, shows "Setting up" spinner, auto-goes to /home when ready. Both wired as ChangeNotifier-driven router steps (EnvCheckState, WorkspaceInitState). Profile/permission/branch loading moved from HomePage to WorkspaceInitScreen.

2026-06-09 — PIN quick-login: PinService (sha256 + random salt, flutter_secure_storage + Supabase users.pin_hash sync); PinPad widget (0-9 grid, delete, dot dots, haptic feedback); PinSetupScreen (set + confirm 4-digit PIN); PinLockScreen (verify to unlock, 3 failed attempts clears PIN → /login); app-lock on resume via WidgetsBindingObserver in App; PinLockState ChangeNotifier for router redirect (pin_locked > loggedIn).

2026-06-09 — Biometric unlock: PinLockScreen shows "Use Face ID / fingerprint" button via local_auth when device supports biometrics + user enabled it in HomePage Security card; toggle persisted via PinService.isBiometricsEnabled/isBiometricsEnabled in secure storage; authenticate() on success unlocks without PIN.

2026-06-09 — Device registration: DeviceService gathers device name/model/OS via device_info_plus, builds sha256 fingerprint from model+OS+per-install UUID (stored in secure storage), upserts into public.devices (tenant_id, user_id, device_name, device_model, os_info, fingerprint_hash, last_seen_at) on workspace init after profile loads; AuthProfile gained tenantId from existing profile query.

2026-06-09 — Device management screen (/devices): admin-gated via PermissionGate(module:'settings', action:'update'); lists all tenant devices with trust badges (TRUSTED/REVOKED/PENDING) and OS-aware icons; ADMIN can Approve (authorized=true, trust_level=TRUSTED) or Revoke (REVOKED) each device; datasource methods loadDevices/approveDevice/revokeDevice; DevicesController with refresh after mutation.

2026-06-09 — TOTP MFA: MfaService wraps Supabase built-in MFA API (enroll/challenge/verify/unenroll, needsAal2 via getAuthenticatorAssuranceLevel, getEnrolledFactorId via listFactors). /mfa-enroll shows QR (qr_flutter) + secret + code entry. /mfa-challenge auto-challenges + verifies when currentLevel=1 and nextLevel=2. MfaState ChangeNotifier signals needsMfa to router (workspace-init → needsAal2 → /mfa-challenge → verify → home). HomePage Security card shows authenticator setup/status button. upserts public.mfa_configs on enroll/unenroll.

2026-06-11 — Home Security & Admin section: Replaced conditional _SecurityCard with always-visible _SecuritySection (AppCard with rows). PIN, biometric toggle, and authenticator rows visible to all roles. Admin rows (devices/sessions/security-logs) gated by PermissionGate. MFA-enroll always surfaced (fixes catch-22 where MFA button was hidden unless MFA already active). Layout: scrollable content between fixed header and logout button. Stable loading placeholder replaces flickering SizedBox.shrink().

2026-06-11 — Clean architecture for Devices: DeviceRepository (abstract) + DeviceRepositoryImpl → LoadDevices / ApproveDevice / RevokeDevice use cases → DevicesController. Controller no longer calls datasource directly. Datasource methods kept in auth_remote_datasource.

2026-06-11 — Clean architecture for Sessions: SessionRemoteDataSource (Edge Function wrapper) → SessionRepository + impl → ListSessions / RevokeSession use cases → SessionsController → ConsumerStatefulWidget screen. Supabase function calls moved out of UI layer.

2026-06-11 — Clean architecture for Security Logs: loadAuditLogs added to auth_remote_datasource → AuditLogRepository + impl → LoadAuditLogs use case → SecurityLogsController → ConsumerStatefulWidget screen.

2026-06-11 — Router-driven navigation cleanup: Removed manual context.go() after state changes in MfaChallengeScreen (clear MfaState), PinLockScreen (unlock PinLockState), BranchSelectPage (selectBranch). Redirect now handles all post-state-change navigation. PinSetupScreen keeps explicit nav (no redirect gate covers /pin-setup).

2026-06-11 — Minor correctness + docs: Profile double-load removed (_ProfileCard reads existing state). Audit service gains debugPrint + one retry. Orphaned authStateProvider + watch_auth_state use case deleted. AUTH_COMPLETE_RUNBOOK.md created. PROJECT_STATE.md fully reconciled.

2026-06-11 — Migration reconciliation: missing local file `20260611000000_failed_login_rpc.sql` recovered from remote DB via `supabase db query --linked`; local and remote migration lists now aligned.
2026-06-11 — Taxonomy management UI: categories + brands screens under lib/features/inventory/presentation/; list screens use plain Scaffold (not ResponsiveFormScaffold) to avoid infinite-width ListView crash; forms use Scaffold+AppBar+SingleChildScrollView; PermissionGate(module:'inventory') gates create/edit/delete; DuplicateSkuFailure surfaced as name-collision message in taxonomy forms.
2026-06-11 — Detail-screen nav fix: Settings rows use context.push (not context.go) for pin-setup, mfa-enroll, devices, sessions, security-logs. Sub-screens use Scaffold+AppBar with back button, not ResponsiveFormScaffold (avoids infinite-width ListView crash). Empty-state distinction: neutral empty state (muted icon + text) vs red error banner. /pin-setup removed from waitingRoutes so push isn't yanked. MFA enroll surfaces real error via debugPrint.

2026-06-11 — AppButton default fullWidth changed true→false (opt-in): stops infinite-width crash when placed inside Row/list items; form buttons in stretch-CrossAxisAlignment or ResponsiveFormScaffold remain full-width via parent layout; explicit fullWidth:true added to login/category-form/brand-form CTA buttons.

2026-06-11 — Products list with search + filters: ProductsController (Notifier loading/search), ProductsPage (plain Scaffold, AppTextField with controller-listener debounce 300ms, category/status filter chips via modal bottom sheets, product AppCards with name/sku/barcode/price/status chip), /inventory/products route + stub create/detail routes, inventory tab updated to link to products.

2026-06-11 — Product create/edit form: ProductEditController (Notifier with sub-resource state), ProductFormPage (core fields + collapsible sub-sections for variants/images/pricing via dialogs), routes /inventory/products/create and /inventory/products/:productId replacing stubs; sub-sections disabled until first core save; nested lists use Column of cards not ListView.
Inventory Slice A (Product Catalog) complete: full §3.4 schema + clean-arch inventory feature + Inventory tab; gated by inventory:* matrix; search via search_vector. Next: Slice B stock engine (warehouses, stock_balance, immutable stock_ledger).
2026-06-11 — Four inventory fixes: (1) product form dropdowns now watch AsyncValue for categories/brands; (2) delete controllers capture and surface (bool,failure) instead of swallowing errors; (3) SKU switched to system-generated via DB trigger (PRD-000001), removed from UI; (4) product search replaced FTS+ilike-or with trigram-accelerated ILIKE substring matching (pg_trgm GIN indexes + single .or() query).
2026-06-12 — Soft-delete moved to SECURITY DEFINER RPCs (soft_delete_brand/category/product) enforcing tenant + inventory:delete; duplicate catalog UPDATE policies collapsed to one per table; the earlier "client-side session loss" diagnosis was incorrect — the JWT was always valid, the fix required collapsing duplicate policies and bypassing the WITH CHECK via owner-privileged RPCs.

2026-06-13 — Stock Engine (Slice B) complete. Architecture: stock_balance is a trigger-maintained materialized projection of the immutable append-only stock_ledger (trg_apply_stock_ledger AFTER INSERT on stock_ledger). All stock writes go through the single post_stock_movement SECURITY DEFINER RPC — clients NEVER insert/update stock_balance or stock_ledger directly (RLS revokes insert/update/delete on both tables from anon+authenticated). Negative stock is blocked in fn_apply_stock_ledger (P0001 'insufficient stock'). Weighted-avg cost computed in the same trigger. Stock_ledger immutability enforced by trg_stock_ledger_immutable (raises P0001 on any UPDATE/DELETE). Branch isolation via auth_has_branch() helper. Warehouse scope via nullable warehouse_id on stock_balance; default warehouse per branch with uq_warehouses_one_default_per_branch partial index. Spec-gap fixes: chk qty_on_hand >= 0 added (negative BLOCKED, not allowed); warehouse seeding via ensure_default_warehouse RPC + seed INSERT; stock_ledger shipped non-partitioned (partitioning deferred to performance milestone). Supabase RLS (auth_tenant_id/auth_has_branch/auth_has_permission) supersedes the schema doc's SET LOCAL app.current_tenant_id pattern. Flutter data layer mirrors exact DB column names from migration 20260613061924_stock_engine.sql; all RPC results parsed as single-row Map (not List) — post_stock_movement and ensure_default_warehouse return single record types. Opening-balance form computes client-side delta = target - current_on_hand and hardcodes operation_type=OPENING_BALANCE, reference_type='OPENING'. Products list invalidation moved to controller saveProduct for reliability. Edit-product form seeding fixed: replaced didChangeDependencies with reactive ref.listen(productEditProvider) + _didSeed guard. Remaining stock operations (transfers, adjustments, counts, IMEI, scrap, valuation) deferred to Slice C.

2026-06-13 — Stock Ops (Slice C) complete. Migration 20260613075616_stock_ops.sql. All ops post through the Slice B ledger (post_stock_movement); negative blocked; projection invariant preserved. Adjustments: threshold-gated approval (inventory_settings.adjustment_approval_threshold → requires_approval boolean on row; inventory:approve permission gates the approve RPC); below-threshold auto-post within create_stock_adjustment RPC; WRITE_OFF/DAMAGE/THEFT/EXPIRED reasons with negative qty post as SCRAP movement type. Transfers: DRAFT→IN_TRANSIT→(PARTIALLY_RECEIVED)→RECEIVED state machine, or CANCELLED; dispatch posts TRANSFER_OUT (negative); receive posts TRANSFER_IN (positive) per item; cancel returns in-transit items via TRANSFER_IN; transfer numbers via number_series (TRF- prefix) + next_number RPC (SELECT FOR UPDATE). Counts: open snapshots system_qty from stock_balance into stock_count_items; record line updates counted/variance in real time; complete posts variance as ADJUSTMENT + stamps last_stock_take. IMEI: global-unique imei_records (uq_imei_records_imei), lifecycle via imei_status_enum (7 values), register_imei optionally posts +1 OPENING_BALANCE. Gap-fixes shipped: inventory_settings table + updateApprovalThreshold (minimal approval primitive — full approval_workflows engine deferred to its own module); number_series table + next_number RPC created here and reused (STOCK_TRANSFER/STOCK_COUNT prefixes seeded); imei_id FK added to stock_ledger (was deferred Slice B gap). Flutter controllers mirror Slice B patterns: AsyncNotifier per aggregate, build() loads for branch, mutations invalidate self + stockLevels. Inventory module (Slices A–C) complete. Next: Sales module (POS) or Purchasing.

2026-06-15 — Barcode scanning via mobile_scanner ^7.2.0. Shared `scanBarcode()` helper + `BarcodeScanPage` widget in `lib/core/widgets/` (full-screen camera preview, scan-window overlay, torch toggle, 1.5s detect debounce, manual-entry fallback via sentinel, permission-denied screen). Platform guard: `barcodeScanSupported` getter in `lib/core/services/scanner_support.dart` returns true only on `!kIsWeb && (iOS || Android)` — desktop/web degrade silently to manual entry. Scan buttons wired into 4 inventory screens: product search (sets search text + triggers debounced search), product form barcode field, IMEI lookup, and count session (matches scanned SKU/barcode to count line, scrolls + focuses quantity). Native permissions declared: `NSCameraUsageDescription` in iOS Info.plist, `CAMERA` + `uses-feature` in Android manifest.

2026-06-15 — Label printing completes workflow §6.6. Packages `pdf`/`printing`/`barcode` added. `LabelPdfService` in `lib/features/inventory/data/services/` renders template-sized labels per product on an A4 grid (CODE128/EAN13/QR with SKU fallback; EAN13 validates 13-digit format, degrades to CODE128 on mismatch). Products page gains multi-select mode gated by `inventory:export` (ADMIN only); selection flows into `LabelPrintPage` (template picker defaulting to `isDefault`, per-product quantity controls, Preview/Print via `Printing.layoutPdf` native sheet). `BarcodeTemplate` layout JSON controls field visibility (showName/showBarcode/showPrice). Route `/inventory/labels` carries selected product IDs as extra.

2026-06-16 — Notifications domain (§3.13) implemented. Migration `20260615082538_notifications_low_stock.sql`: `notifications` table (title, body, channel/priority/status enums, action_type/action_id for deep-linking, metadata jsonb, read_at) + `notification_preferences` table (per-user, per-event-type toggles, default enabled). Database trigger `trg_low_stock_notify` on `stock_balance` fires on downward qty_on_hand crossing at/below effective reorder_point (stock_balance.reorder_point override else product.reorder_point); dedupes per product+branch while an unread notification exists; inserts one row per tenant ADMIN respecting `notification_preferences.enabled`. `mark_notification_read(p_id)` SECURITY DEFINER RPC; `markAllRead` via client update (RLS: `notif update own`). Flutter: `lib/features/notifications/` clean-arch feature folder (`NotificationRemoteDataSource` → `NotificationRepository` → `NotificationsController` AsyncNotifier + `unreadCountProvider` FutureProvider). `NotificationsPage` list with priority chip, unread dot, time-ago timestamps; tap low_stock → deep-links to `/inventory/stock/:actionId`; "Mark All Read" button. Inventory hub AppBar gains bell icon with unread badge (destructive counter, 99+ cap). Route `/inventory/notifications`.

2026-06-16 — Bulk product import via `file_picker` + `csv` packages. `ImportProductsPage`: pick .csv → parse headers → column-mapping UI with smart auto-map (header.toLowerCase() substring match on target fields) + per-column dropdown (target field or Ignore); preview first 20 rows in DataTable with missing-name rows flagged; "Import N products" → `jsonEncode` → `ProductsController.bulkImport()` → RPC `bulk_import_products(p_rows jsonb)`. RPC is SECURITY DEFINER, gated by `inventory:create`; per-row try/catch with `{ok, failed, errors[{row, error}]}` return; resolves category/brand by name (case-insensitive, tenant-scoped); SKU auto-generated by DB trigger; non-null columns default (unit_of_measure→PCS, cost_price→0, selling_price→0). Route `/inventory/import`.

2026-06-16 — Migration import M1 data layer in `lib/features/migration_import/`. Mirrors clean-arch pattern from inventory. 4 RPCs: migrate_import_categories/brands/products/stock. ImportResult entity with ok/failed/errors. Reuses InventoryFailure. Datasource calls `.rpc()` with params, parses result as Map (not .first). Repository impl maps exceptions to UnknownFailure. 4 thin use cases each with its own Provider.

2026-06-17 — Migration import M2 controller + M3 UI. `MigrationImportController` (Notifier) with `pickAndParse(kind)` — file_picker + csv decode + header→allowed-field pass-through (strips tenant_id/created_at); `run(kind, branchId?)` — jsonEncode → use case → invalidate relevant inventory providers. `MigrationImportPage` — 4 ordered FK-gated step cards (Categories→Brands→Products→Stock Balances); each card: Choose CSV, preview table (10 rows), Import N rows button, result line with expandable error list. Steps 2-4 disabled until previous step completed. Stock imports into current branch. Gate: PermissionGate(inventory:create). Route `/inventory/import-migration`; hub row in InventoryHubPage under "Data" section.

2026-06-17 — Migration import bugfixes: (a) `file_picker` .bytes is null on macOS without `withData:true` — added `withData:true` + `utf8.decode(allowMalformed:true)` + path fallback in both migration controller and F4 import page. (b) Button hang: controller `run()` had no try/finally — added try/finally guaranteeing `running=false` always, plus `failure` field in state + `AppInlineBanner` in page. (c) jsonb params: datasource was sending `jsonEncode(...)` as a Dart String, making Postgres receive text (not jsonb) — changed all 4 datasource methods + use cases + repo to pass `List<Map<String,dynamic>>` directly. Same fix applied to F4 `bulkImportProducts` across datasource/repo/use case/controller/page.

2026-06-16 — Voice search via `speech_to_text ^7.4.0`. `voiceSearchSupported` guard in `lib/core/services/voice_support.dart` (iOS/Android only, hidden on desktop/web). `VoiceInputService` wraps `SpeechToText` instance: lazy init, `listen()` returns `Future<String?>` via `Completer` with 5s timeout + `SpeechListenOptions(listenMode: dictation, pauseFor: 2s)`, streams partial transcripts → `onPartial` callback. Mic icon in products search bar (gated by `voiceSearchSupported`): while listening shows red mic + "Listening… '[partial]'" subtitle, partial text live-populates search field, final result triggers `ProductsController.search()`. Native permissions: `NSMicrophoneUsageDescription` + `NSSpeechRecognitionUsageDescription` in Info.plist, `RECORD_AUDIO` in Android manifest.

2026-06-18 — Sales DB foundation (migration 20260618114258_sales_foundation): tables customers, cashier_sessions, invoices, invoice_items, payments + RLS; RPCs create_sale, open_cashier_session, close_cashier_session; invoice-immutability trigger; INVOICE number_series seeded (prefix INV-, per tenant). create_sale runs inside an open cashier session, decrements stock via post_stock_movement (SALE, negative qty, reference SALE→invoice), captures stock_balance.avg_cost as invoice_items.cost_price, enforces credit-needs-customer, returns single Map. IMEI sold via imei_records.sold_invoice_id + status='SOLD'. No sales-permission backfill (sales:* already seeded for ADMIN/CASHIER). Returns gate on sales:update, void on sales:delete. Operation-aware post_stock_movement gate (20260619115907) applied: SALE/RETURN_IN accept sales perms; other movements still inventory:update.

2026-06-18 — Sales S2 data+domain layer under lib/features/sales/. Clean-arch mirror of inventory: 7 entities, sealed SalesFailure (11 variants), abstract SalesRepository, 6 models, SalesRemoteDataSource (3 RPCs), SalesRepositoryImpl, 7 use cases, 4 controllers. No UI pages yet.

2026-06-18 — Sales S3 POS terminal cash flow + session UI + receipt PDF/share. Pages: OpenSessionPage, CloseSessionPage, PosTerminalPage (split-pane product search+cart), PaymentSheet (cash only), SaleSuccessPage, ReceiptPage. ReceiptPdfService: 80mm PDF (pdf+printing stack). New packages: share_plus, url_launcher. Routes: /sales/pos, /sales/open, /sales/session/close, /sales/payment, /sales/success, /sales/receipt. Bottom-nav tab: Sale→Sales. Gate: PermissionGate(sales:create). Scan reuses S0-F6 scanBarcode helper.

2026-06-18 — Sales S4 customers + split payment + credit. customer_picker_sheet bottom-sheet (search/Walk-in/inline create). PaymentSheet multi-payment rows (method dropdown, amount, reference); client-side credit block. SaleSuccessPage status chip (PAID/PARTIALLY_PAID/CREDIT). POS header customer chip.

2026-06-18 — Sales S5 history + detail. sales_history_page: InvoicesController list with status filter chips + invoice-number client-side search; per-row status chip + balance flag. invoice_detail_page: full detail with items table, payments list, Reprint (ReceiptPdfService+Printing.layoutPdf), Share (Printing.sharePdf). Routes: /sales/history, /sales/invoice/:id. POS AppBar history button.

2026-06-18 — Sales S6 returns. sales_return_page: scan/search invoice → load lines → per-line return qty → reason picker → refund → CreateSalesReturn RPC. Data layer: createSalesReturn. Route /sales/return, gated by PermissionGate(sales:update).

2026-06-18 — Sales R2: POS routes gated by Sales permissions. All 9 sales pages wrapped in PermissionGate with NoAccessScaffold fallback. Bottom-nav Sales tab hidden via permission-based destination filtering.

2026-06-18 — Sales R5: product tax applied at checkout. pos_terminal_page._addLine reads products.tax_rate + tax_inclusive; tax_inclusive backing-out yields ex-tax unit_price for CartLine. Cart footer shows Subtotal/Discount/Tax/Total breakdown. Fixed footer bug (Subtotal was displaying grandTotal). create_sale RPC already accepts tax_pct per item — no RPC change needed.

2026-06-18 — Sales R6A hold/resume. held_sale entity + model; datasource holdSale/loadHeldSales/deleteHeldSale on held_sales table; 3 use cases; PosCartController.hold() serializes cart→cart_json + clears; resume() deserializes+deletes held row. UI: Hold button (label dialog) in cart footer; Resume button in POS AppBar (held_sales_sheet list).

2026-06-18 — Sales R6B void. voidInvoice datasource (.rpc('void_invoice')), repo, use case, InvoicesController.voidInvoice(). invoice_detail_page: Void button gated by PermissionGate(sales:delete), confirm dialog + reason, snackbar + pop on success. Voided invoices hidden from void action.

2026-06-18 — Sales V1 DEFERRALS (built later as their own phases): delivery_orders, loyalty_transactions (+ customers.loyalty_points wiring), tax_rules table, payment_methods config table, customer_groups, pricing-tier engine (wholesale/retail tier selection beyond min_selling_price), offline sync (is_offline/synced_at/correlation_id columns exist but no sync logic).

2026-06-18 — Sales V1 core COMPLETE. Applied operation-aware post_stock_movement gate (SALE/RETURN_IN accept sales perms; other movements still inventory:update) — cashier sales work. create_sale enforces min_selling_price + credit_limit, overridable by sales:approve (20260619124544). POS routes permission-gated. Hold/resume (R6A) + void (R6B) built. End-to-end: ADMIN cashier session → POS cart → search/scan → customer picker → multi-payment cash/credit → create_sale → stock decrement → receipt PDF/share → history → void/return. Next: Purchasing module.

2026-06-19 — Sales SF1: DB-backed open-session resolution fixes cold-start dead-end. SessionController.build() queries cashier_sessions via loadOpenSession, depends on currentBranchProvider. OpenSessionPage shows Resume/Close card when session exists; on ERR_SESSION_ALREADY_OPEN invalidates provider. CloseSessionPage shows full variance from RPC (expected/closing/variance/sales/txns). POS banner shows relative time + staleness chip (>12h). SessionAlreadyOpenFailure wording per-cashier. Datasource loadOpenSession already existed; added repo method + LoadOpenSession use case.

2026-06-19 — Sales SF2 polish: negative opening-float blocked (client-side validation + banner). Receipt WhatsApp share text fix (dangling "Total: PKR" removed).

2026-06-19 — Sales SF3 cart autosave. WidgetsBindingObserver on POS terminal: on AppLifecycleState.paused, auto-holds non-empty cart via existing held_sales flow ("Auto-saved HH:MM"). On resumed, checks for auto-saved carts and shows dismissible banner "Auto-saved cart available — tap Resume". Empty carts never auto-held.

2026-06-19 — Sales SF4 POS fixes: (1) createCustomer datasource payload now includes tenant_id (RLS insert fix). (2) Narrow layout: products take full body, cart in bottom sheet via FAB button (item count + total). Wide/landscape side-by-side preserved. (3) Product cards show stock qty chip (N, Low, Out) from stockLevelsProvider; out-of-stock disabled. (4) Banner live sales: loadSessionSales sums invoice grand_totals for current session, replaces stale session.totalSales.

2026-06-19 — Dashboard D1 data layer: DashboardSummary entity (todaySales/todayTxns/todayProfit/receivables/stockValue/lowStockCount + nested RecentSale/TrendPoint/paymentBreakdown). DashboardSummaryModel fromJson. DashboardRemoteDataSource .rpc('dashboard_summary'). DashboardRepository + DashboardFailure. LoadDashboardSummary use case. DashboardController AsyncNotifier (build reads currentBranchProvider, refresh = invalidateSelf).

2026-06-19 — Dashboard D2 UI. Replaced Dashboard placeholder with full page: PermissionGate(reports:read), pull-to-refresh, KPI card grid (2-col, 6 cards), recent sales list (status chip + tap → invoice detail), quick-launch buttons (POS/Inventory/History gated by permission matrix). Branch-aware via controller dependency on currentBranchProvider.

2026-06-19 — Dashboard D3 charts. Added fl_chart (mature, Apache-licensed, lightweight chart lib). 7-day sales trend bar chart (BarChart with PKR tooltips, AppColors.accent bars). Payment-method breakdown pie chart + legend (PieChart, AppColors palette). Both placed between KPI grid and recent sales.

2026-06-19 — Dashboard V1 ships: today's sales/profit/txns, receivables, stock value, low-stock, recent sales, 7-day trend, payment breakdown via dashboard_summary RPC. DEFERRED: payables + cash/bank balances (no purchasing/accounting module yet), P&L/balance-sheet, drilldown reports, scheduled/email reports, configurable KPI grid — all from Pipeline M10, deferred to a Reporting phase.

2026-06-19 — POS bugfix round: (1) cart sheet now watches provider live (ref.watch instead of frozen snapshot), +/-/X rebuild instantly. (2) banner loads live sales on entry + session change (not just lifecycle resume). (3) same-product addLine deduplicates (qty+1 instead of new line). (4) product search capped at 50 rows (searchProducts limit, loadProducts unchanged for inventory page).

2026-06-20 — Products card stock fix: card was showing product.reorderPoint labeled "Stock:" (all=10 from migration). Now fetches real qty_on_hand from stock_balance (warehouse_id IS NULL, branch-scoped) via batch query loadProductsStock, merged into Product.qtyOnHand field. Card shows actual stock with Low (warning) / Out (destructive) / — (no stock row) labels.
2026-06-20 — Products stock merge fix v2: loadProductsStock was using inFilter('product_id', [8294 UUIDs]) → 307KB URL → HTTP 414 → error swallowed → all "—". Replaced with branch-wide query (eq branch_id + isFilter warehouse_id null), no inFilter needed. Controller now ref.watch currentBranchProvider (rebuilds on branch resolve) and throws on stock failure (no silent swallowing).

2026-06-24 — close_cashier_session hardened (migration 20260624185804_close_cashier_session_permission_gate): added auth_has_permission('sales','create') gate + own-session-only restriction (session.cashier_id = auth.uid()), overridable by sales:approve (manager). Tenant scope, variance math and return Map unchanged. Closes audit CRITICAL #1 — it was the one ungated sales RPC.

2026-06-24 — Dashboard failure surfacing: DashboardController propagates failure as AsyncError instead of returning a zero-filled summary; dashboard_page shows AppInlineBanner + retry. Prevents a false "no sales today" on RPC error. (Built during Dashboard V1; logged retroactively — closes audit item #16.)

2026-06-24 — PinLockState reset on user change (audit C.1): resetUserScopedState() now calls PinLockState.instance.unlock() alongside the other user-scoped singleton resets. Was the one user-scoped state not cleared on sign-out — user A's PIN lock survived (process-lifetime singleton) into user B's session and trapped B behind A's lock via the router redirect. Same-user resume-lock (_checkPinLock on AppLifecycleState.resumed) unaffected.

2026-06-24 — Shared formatPkr currency formatter (audit C.2): new pure-Dart util lib/core/design/format.dart — formatPkr(num) → 'PKR 1,234.50' (2 decimals + thousands grouping, sign-safe). Replaced ~28 inline 'PKR ${x.toStringAsFixed(n)}' sites across 13 files (all sales pages + dashboard + products_page + receipt_pdf_service + label_pdf_service) that had inconsistent precision (same grandTotal showed (0) in POS FAB vs (2) at checkout). Display-only, no arithmetic changed. Own code, no intl dependency (intl is transitive-only; CLAUDE.md prefers own code over undeclared package). Non-money toStringAsFixed (qty/%/stock/input-seeds/internal costs) left untouched.

2026-06-24 — MFA moved to clean-arch + typed verify (audit D.1): deleted lib/core/services/mfa_service.dart (a const singleton calling supabase.auth.mfa.* directly with catch(_){return false;}). New MfaRemoteDataSource holds all supabase.auth.mfa.* + mfa_configs upsert; MfaRepository/Impl maps exceptions → typed AuthFailure; 6 usecases (verify/enroll/challenge/getEnrolledFactorId/needsAal2/unenroll). verify() now returns AuthFailure? — null=success, InvalidOtpFailure=wrong/expired code (AuthApiException), new MfaTransientFailure=network/unknown. Mapping order is critical: AuthRetryableFetchException (a subclass of AuthException) is checked BEFORE AuthApiException so a network fault no longer renders as "Incorrect code" — the actual bug (flaky connection = permanent MFA lockout). mfa_challenge/enroll screens → ConsumerStatefulWidget, show f.message (connection banner for transient, "Incorrect code" only for real rejection); settings_page + workspace_init read usecases. No supabase call remains outside the datasource.

2026-06-24 — Core services behind datasources (audit D.2): pin/device/audit/login_throttle services no longer call supabase.from()/rpc() directly. All calls moved into auth_remote_datasource (insertAuditLog, increment/resetFailedLogin, registerDevice, updatePinHash/selectPinHash); mapped through repositories to typed failures via shared core/error/supabase_error_mapper.dart (PostgrestException/AuthException → ServerErrorFailure — previously a dead type, now used). Extended DeviceRepository + AuditLogRepository; new PinRepository + LoginThrottleRepository. The 6 silent catch(_){} / catch(_){return false/null} are replaced with map-to-failure + debugPrint — background writes stay fire-and-forget but visible. Services kept as singletons (smallest blast radius — many .instance callers) but now compose a repo built from the global supabase client, injectable for testing. Committed one-per-service (audit/throttle/device/pin) for bisectability. No supabase.from/.rpc remains in lib/core/services.

2026-06-24 — POS live-sales off direct datasource read (audit D.3): pos_terminal_page no longer calls ref.read(salesRemoteDataSourceProvider).loadSessionSales directly with catch(_){} (silent PKR 0.00 on error). Added SalesRepository.loadSessionSales → LoadSessionSales use case → sessionSalesProvider (FutureProvider.autoDispose.family<double,String> keyed by session id; throws the mapped SalesFailure). _SessionBanner is now a ConsumerWidget that watches the provider: data→formatPkr, loading→'Sales: …', error→'Sales: —' in destructive colour (no silent zero). Removed _liveSales/_lastSessionId/_loadLiveSales; session-change refetch is automatic via the family key, resume invalidates the provider, post-sale refetch happens on the fresh POS page (sale_success go('/sales/pos')). Sum logic (invoices.grand_total) unchanged.

2026-06-24 — Two swallowed failures surfaced (audit D.5): (1) count_session_page._record no longer catch(_){} — it catches the typed InventoryFailure thrown by recordItem, sets _error (existing AppInlineBanner) and re-selects the field's typed qty so a failed stock-count entry is retained + retryable, not silently lost. (2) pos_cart_controller.loadHeld no longer collapses a failure to []; CartState gained a heldError field + copyWith, loadHeld records the failure there; held_sales_sheet reads posCartProvider.heldError and shows an error banner (distinct from the "No held sales." empty state). loadHeld's List signature kept so pos_terminal._checkAutoSaved is unaffected. product_edit_controller left untouched (its (_, failure) already returns the failure).

2026-06-24 — Products pagination (audit F.1): inventory_remote_datasource.loadProducts was unbounded and silently truncated at Supabase's ~1000-row cap (8,294 products at Ahmad Store looked complete but weren't). Added page/pageSize (default 200) with .range(from, from+pageSize-1) threaded datasource→repo→usecase. ProductsController tracks _page/_hasMore/_isSearching + stored filters; build() and the browse path load page 0, loadMore() appends the next page (merges branch-wide stock, returns InventoryFailure? so the page can surface it). products_page adds a ScrollController that calls loadMore near the bottom (browse only), shows a trailing spinner, and a SnackBar on loadMore failure (list kept). Search path unchanged (capped 50, no paging). loadProductsStock untouched (branch-wide, warehouse_id IS NULL) so stock chips stay correct across pages.

2026-06-25 — Schema-doc reconciliation (audit G): DATABASE_SCHEMA.md gained the two as-built utility tables missing from it — product_sku_sequences (under 3.4, per-tenant SKU counter, SECURITY DEFINER only, no client RLS) and inventory_settings (under 3.7, tenant thresholds for the stock-adjustment approval flow). Added a top note marking the doc as the TARGET 89-table schema (37 built as of 2026-06-25); the as-built set is supabase/migrations/ + PROJECT_STATE.md. Docs-only, no schema change.

2026-07-11 — Set Opening Stock canonical-warehouse fix (audit H): stock_movement_form_page seeded _selectedWarehouseId from the real default-warehouse UUID (ensureDefaultWarehouse), then read AND wrote at that non-canonical warehouse_id. Read via eq(uuid) matched 0 of the tenant's all-NULL canonical stock_balance rows → showed on-hand 0 for stocked products; the form is ABSOLUTE (delta = target − currentOnHand) so a false 0 corrupts the posted qty_change; the write would have landed a non-null warehouse_id ledger row invisible to every other screen (the qty_on_hand == Σ ledger invariant still holds, so nothing would flag it). Never fired (DB: 0 non-null warehouse_id ledger rows). Fix: removed the warehouse concept from the form entirely — reads/writes canonical warehouseId: null, deleted _WarehousePicker (its "None (branch default)" option was itself broken — Navigator.pop without setState, could never clear the UUID) + orphaned imports/field; _loadCurrentBalance now surfaces a read failure as an error banner instead of masking it as 0 (deleted the no-op empty-result branch). Also fixed the latent twin: loadStockBalances + loadProductLedger emitted NO warehouse_id predicate when warehouseId==null (rows across all warehouses); now isFilter('warehouse_id', null), matching loadProductsStock/loadStockLevels. Design: no screen can display non-null-warehouse stock, so multi-warehouse write paths are traps until a warehouse-aware stock view exists.

2026-07-11 — receive_goods enum-cast fix (migration 20260711101802): the PO status advance did `set status = case when v_all_received then 'RECEIVED' else 'PARTIALLY_RECEIVED' end` — a CASE over untyped string literals is typed text, and text→purchase_order_status_enum has no implicit assignment cast, so every GRN receive raised 42804. Found when the first GRN receive ran (in a rolled-back dry-run, before any real receipt existed). Fix = cast the CASE to `::purchase_order_status_enum`; shipped as a forward CREATE OR REPLACE (idempotent, zero data change) rather than editing the already-applied 20260711100014. Verified against the deployed function in a rolled-back tx (impersonated ADMIN via jwt claims): 2-line PO → submit → approve → receive 6/10 → PARTIALLY_RECEIVED, GRN row warehouse_id NULL, one +6 PURCHASE_RECEIPT ledger row, qty_on_hand == Σ ledger, 0 GRN ledger rows with non-null warehouse_id.

2026-07-11 — Purchase foundation (migration purchase_foundation_suppliers_perms): suppliers table (schema §3.8) with tenant-read + purchase-gated write RLS; enums supplier_status_enum/purchase_order_status_enum/purchase_invoice_status_enum; NEW 'purchase' permission module (6 actions) backfilled to all ADMIN roles + a roles AFTER-INSERT trigger seeds it for future tenants (no handle_new_user rewrite); seeded PURCHASE_ORDER/GRN/PAYMENT_VOUCHER number series (PO-/GRN-/PV-, default padding 6) for all tenants. Suppliers = client CRUD like customers; soft-delete via deleted_at.

2026-07-11 — Suppliers CRM feature (lib/features/suppliers/) built as its own clean-arch folder mirroring sales/customers: Supplier entity + SupplierStatus, SupplierLedger/PayablesAging entities from the two read RPCs, sealed SupplierFailure (PermissionDenied/NotFound/Unknown), abstract SuppliersRepository, 7 thin usecases, SupplierModel (toInsert/toUpdate whitelist writable cols + tenant_id/created_by on insert for the RLS gate, mirroring the customers tenant_id fix), SuppliersRemoteDataSource (all supabase calls: loadSuppliers ILIKE name/phone + status filter + deleted_at is null + order(name), load/insert/update/softDelete, rpc supplier_ledger/payables_aging), repo impl maps PostgrestException/AuthException → typed failures. Presentation: SuppliersController (AsyncNotifier list + search + status filter + create/edit/remove — folded into the list controller mirroring customers/brands rather than a separate SupplierFormController, which would only duplicate the invalidate wiring) + supplierLedger/payablesAging FutureProviders; SuppliersPage (search, status chips, payable hint from payables_aging, FAB gated purchase:create), SupplierFormPage (grouped fields, Scaffold+AppBar not ResponsiveFormScaffold — the latter has no back button, consistent with every other form since 2026-06-11), SupplierDetailPage (header + supplier_ledger balance + entry timeline + Record Payment TODO hook until supplier-payments ships). Top-level routes /suppliers[/create|/:id|/:id/edit]; Suppliers row on the Inventory hub under a Purchasing section until a Purchase hub exists. DB already migrated (no new migration). Verified against live RLS gates (impersonated ADMIN, rolled back): insert→list→edit→soft-delete hides→empty ledger without error. flutter analyze clean.

2026-07-11 — Purchase orders (migration purchase_orders_and_rpcs): purchase_orders + purchase_order_items (schema §3.6, verbatim); client-read RLS, writes via SECURITY DEFINER RPCs. create_purchase_order (jsonb items; computes line_total, unit_cost_base = unit_cost×exchange_rate, subtotal/tax_total, landed_cost = freight+insurance+duty allocated to lines by line_total share, grand_total; po_number via next_number('PURCHASE_ORDER',branch)); update_purchase_order (DRAFT only); submit/approve(purchase:approve, sets approved_by/at)/cancel state machine. PO-above-threshold auto-approval deferred (no purchase threshold config yet).

2026-07-11 — Purchase invoices + supplier payments (migration purchase_invoices_and_payments): purchase_invoices + supplier_payments (schema §3.6, verbatim; supplier_payments.bank_account_id has NO FK — bank_accounts is M07). create_purchase_invoice (purchase:create; validates GRN belongs to PO; total = amount+tax; balance = total; returns 3-way match_variance vs PO grand_total; flips PO → INVOICED). record_supplier_payment (purchase:create; full/partial; blocks overpayment; voucher via next_number('PAYMENT_VOUCHER'); updates invoice paid_amount/balance, flips PAID at zero balance). Payables tracked on invoice.balance + supplier.opening_balance (no GL until M07).

2026-07-11 — Supplier ledger + payables aging (migration purchase_supplier_ledger_aging): supplier_ledger(purchase:read) returns opening balance + invoice-debit/payment-credit entries with running balance; payables_aging(purchase:read) buckets open invoice balances by due-date age (current/1-30/31-60/61-90/90+) tenant-wide + by supplier (schema §8.5). Read-only, no GL.

2026-07-11 — Purchasing Flutter feature (lib/features/purchasing/) — full lifecycle UI, clean-arch mirroring suppliers/sales. Domain: 6 entities + PurchaseOrderStatus/PurchaseInvoiceStatus enums + 5 RPC result types; sealed PurchaseFailure (PermissionDenied/BadTransition/OverReceipt/Overpayment/GrnMismatch/NotFound/Unknown) mapped from RPC ERR_* codes; abstract PurchasingRepository; 13 usecases. Data: 6 table models + result parsers; ONE PurchaseRemoteDataSource (all selects tenant-scoped + deleted_at filter; 8 RPCs — create/update/submit/approve/cancel PO, receive_goods, create_purchase_invoice, record_supplier_payment); repo impl → typed failures. Presentation: PurchaseOrdersController (list+status filter+create/edit/submit/approve/cancel/receive — receive returns ReceiveResult; note: mutating methods that carry RPC data the UI needs return (Result?, Failure?) not just Failure?), purchaseOrderDetailProvider (record {po,items}) + poGrnsProvider, PurchaseInvoicesController (create returns InvoiceCreateResult for match_variance) + invoicePaymentsProvider, PurchasePaymentsController. 10 pages built by 3 parallel subagents against a shared contract: PurchaseHubPage, PurchaseOrders list, PO form (supplier picker + inline product-search multi-line editor + charges + live totals mirroring the RPC math; edit DRAFT-only; accepts initialLines from Reorder), PO detail (status-driven gated actions Submit/Approve/Cancel/Receive/Create-Invoice + linked GRNs/invoices), GrnReceivePage (per-line qty/reject/batch/expiry; IMEI capture via scanBarcode for products where type==SERIALIZED — there is NO boolean serialized flag; no warehouse picker), PurchaseInvoiceMatchPage (3-way match, shows match_variance), PurchaseInvoices list + detail (payment history + Record Payment), SupplierPaymentPage (method=payment_method_enum, blocks overpayment, bank_account_id always null), ReorderSuggestionsPage (products at/under reorder_point → tap builds a new PO, never auto-creates). Routes /purchasing/* top-level + a 5th "Purchase" bottom-nav branch gated purchase:read (order: Dashboard/Inventory/Purchase/Sales/Settings); suppliers "Record Payment" wired to /purchasing/payments/create. Two receive_goods DB bugs found while verifying and fixed as forward migrations: enum-cast (20260711101802, earlier) and imei status 'IN_STOCK'→'AVAILABLE' (20260711111535 — imei_status_enum has no IN_STOCK, so every serialized receipt raised 22P02). Verified DB lifecycle end-to-end (impersonated ADMIN, rolled back): PO 2-line → submit → approve → serialized receive (stock +2, 2 imei_records AVAILABLE) → RECEIVED → invoice (match_variance) → partial+final payment → PAID → ledger/aging reflect. flutter analyze clean (only pre-existing infos in auth/sales). Device run pending (flutter run).

2026-07-11 — Purchase returns (SCHEMA EXTENSION; migrations purchase_return_number_series_enum + purchase_returns): NEW tables purchase_returns + purchase_return_items and purchase_return_status_enum, styled to §3.6; PURCHASE_RETURN number series (PR-). create_purchase_return (purchase:update; inner post_stock_movement enforces inventory:update) atomically reverses stock via RETURN_OUT at canonical warehouse_id=NULL, validates qty against received−prior-returns, marks returned IMEIs, records credit value; does NOT mutate po_items/PO/invoice by default (invoice-balance reduction opt-in via p_reduce_invoice). supplier_ledger now nets CONFIRMED returns as credits. Not in the 89-table target schema — documented as an extension.
2026-07-11 — Purchase returns Flutter feature (lib/features/purchasing/, no new folder): mirrors PO/GRN clean-arch. Domain PurchaseReturn + PurchaseReturnItem + PurchaseReturnStatus(draft/confirmed/cancelled) + ReturnCreateResult; sealed PurchaseFailure gained ReturnExceedsReceived/ImeiCountMismatch/ImeiNotFound (err_invoice_mismatch reuses GrnMismatch; imei_not_found checked BEFORE the generic _not_found). 4 usecases (LoadPurchaseReturns/LoadPurchaseReturn/LoadReturnedQtysForPo/CreatePurchaseReturn); datasource loadPurchaseReturns/loadPurchaseReturn(+items)/loadReturnedQtysForPo (embedded purchase_returns!inner filter on po_id+status=CONFIRMED, summed per po_item client-side)/rpc create_purchase_return with p_reduce_invoice hardcoded false (opt-in not surfaced). PurchaseReturnsController (list+status filter+create→ReturnCreateResult, invalidateSelf) + purchaseReturnDetailProvider + poReturnedQtysProvider. Pages: PurchaseReturnsPage (list+filter, no FAB — returns are entered from a PO), PurchaseReturnDetailPage (header+lines+returned IMEIs+link to PO), PurchaseReturnFormPage (mirrors grn_receive_page: per received line shows received/already-returned/available, qty bounded to available, SERIALIZED lines require exactly qty IMEIs via type/scan, reason required, live total, on success invalidates PO detail + poReturnedQtys + supplierLedger + payablesAging). Entry points gated purchase:update: PurchaseOrderDetailPage "Return" (any line qty_received>0) + PurchaseInvoiceDetailPage "Return Against Bill" (passes poId+invoiceId). Hub row; routes /purchasing/returns[/create(extra {poId,grnId?,invoiceId?})|/:id]. supplier_detail _EntryRow renders kind=RETURN as a credit with assignment_return icon. No migration (RPC + tables + PR- series already live). RPC chain re-verified in a rolled-back dry-run (impersonated ADMIN): PO→approve→receive 5→return 2 = PR-BR01-000001, RETURN_OUT −2 at null warehouse, qty_on_hand==Σ ledger, over-return raises ERR_RETURN_EXCEEDS_RECEIVED. flutter analyze clean (only pre-existing infos in auth/sales).

2026-07-11 — Customer write-drop fix (lib/features/sales/data/models/customer_model.dart): the old CustomerModel.toJson was DEAD CODE (0 callers) and, worse, the customer create path (sales_repository_impl.createCustomer) inserted the raw form map with NO column whitelist — while the entity/DB carry credit_limit/credit_terms/loyalty_points/opening_balance/group_id/tags, callers only ever passed name+phone, so those columns were unreachable and credit_limit stayed 0, silently defeating create_sale's ERR_CREDIT_LIMIT_EXCEEDED guard (a no-op above 0). Replaced toJson with toInsert/toUpdate + a _writable whitelist covering EVERY writable column (name, phone, phone_secondary, email, address_line1/2, city, state, postal_code, country, tax_number, group_id, credit_limit, credit_terms, loyalty_points, opening_balance, status, tags, notes) — mirrors SupplierModel.toInsert/toUpdate exactly; repo now routes createCustomer(CustomerModel.toInsert(data)). custom_fields_json left out (not on the Customer entity yet — parity gap, not needed for this fix). No create_sale / POS change. Verified via a rolled-back impersonated-ADMIN insert: a customer with credit_limit=50000 + tags=['vip'] round-trips (both persist on readback). flutter analyze clean (only pre-existing infos in auth/sales).

2026-07-11 — Customer receivables (migration customer_ledger_receivables_aging): customer_ledger(customers:read) + receivables_aging(customers:read) reading invoices(debit)/payments(credit) — exact mirror of supplier_ledger/payables_aging, no new tables. Closes the customer/supplier ledger asymmetry on the DB side.
2026-07-11 — Customers CRM brought to parity with suppliers (new lib/features/customers/, mirrors lib/features/suppliers/ verbatim). Customer entity + CustomerModel MOVED here from features/sales; old sales paths (domain/entities/customer.dart, data/models/customer_model.dart) are now one-line re-exports so customer_picker_sheet / invoice_detail / sales_history / sales_return / SalesRepository keep working UNCHANGED (POS untouched). New: CustomerLedger + ReceivablesAging entities (from customer_ledger/receivables_aging RPCs), sealed CustomerFailure, abstract CustomersRepository, 7 usecases (LoadCustomers/LoadCustomer/CreateCustomer/UpdateCustomer/SoftDeleteCustomer/LoadCustomerLedger/LoadReceivablesAging), CustomersRemoteDataSource (ONE datasource; deleted_at-null + ILIKE name/phone + status filter, load/insert/update/softDelete, rpc customer_ledger/receivables_aging), repo impl → typed failures. Presentation: CustomersController (list+search+status+create/edit/remove, invalidates ledger/aging) + customerLedger/receivablesAging/customerOutstanding providers. Pages: CustomersPage (list + search + status filter + receivable hint + FAB gated customers:create), CustomerFormPage (ALL editable columns incl credit_limit/credit_terms/opening_balance — the screen that finally sets a customer's credit limit; submit gated customers:create/update), CustomerDetailPage (header + credit summary card [limit/outstanding/remaining] + ledger timeline, edit/delete gated customers:update/delete), ReceivablesAgingPage (buckets + by-customer, rows → detail). Wiring: routes /customers[/create|/:id|/:id/edit] + /receivables; Customers section (Customers + Receivables Aging) on the inventory hub next to Suppliers; dashboard Receivables KPI now taps through to /receivables (was a dead-end number); POS customer chip shows remaining credit (limit − outstanding via customerOutstandingProvider) for a selected credit customer; new CreditLimitExceededFailure mapped from create_sale's ERR_CREDIT_LIMIT_EXCEEDED so an over-limit credit sale shows "Credit limit exceeded" not the generic "select a customer". DEFERRED (out of scope, still Sales-V1 deferrals): customer_groups, loyalty logic, communication_logs, bulk import, statements. No migration (customers table + customers:* perms + customer_ledger/receivables_aging RPCs already live). flutter analyze clean (only pre-existing infos in auth/sales).

2026-07-11 — Roles/tenants RLS tenant-scoped (migration 20260710132545_tenant_scope_roles_tenants_rls): replaced the permissive `using(true)` policies on public.roles and public.tenants — which let any authenticated user read every tenant's name and role hierarchy cross-tenant — with tenant-scoped policies via auth_tenant_id(). Closes the audit Known Issue.

2026-07-11 — Customers mutation permissions (migration 20260710134809_customers_mutation_permissions): added the `customers` permission module and split customers-table RLS into granular gated policies — INSERT gated customers:create, UPDATE customers:update, DELETE customers:delete, plus tenant SELECT. ADMIN gets all six actions; CASHIER gets create+read only (can add a customer at POS, cannot edit or delete). This is what makes a CASHIER-can't-edit gate real at the DB, not just in UI.

2026-07-11 — next_number revoked from authenticated (migration 20260710143731_revoke_next_number_from_authenticated): EXECUTE on next_number(text,uuid) revoked from the authenticated role so clients cannot mint document numbers directly. All real callers are SECURITY DEFINER RPCs (create_purchase_order/receive_goods/create_purchase_invoice/record_supplier_payment/create_purchase_return/create_sale) which run as owner and are unaffected.

2026-07-11 — Document numbers include the branch code (number_series.include_branch_code defaults true): PO/GRN/PV/PR numbers render as PO-BR01-000001 / GRN-BR01-000001 / PV-BR01-000001 / PR-BR01-000001 — the branch code segment is expected, not a bug. Dropping it is a one-line number_series config change if a tenant wants flat numbering.

2026-07-11 — Clarification on customer CRM access control (re the 2026-07-11 customers-parity entry): customer READS are enforced server-side only — RLS tenant SELECT on the customers table + `customers:read` gate inside customer_ledger/receivables_aging RPCs; the list/detail/aging pages are not wrapped in a read-time PermissionGate. Customer WRITE actions (create/edit/delete) are gated in the UI via PermissionGate(customers, create|update|delete) AND at the DB via the gated RLS policies.

2026-07-11 — Accounting foundation (migration accounting_foundation_coa): accounts (chart of accounts), fiscal_periods, tax_rules, ledger_accounts (schema §3.8/§3.9/§3.14 verbatim); NEW 'accounting' permission module (backfill + roles trigger). Seeded 15-account default CoA (is_system, stable codes 1000–6100) + current OPEN fiscal period + GST17 default tax rule for all tenants. Helpers acct_id(tenant,code) + current_fiscal_period(tenant,date). Tax rules = client CRUD gated accounting:*; accounts/periods = read + RPC-write.

2026-07-11 — Journal engine (migration accounting_journal_engine): journal_entries + journal_lines (schema §3.9, IMMUTABLE) with the three mandated triggers — fiscal-period-open (verbatim §9.9), immutability (no update/delete), deferred balance-check (SUM debit=credit). post_journal (the ONLY ledger writer; jsonb lines by account_code; auto-post callers pass p_gate=false; maintains accounts.current_balance) + reverse_journal (audit-safe counter-entry, accounting:approve). Corrections only via reversal.

2026-07-11 — Vouchers/bank/expenses (migration accounting_vouchers_bank_expenses): bank_accounts (+ reconciliations), expense_categories + expenses, vouchers (schema §3.9 verbatim). create_voucher (payment/receipt/contra/journal → posts via post_journal, numbers by type) + create_expense (Dr expense/input-tax, Cr cash/bank; maintains bank balance). bank_accounts + expense_categories client-CRUD gated accounting:*; vouchers/expenses/recons read + RPC-write.

2026-07-11 — Reports (migration accounting_reports): trial_balance / profit_loss(from,to) / balance_sheet / account_ledger read RPCs, tenant-scoped, normalising debit/credit by account type. Read straight from journal_lines — no materialised report tables (YAGNI until volume demands).

2026-07-11 — bank_accounts client-grant fix (migration fix_bank_accounts_client_grant): the vouchers migration bundled bank_accounts into the RPC-only revoke loop (revoke insert/update/delete) then added "bank gated insert/update" RLS policies without re-granting the table privilege. Postgres checks GRANTs before RLS, so the app could never create/edit a bank account — the policies were dead. Forward migration re-grants insert, update (delete stays revoked; soft-delete via deleted_at). bank_accounts is client-CRUD master data like suppliers.

2026-07-12 — record_customer_payment migration collision resolved (migration fix_record_customer_payment_gl). Two migrations defined the RPC: accounting_autopost_sales (184605, GL-aware — posts Dr cash/bank Cr AR, sets PARTIALLY_PAID, stores bank_account_id) and a later record_customer_payment (185637, "no GL hook yet" — dropped the GL post + bank_account_id, never set PARTIALLY_PAID, generated an unstored RECEIPT_VOUCHER number, updated dead ledger_accounts rows). 185637 sorts after 184605 so it silently won on every replay, regressing the GL hook. Forward migration 192129 (sorts after both) restores the GL-aware definition as canonical. Dropped for good: the RECEIPT_VOUCHER number (payments has no column to store it — it only burned the sequence) and the ledger_accounts update (no CUSTOMER rows exist; AR is carried on invoices.balance, which customer_ledger/receivables_aging and the GL both reconcile against). Lesson: keep one migration per RPC; a later out-of-order redefinition wins on sort order regardless of which was written last.

2026-07-11 — GL auto-post begins at the sales funnel (migration accounting_autopost_sales, slice 1 of A5): create_sale now posts a balanced SALE journal (Dr cash@applied + Dr AR@balance = Cr revenue@subtotal-disc + Cr output-tax; plus Dr COGS / Cr inventory@sum cost_price·qty), and a NEW record_customer_payment RPC settles a credit invoice (insert payments row, reduce invoice balance, Dr cash/bank Cr AR) — filling the A0 gap where no customer-settlement path existed. Auto-posts call post_journal with p_gate=false (a CASHIER without accounting:create must still complete a sale). GL mirrors the AR subledger 1:1 (net AR journal = invoices.balance, verified). Zero-value lines are skipped (post_journal rejects both-zero lines). Idempotency via a unique index on journal_entries(tenant_id, reference_type, reference_id) where reference_id not null. Ceilings: all sale payments book to Cash 1000 (bank/card split by payment method deferred); GRN posts no GL (inventory books at purchase-invoice time). Purchase-side + sales-return auto-post = next slice.

2026-07-11 — record_customer_payment (migration record_customer_payment): collects against a customer CREDIT invoice — mirror of record_supplier_payment. Inserts payment, reduces invoices.balance/paid_amount, flips PAID at zero, decrements ledger_accounts (CUSTOMER), blocks overpayment; RECEIPT_VOUCHER number by invoice branch. Closes the half-built credit lifecycle (create_sale minted balances that nothing collected). GL auto-post added separately in the auto-post phase.

2026-07-12 — A5 auto-post COMPLETE — all five money paths post a balanced journal on prod (migrations autopost_supplier_payment 200843, autopost_purchase_invoice 201211, autopost_purchase_return 201702, after the sales pair). Canonical reference_type labels (the SALE_INVOICE/RECEIPT sketch was NOT used): SALE, CUSTOMER_PAYMENT, PURCHASE_INVOICE, SUPPLIER_PAYMENT, PURCHASE_RETURN. Postings: SALE Dr cash/AR Cr revenue/tax + Dr COGS/Cr inventory; CUSTOMER_PAYMENT Dr cash/bank Cr AR; PURCHASE_INVOICE Dr inventory/input-tax Cr AP (inventory books at invoice — GRN posts no GL); SUPPLIER_PAYMENT Dr AP Cr cash/bank; PURCHASE_RETURN Dr AP Cr inventory Cr input-tax. Perpetual model — a purchase return credits Inventory 1200, NOT the periodic 5100 Purchase-Returns contra (5100 stays unused). CRITICAL cost-basis rule: PURCHASE_RETURN credits 1200 at v_cogs_basis = Σ round(v_cost_unit·qty,4) — the exact cost the stock reversal removed (unit_cost_base + landed_cost_allocated/qty_ordered) — NOT v_subtotal (Σ qty·unit_cost), which diverges once landed cost or FX≠1 exists and would drift inventory-on-books from stock. Proven by a landed-cost stress dry-run (unit_cost 800 vs cost_unit 500: GL 1200 credit = stock cost removed = 1000, not the 1600 the subtotal basis would have posted). Verified end to end by a five-path rolled-back gate: every entry balanced, trial_balance + balance_sheet both true. GL reconciles 1:1 with the AR/AP subledgers. Bank/card split (all cash→1000) and sales-return (create_sales_return) GL remain deferred. Two migration-collision lessons banked this phase: one RPC per migration (185637 clobbered 192129 on sort order), and code ships ahead of docs — reconcile the runbook after the commits, not before.

Pages: AccountingHub, ChartOfAccounts (tree by type via parent_id), AccountLedger, JournalEntries+Detail (reverse gated accounting:approve), ManualVoucher (dynamic debit/credit line editor, submit disabled until balanced), Expenses+Form, ExpenseCategories, BankAccounts+Form, TaxRules+Form, Reports (P&L/BS/trial-balance). Exposed via Inventory-hub Accounting section (gated accounting:read) — deferred a dedicated bottom-nav tab (5 slots full; adding a branch is heavier). Manual voucher enforces balance client-side AND server-side (post_journal ERR_JOURNAL_UNBALANCED surfaced inline). Customer collect-payment: record_customer_payment wired into customers feature (unpaid-invoice picker → CustomerPaymentPage, /customers/:id/collect, gated sales:create, overpayment→CustomerOverpaymentFailure) + Collect Payment action on CustomerDetailPage — finally lets a shop clear a credit invoice in-app; receivables_aging/customer_ledger reconcile against real collections. Built via two parallel subagent waves (non-UI stack, then pages) with the reports layer + all router/nav integration done in the main thread. flutter analyze clean (only pre-existing auth/sales infos). On-device click-through still pending.

2026-07-12 — M07 A6.2 accounting reports shipped (Flutter, read-only, no migration). Four filterable pages in lib/features/accounting/: TrialBalancePage (as-of + branch, balanced badge, Dr/Cr rows, export affordance gated accounting:export), ProfitLossPage (from/to + branch), BalanceSheetPage (as-of + branch, assets vs liabilities+equity+RE), CashBankBookPage (account 1000/1010 + range, running balance) — reading trial_balance/profit_loss/balance_sheet/cash_bank_book RPCs via 4 family FutureProviders keyed by filter records. Replaced the A6.1 combined ReportsPage/financialReportsProvider with these dedicated ones; shared ReportBranchDropdown/ReportDateChip widgets. Accounting hub Reports row → 4 report rows. STOPPED (per instruction, no hand-written DB SQL) on FiscalPeriodsPage + BankReconciliationPage: close_fiscal_period and create/update_bank_reconciliation RPCs do not exist — flagged to user for migrations. Dashboard payables/cash KPIs also deferred (need dashboard_summary fields, backend). flutter analyze clean (only pre-existing auth/sales infos).

2026-07-12 — M07 A6.3 fiscal-period control + bank reconciliation (Flutter + 3 migrations). Migrations close_fiscal_period/reopen_fiscal_period (gated accounting:approve; OPEN↔CLOSED; LOCKED terminal) and create_bank_reconciliation/complete_bank_reconciliation (snapshot statement vs ledger balance of the bank's chart account; DRAFT→COMPLETED). CRITICAL fix migration fix_current_fiscal_period_no_auto_reopen: current_fiscal_period() matched only status='OPEN', so after a close it found nothing and auto-INSERTed a fresh OPEN period covering the same date — silently defeating close_fiscal_period (every post auto-reopened; proven close+post left 1 CLOSED + 1 OPEN). Now resolves the covering period REGARDLESS of status and returns it (CLOSED/LOCKED → trg_journal_check_period rejects with ERR_ACCOUNTING_PERIOD_CLOSED — the trigger stays the single enforcer); auto-creates ONLY when no period covers the date (genuine new-month gap). Verified via rolled-back dry-runs: close→journal rejected + no new period minted; close→reopen→journal posts; gap-month post auto-creates one OPEN period. Flutter: FiscalPeriodsPage (/accounting/periods; close warns hard when closing the current period; Reopen for CLOSED; LOCKED terminal) + BankReconciliationPage (/accounting/banks/:id/reconcile; per-bank statement-date+balance → difference → complete; prior recons listed; labelled manual snapshot, not line-by-line matching) — reached via a Reconcile action on each bank row + hub rows. AccountingFailure gained PeriodLocked/PeriodAlreadyClosed/AlreadyCompleted. Built via subagent (data+pages); router/hub wired in main thread. flutter analyze clean.

2026-07-12 — M07 LEDGER COMPLETE — create_sales_return auto-posts a balanced SALES_RETURN journal (migration autopost_sales_return), the 6th and final money path. Tax-free per the current refund math (v_grand carries no tax; tax_amount stored 0): revenue side Dr 4100 Sales Returns=v_grand / Cr 1000 Cash=v_refund / Cr 1100 AR=v_grand−v_refund; goods back at COST BASIS Dr 1200 Inventory / Cr 5000 COGS = Σ round(v_item.cost_price·qty,4) — the exact cost RETURN_IN added to stock (same cost-basis rule as the purchase return). Ungated (p_gate=false); zero legs omitted; body verbatim from live except the v_cogs_basis+v_lines declares, the accumulator, and the GL block (fully-returned-check UPDATE preserved verbatim). Verified via rolled-back dry-runs: entry balanced, cost-basis tie (gl 1200 debit = stock cost added = 1485.7143, NOT the 5000 sale value — divergent-cost trap), legs correct, and a six-path gate (SALE/CUSTOMER_PAYMENT/PURCHASE_INVOICE/SUPPLIER_PAYMENT/PURCHASE_RETURN/SALES_RETURN all balanced, trial_balance + balance_sheet true).

2026-07-12 — DEFERRED: sales returns refund TAX-FREE (v_grand carries no tax); the GL hook posts no Output-Tax (2100) reversal to match. Tax-inclusive refunds are a separate correctness question that changes create_sales_return's refund math (not the hook) — deferred.

2026-07-12 — DEFERRED: non-cash payments still debit 1000 Cash, not 1010 Bank; the Cash/Bank Book and Bank Reconciliation run against a near-empty Bank account until a payment-method→account split is built — deferred.

2026-07-12 — Behavior change (flagged): a sales-return over-refund (refund > grand) now hard-fails at the GL (post_journal ERR_JOURNAL_UNBALANCED / chk_journal_lines_one_side) instead of silently writing a negative invoice balance — acceptable; over-refund was never valid.

2026-07-13 — Device fingerprint uniqueness is now PER-TENANT (uq_devices_tenant_fingerprint on (tenant_id, fingerprint_hash)), replacing the global uq_devices_fingerprint. Device identity is per-tenant, matching the tenant-isolated RLS model. Fixes a cross-tenant upsert collision (42501): a device seeded under one tenant routed a second tenant's login-time upsert (onConflict fingerprint_hash) to the UPDATE path against the other tenant's row, which "devices admin manage" (USING tenant_id=auth_tenant_id()) correctly rejected. RLS was correct; the uniqueness model was wrong. Client upsert onConflict → 'tenant_id,fingerprint_hash' (must match the new index). Dev artifact: shared simulator across seed tenants. Pre-checked: no duplicate (tenant_id,fingerprint_hash) pairs.

2026-07-13 — Inventory hardening (migration inventory_service_type_guard): post_stock_movement now rejects any movement of a type='SERVICE' product (ERR_SERVICE_NOT_STOCKED) — the chokepoint guard makes SERVICE genuinely non-stock across GRN/count/transfer/sale/repair. Pre-existing M05 hole (SERVICE valid enum, never enforced non-stock); the repair REPAIR-SERVICE sentinel was the first SERVICE product, exposing it. Closes the M09 DoD item "sentinel REPAIR-SERVICE can't be GRN'd/counted"; also protects all future SERVICE products (a stocked sentinel would leak avg_cost+balance into inventory valuation/balance sheet). Whole-body redefine, carries the R3 REPAIR_USE branch verbatim. Verified rolled-back: SERVICE GRN rejected; STANDARD receipt + SALE + REPAIR_USE deducts unchanged. Secondary picker/query filter (exclude SERVICE from GRN/count/PO) deferred as UX defense-in-depth.

2026-07-13 — SERVICE non-stock pickers (UI, additive, no RPC touched): stock-op product pickers — stock movement, adjustment, transfer, count scan/sku lookup, PO line add — now exclude type=SERVICE client-side (.where p.type != ProductType.service). Query-level .neq was rejected: searchProducts/loadProducts are a single shared datasource pair reached by the POS terminal + catalog via productsProvider, so filtering the query would also hide SERVICE from sales (guardrail: leave sales as-is) and the catalog. PO form + repair parts call searchProducts directly; only PO filtered (repair parts left per scope; labour is SKU-resolved, not a picker). GRN has no picker (PO-driven, getProduct by id). UX defense-in-depth atop the post_stock_movement ERR_SERVICE_NOT_STOCKED chokepoint guard.

2026-07-14 — M09 completion C1: filtered type='SERVICE' from GRN/count/PO/transfer product pickers (datasource query .neq type SERVICE). UX defense-in-depth over the post_stock_movement C-guard; sales + repair-labour paths untouched.
2026-07-13 — Repair signature capture (C2). Decisions: (1) private 'signatures' bucket + RLS + short-lived signed URLs, NO client-side AES — the "mirror TOTP key handling" premise was false (TOTP is 100% Supabase-Auth-managed; app holds no symmetric key), a device-local key would break cross-device viewing, and an encrypted blob can't render via a signed URL; private bucket + RLS already meets "not publicly accessible" (Storage also encrypts at rest). (2) Minimal wiring — no file_uploads/attachments audit rows for now (deferred). (3) Signature captured in the existing Close & Invoice dialog, not a new screen. Implementation: SignaturePad shared widget (CustomPaint→PNG via RepaintBoundary.toImage, no new dependency); repair datasource uploadSignature (uploadBinary to 'signatures/<tenant>/<repair>.png', upsert) + signatureSignedUrl (60s); migration signatures_storage_policies adds storage.objects RLS (tenant path-prefix + repair:update write / repair:read read). Stored value = the storage PATH (not an expiring signed URL) so Detail signs fresh at view time via Image.network. Deferred: intake signature; file_uploads/attachments audit trail; on-device click-through.

2026-07-14 — M09 completion C2: customer signature capture on Delivery; PNG → private 'signatures' bucket (tenant-RLS, signed URLs, never public) → file_uploads + attachments(entity_type='repair_job') → ref stored in customer_signature_url via close_repair_job. Encryption: [private-bucket at-rest | app AES-256-GCM] per sign-off. Satisfies "signature stored as encrypted blob".
2026-07-14 — M09 completion C3: bulk_change_repair_status (loops the sole writer change_repair_status — full validation + history + notify per job, collects failures into {succeeded, failed[{repair_id,error}]}, batch never aborts) + technician performance folded into technicianWorkloadProvider (delivered count + avg turnaround received→delivered days, computed client-side from the already-loaded job list — no new query/RPC; cancelled excluded; delivered-only techs now surface). 2026-07-14 — M09 completion C4: change_repair_status now logs a customer-facing outbound (communication_logs + REPAIR_STATUS template_code + payload) at status milestones AWAITING_APPROVAL/READY — best-effort, non-blocking (own EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS, never blocks the status change). Technician in-app notification unchanged. New table communication_logs (tenant-scoped RLS read; insert/update/delete revoked from authenticated — written only by the definer RPC + future M11 worker via service_role). Scope calls (ponytail/YAGNI): NO separate sms/email template tables — template body owned by the M11 sender; we log template_code + payload only. Channel = phone→SMS else email→EMAIL; no contact → no row (status still changes). Actual SMS/email SEND is DEPENDENT on the M11 provider/worker; until then rows sit status='PENDING' (documented dependency, not a bug). Gate: rolled-back txn, READY milestone → one PENDING SMS row for the customer; no-contact customer → status changes with zero rows (non-blocking proven).

2026-07-14 — M09 completion C5 (migration repair_parts_tax): close_repair_job now taxes parts lines. Correction to the phase premise — the LIVE create_sale does NOT read products.tax_rate nor honor tax_inclusive; it applies a caller-supplied per-line tax_pct, EXCLUSIVE only (v_ta=round((qty*unit-disc)*tax_pct/100,4)). Faithful mirror = exclusive, no inclusive logic. Server-side (no caller line) the parts tax basis is resolved from products.tax_rate; parts sell = cost*markup is inherently pre-tax so exclusive is correct. Parts line_total stays EXCLUSIVE (matches the labour-line convention); tax rides in tax_amount → summed into v_parts_tax → v_tax → 2100 Output Tax credit. COGS legs (5000/1200 at captured cost) UNCHANGED; cost-tie invariant preserved. Removes the R4 "parts tax-free" deferral. Gate (rolled back, taxable part, cost≠price, markup 20%, labour 17%): journal dr=cr=7259.7044; 2100=1040.341=labour 1020 + parts 20.341; 1200 credit=99.7106=Σ captured cost; trial_balance balanced.

Rejects non-delivered (ERR_NOT_DELIVERED) / expired (ERR_WARRANTY_EXPIRED). close_warranty_claim: zero customer charge, NO invoice, NO revenue — parts consumed normally (REPAIR_USE), captured cost posted Dr 5200 / Cr 1200 Inventory (account_code shape — the live contract; account_id fails), reference_type REPAIR_WARRANTY (free varchar, no enum change), final_cost=0, READY→DELIVERED directly. Gate (rolled back): claim has 0 invoices; journal dr=cr=49.8553; Dr 5200 == Σ repair_parts.total_cost == Cr 1200 == 49.8553; 0 revenue lines (4000/4200); original→WARRANTY_CLAIM; original_repair_id link set; trial balance true. KNOWN pre-existing gap (not introduced): handle_new_user seeds NO accounts, so future tenants get no COA (incl 5200) until that provisioning gap is addressed. Closes "warranty claim tracking".

2026-07-14 — M10 HR & Payroll H1 foundation (migration hr_foundation): first HR slice — 7 status enums, employees + shifts tables (tenant/branch scoped, soft-delete+version), RLS tenant-read + hr-gated writes. NEW permission module 'hr': backfilled to all existing ADMIN roles + trg_seed_hr_perms AFTER-INSERT trigger on roles so future ADMINs auto-inherit (mirrors the repair-module perm pattern). CoA sign-off (H0-verified free codes): 6200 Salary Expense / 2120 Payroll Deductions Payable / 1150 Employee Advances, is_system, seeded for all existing tenants via tenants cross-join (idempotent) — 5200 is Warranty Cost and was NOT reused. Push failure + fix: `insert...select v.type` into accounts.type (account_type_enum) failed SQLSTATE 42804 — Postgres does NOT implicitly cast text→enum through a VALUES-derived column (bare literal insert would); fix = `v.type::account_type_enum`. Whole txn rolled back (nothing partial on prod), git had committed the broken file → amended to 624d898 after clean re-push. Gate (real rows, not rolled back): 7 enums live; ADMIN hr_perms=30 (5 tenants×6 actions); 3 accounts correct enum types + is_system; employees+shifts present. Known pre-existing gap unchanged: handle_new_user still seeds NO accounts, so future tenants get no CoA (incl these HR codes) until provisioning addressed. LESSON: text→enum cast needed inside VALUES/jsonb-derived columns; won't recur in payroll GL (post_journal takes account_code strings, not enum values).

Leave-balance-per-year entitlement deferred.
