2026-07-14 — HR payroll calc (migration hr_payroll_calc): payroll_runs/payroll_items + salary_advances §3.10 verbatim, RLS RPC-only writes. create_payroll_run (DRAFT, unique per tenant/branch/period), calculate_payroll (per ACTIVE employee: basic + p_allowances + OT-from-attendance [hourly = base/(30*8), ×Σattendance.overtime_hours in period] − advance auto-recovery [salary_advances.recovery_amount, capped by balance] − p_extra_deductions; net>=0; sums to run totals), approve_payroll_run (hr:approve → APPROVED). NO GL yet (H5). Monthly pro-ration + OT-policy rate simple — refine flagged. Gate (rolled back, JWT, seed 2 emps 50000/30000 + 2h OT on P1 + active advance 5000; salary_advances seeded via `reset role` since table is RPC-only/no authenticated INSERT grant): count=2; total_gross=80416.6667 (80000 basic + 416.6667 OT); total_deductions=5000; total_net=75416.6667 (ties gross−ded); P1 item deductions_json.advance=5000, net=45416.6667; P2 advance=0, net=30000; approve → APPROVED.

2026-07-14 — HR payroll disbursement (migration hr_payroll_disburse + fix_disburse_payroll_je): disburse_payroll_run (APPROVED→DISBURSED, hr:approve) posts a balanced PAYROLL journal (Dr 6200 Salary Expense=total_gross; Cr 1150 advances recovered; Cr 2120 other deductions payable; Cr 1000 Cash=total_net) mirroring the live post_journal contract (account_code lines, correlation_id ref, p_gate=false), reduces recovered salary_advances balances (oldest-first, capped, stamps fully_recovered_at), marks items PAID, links journal_entry_id. 8th balanced money path. Cash 1000 (bank split deferred, consistent with M07). BUG FOUND + FIXED at gate: original RPC did `select post_journal(...) into v_je` where v_je is uuid but post_journal returns jsonb {total,entry_number,journal_entry_id} → 22P02 invalid input syntax for uuid; every disburse failed. Fix (forward create-or-replace migration): v_je := (post_journal(...))->>'journal_entry_id'. Gate (rolled back, JWT, gross≠net via advance): dr=cr=80416.6667 balanced; 6200 dr=80416.6667=total_gross; 1000 cr=75416.6667=total_net; 1150 cr=5000=advance recovered; 2120 none; status DISBURSED w/ journal_entry_id; trial_balance balanced=true. LESSON: post_journal returns jsonb, not a uuid — capture the id with ->>'journal_entry_id' (existing RPCs use `perform` and discard it; anything needing the je id must extract).

2026-07-14 — HR salary advances (migration hr_salary_advance + fix_disburse_advance_je): disburse_salary_advance (hr:approve) posts Dr 1150 Employee Advances / Cr 1000 Cash via post_journal (reference_type SALARY_ADVANCE, throwaway reference_id — journal traced back only via salary_advances.journal_entry_id forward link) and creates the salary_advances row (balance=amount, recovery_amount stored). Recovery flows through calculate_payroll (deductions_json.advance = recovery_amount capped by balance) → disburse_payroll_run (Cr 1150, reduces balance oldest-first). 9th balanced money path. Cash 1000 (bank split deferred, M07-consistent). BUG FOUND + FIXED at gate: same 22P02 as H5 — `select post_journal(...) into v_je uuid` (post_journal returns jsonb); fix = `v_je := (post_journal(...))->>'journal_entry_id'`. Gate (rolled back, JWT): advance amount=balance=10000, recovery=5000, je set; Dr 1150=Cr 1000=10000, journal balanced; trial_balance true. Tie-to-H5: a following full payroll cycle recovers 5000 → advance balance 5000 (not fully_recovered), trial_balance still true. LESSON REINFORCED: every RPC capturing post_journal's return must extract ->>'journal_entry_id' (two RPCs shipped with this bug — H5 disburse_payroll_run + H6 disburse_salary_advance).

2026-07-14 — Approvals engine (migration approvals_engine): upsert_approval_workflow (levels_json validated), request_approval (resolves active workflow by threshold; NULL threshold = always; no match → required:false so callers proceed unchanged; idempotent per open entity via unique index), act_on_approval (approvals:approve + level required_role [ADMIN super-approver]; min_approvers per level; advances current_level; last level → APPROVED; REJECT → REJECTED; one action per actor per level; append-only approval_actions audit), cancel_approval_request (requestor/admin), approval_status helper. Verified multi-level cycle + idempotency + reject rolled-back: 2-level wf (L1 MANAGER / L2 ADMIN, threshold 100000); below→required:false; above→PENDING; ADMIN super-approver acts L1→current_level 2 PENDING→L2→APPROVED/completed_at set, 2 audit rows; duplicate request on same entity returns existing:true same request_id; reject→REJECTED.

2026-07-14 — Approvals escalation (migration approvals_escalation): escalate_expired_approvals batch (PENDING past expires_at → ESCALATED + escalated_at + audit). Scheduling = pg_cron hourly (extension enabled by this migration — A0 had it absent; live job approvals_escalation_hourly, schedule `0 * * * *`, calls select public.escalate_expired_approvals()). ESCALATED remains an open, actionable state (in the PENDING/ESCALATED index; act_on_approval accepts it). Gate (rolled-back, JWT ADMIN): EXPENSE wf L1 MANAGER, request PENDING, forced expires_at = now()−1h → escalate returns {escalated:1}; request ESCALATED w/ escalated_at set + ESCALATED audit row.

2026-07-14 — Notification dispatch (migration notifications_dispatch): notify() — the single producer entry point: reads notification_preferences (default IN_APP), writes IN_APP as DELIVERED + enqueues one PENDING row per extra enabled channel for the sender to drain; render_template ({{var}}); mark_all_notifications_read; unread_notification_count (IN_APP); upsert_notification_preference. Producers migrate to notify() incrementally (repair keeps working meanwhile). BUG FOUND at gate (fixed by migration notify_status_enum_cast_fix): notify()'s status CASE was two bare string literals → resolved to text, and there is NO implicit text→notification_status_enum cast, so every notify() call threw 42804 and inserted nothing (dispatch entirely dead). FIX: cast the CASE to ::notification_status_enum (body otherwise byte-identical). Gate re-proven rolled-back (ADMIN bdcf170d, tenant 97516251): no-pref→IN_APP DELIVERED only; +EMAIL pref→EMAIL PENDING queued alongside IN_APP DELIVERED; render_template('Hi {{name}}, {{n}} due')→'Hi Ali, 5 due'; unread≥1 then mark_all_notifications_read→0.

2026-07-14 — Notification detectors (migration notifications_detectors): fn_overdue_receivables_notify / fn_unpaid_salaries_notify / fn_stock_mismatch_notify → daily-idempotent IN_APP HIGH/URGENT alerts to tenant admins; pg_cron 07:00 (job notif_detectors_daily, jobid 8, registered MANUALLY via SQL editor — NOT in migrations, re-run on a fresh project). Joins low-stock (existing fn_low_stock_notify). Pending-transfers detector = same pattern (stub noted). BUG FOUND at gate (fixed by migration fn_overdue_receivables_date_fix): fn_overdue_receivables_notify's overdue predicate was `current_date - (date(created_at) + make_interval(days=>credit_terms)) > 0` → (date−timestamp)=interval compared to integer 0 → 42883 no-operator at plan time, so it threw on EVERY call regardless of data. FIX: compare day-counts directly `(current_date - date(created_at)) > coalesce(credit_terms,0)`. Other two detectors verified clean (flagged:0 on empty). Gate re-proven rolled-back (seed: flip a PAID invoice to balance 500 via session_replication_role=replica to bypass invoice-immutability, run detector): flagged 1 + one 'Overdue receivable' admin notif; second same-day run → notif count still 1 (idempotent via not-exists dedupe on tenant+action_id+title+date).

2026-07-15 — Notification sender (edge function notification-sender, N4): the unified outbound drainer. Service-role (ctx.supabaseAdmin, all tenants, RLS-bypass) via house withSupabase pattern, guarded auth:["secret"] (only the cron/scheduler bearing the service key triggers it — unauth curl → 401, verified). Drains PENDING from notifications (channel SMS/EMAIL/WHATSAPP; PUSH excluded — no transport yet; resolves users.phone/email), communication_logs, report_deliveries (channel+recipient+payload; subject=payload.subject/name, body=payload.body/html/json); sends via Twilio (SMS + WhatsApp whatsapp: prefix) + SendGrid (email); marks SENT+sent_at or FAILED+failed_reason (notifications) / error (comm_logs+report_deliveries), 100/batch. Deployed to prod (inert until keys). PROVIDER BOUNDARY: keys via `supabase secrets set TWILIO_SID/AUTH/FROM/WHATSAPP_FROM SENDGRID_KEY FROM_EMAIL`. TRIGGER DEFERRED (not registered): the pg_net */2 cron would flip every real PENDING row to FAILED with no retry before keys exist — activate only once keys + service-key setting are in place. pg_net extension NOT installed yet (only pg_cron). Activation SQL: create extension pg_net; select cron.schedule('notification_sender_drain','*/2 * * * *', $$select net.http_post(url:='https://pwkmrjzksxwxypqmblel.functions.supabase.co/notification-sender', headers:=jsonb_build_object('Authorization','Bearer '||current_setting('app.service_key',true)))$$);  (store service key in a DB setting/Vault — never hardcode). Real-send gate (insert test PENDING per queue → invoke → SENT + message arrives; bad recipient → FAILED) is deferred to key-set time. Outbound LIVE once keys set; until then rows queue (documented dependency, not a bug).

2026-07-15 — Push / FCM (migration notifications_device_tokens, SCHEMA EXTENSION signed off — device_tokens NOT in DATABASE_SCHEMA.md; N5): device_tokens (tenant/user/token/platform/is_active, unique(user_id,token), partial ix on active) + RLS "dt own" (user_id=auth.uid()) + register_device_token(token,platform) definer upsert (on conflict → is_active=true, last_seen_at=now()). Gate-proven rolled-back (ADMIN bdcf170d): register twice → count stays 1, is_active true, RLS-scoped. Table pre-existed on prod (idempotent skip). Sender (N4 edge fn) extended: fcmAuth() mints an OAuth token from the FCM_SERVICE_ACCOUNT secret (RS256 JWT via Web Crypto → oauth2.googleapis.com, cached 1h) + sendPush() POSTs FCM v1 messages:send; drain now fans channel='PUSH' notifications out to the user's active device_tokens (action_url → data for tap-through), marks SENT/FAILED. Deployed, inert until FCM_SERVICE_ACCOUNT set. FLUTTER firebase_messaging DEFERRED — needs a provisioned Firebase project + platform config (google-services.json / GoogleService-Info.plist / firebase_options.dart); adding the package without them breaks the build. Once provisioned: firebase_messaging, request permission, register_device_token on token refresh, foreground/background handlers route via action_url. Real push gate (register token → send PUSH → arrives + taps through) deferred to Firebase-provisioned time.

Reusable NotificationBell widget (badge from a 30s-polling unreadCountStreamProvider, taps → /notifications) added to Dashboard + Inventory-hub app bars (shell is bottom-nav with per-page app bars — no shared top bar; bell drops into any AppBar actions in one line). NEW NotificationSettingsPage /notifications/settings: 6 known event_types × 5 channel toggles + enabled switch → upsertPreference (login-gated, own prefs). 2026-07-15 — Notification admin UI + bulk send (migration notifications_bulk_communication; lib/features/notifications/ extended): TemplatesAdminPage /notifications/templates (gated notifications:update, direct RLS writes) edits SMS (body) + email (subject/body_html) templates with is_active toggle + {{placeholder}} help; BulkCommunicationPage /notifications/bulk (notifications:create) segment (all/with_balance/by_tag) × channel × template → preview count then enqueue; CommunicationLogsPage /notifications/logs (notifications:read) merges communication_logs+report_deliveries+non-IN_APP notifications with channel/status filters (PENDING labelled "Queued"). Gate-proven rolled-back (seeded customer): dry_run=1, real enqueue → PENDING communication_logs row, recipient set, body rendered, by_tag path works. Reachable via gated links on the settings page. Templates admin = edit/create + soft-delete via is_active (hard delete YAGNI). M11 UI COMPLETE.

  - Demo Store 00000000-…001 — complete=TRUE.
  - Madina Mobile 3ae21967-… — complete=TRUE.
  - Marina Mobile c6e9a22c-… — complete=TRUE.
  - Marina Mobile Shop 4b0b602d-… — complete=TRUE.
  All 5 report open_fiscal_period=1 → every tenant can post a journal today; no tenant blocked. Only live drift = Ahmad Store's 2 missing series; P2 (provision_tenant + backfill sweep) closes it and P3 wires it into handle_new_user so new tenants stop being born short.

2026-07-15 — Provisioning seeder (migration tenant_provisioning_seed + fix tenant_provisioning_seed_tax_mode_cast_fix): provision_tenant(tenant) — idempotent (not-exists guard on every block); seeds CoA (20 flat system accounts incl. 4200/5200/6200/2120/1150), GOLDEN 8 number_series (tenant-level, branch NULL, padding 6 — closes Ahmad Store's missing STOCK_TRANSFER/STOCK_COUNT), 4 tax rules (GST17 default + GST5/EXEMPT/GST17I), current-year OPEN fiscal period (the gate on ALL journal posting), REPAIR-SERVICE SERVICE sentinel, 3 sms + 3 email templates, 1 Main Warehouse/WH01 is_default per branch. Does NOT seed perms (the 6 roles triggers already do) nor inventory_settings (0 rows for every tenant incl. golden — lazily defaulted). anon+authenticated EXECUTE revoked. NOT yet wired into handle_new_user (P3). TWO BUGS caught at gate + fixed (migration tenant_provisioning_seed_tax_mode_cast_fix, both same 42804/42501 class as prior enum-cast bugs): (1) 42804 — tax_rules.mode is tax_calculation_mode_enum but VALUES-derived v.mode went in as bare text (no implicit text->enum cast) → whole fn aborted, seeded NOTHING; fixed v.mode::tax_calculation_mode_enum (applies_to is varchar, no cast). (2) 42501 'branch not assigned' — block 7 reused ensure_default_warehouse(), which guards on auth_has_branch(auth.uid()); there is NO user context inside a definer seeder → threw; replaced with a direct idempotent warehouse insert (definer bypasses RLS). Three gate proofs, all GREEN on linked prod: (a) idempotent no-op on golden Demo Store — accounts stable 20 over 3 runs, runs 2&3 identical; (b) THE REAL PROOF — Ahmad Store false→TRUE, number_series 6→8 with TRF-/SC- present (live drift closed); (c) fresh bare tenant, rolled back — before_complete=false→after=true, 20 accounts + 8 series + 4 tax_rules + OPEN fiscal + REPAIR-SERVICE sentinel in one call, 0 leaked post-rollback. SWEEP applied to prod (select provision_tenant(id) from tenants): all 5 tenants now complete=true, open_fiscal_period>=1. SIDE EFFECT (additive-safe, authorized by the sweep): every tenant went from 1 tax rule (live golden had only GST17) to 4 (seeder follows spec §10's fuller set); single is_default preserved (GST17). If strict golden parity (1 tax rule) is later required, the tax block seeds the superset by design — revisit then.

2026-07-15 — Signup provisioning wired (migration tenant_provisioning_wire_signup, P3): handle_new_user's new-tenant (business_name) branch now calls `perform public.provision_tenant(v_tenant_id)` immediately after the tenant + ADMIN/CASHIER roles + Main Branch inserts — same signup transaction, NO exception swallow (provisioning failure rolls the whole signup back atomically; a failed retryable signup beats a half-provisioned tenant that cannot post GL). Demo Store fallback branch untouched (creates no tenant → already provisioned). Body applied VERBATIM from the live P0.1 dump + that one line; whole-body create-or-replace, nothing else changed. Gate (both rolled-back auth.users inserts on linked prod, all GREEN): (a) regression — non-business signup ('{}'  meta) lands in Demo Store as CASHIER with 1 branch assignment, unaffected; (b) the fix — business signup ('{"business_name":"Gate Test Biz"}') → new tenant born ADMIN, verify complete:true, 20 accounts + 8 number_series + OPEN fiscal + REPAIR-SERVICE sentinel, zero manual steps; 0 leaked rows post-rollback. CLOSES the long-standing "new tenants get no CoA/series/fiscal period" gap (tenants created after a foundation migration were born unable to post a journal). Provisioning is now creation-time, not backfill-time.

2026-07-15 — Provisioning P4 REAL-TRANSACTION gate (the DoD: a brand-new tenant must TRANSACT, not merely "have seeds"). Proven live on linked prod via rolled-back transactions that (a) insert a business-signup auth.users row → handle_new_user provisions, then (b) act as that new tenant's ADMIN (set local role authenticated + request.jwt.claims sub=uid so auth.uid()/auth_tenant_id() resolve) and exercise the real posting RPCs — ZERO manual seeding. CORE DoD (the fault this runbook exists for) GREEN: create product → post_stock_movement OPENING → open_cashier_session → create_sale (2×500 +17%) → invoice INV-BR01-000001, journal dr=cr=1370>0, trial_balance balanced=true. Secondary account-path proofs GREEN (second rolled-back txn): repair (create_repair_job → change_repair_status RECEIVED→IN_REPAIR→READY → close_repair_job) → REPAIR_INVOICE journal with 4200 Service Revenue credited 1000 (proves 4200 + REPAIR-SERVICE sentinel via repair_service_product()); payroll (create_employee → create_payroll_run → calculate_payroll → approve → disburse_payroll_run) → PAYROLL journal with 6200 Salary debited 50000 + net to 1000 (proves 6200); change_repair_status→READY rendered 1 REPAIR_STATUS communication_logs row (notification path); trial_balance balanced=true throughout. NOT exercised: 2120 Payroll Deductions Payable + 1150 Employee Advances post only as conditional lines (deductions/advances present) — this minimal run had none; both accounts are confirmed SEEDED (verify complete ⇒ all 20 present), only the conditional posting lines weren't triggered. All P4 txns rolled back: 0 leaked users/tenants, tenant count still 5. CONCLUSION: the "new tenants get no CoA / cannot post GL" fault is CLOSED end-to-end — a freshly signed-up tenant sells, repairs, and runs payroll with balanced books and no manual steps. GUI E2E (ringing the sale through the Flutter app) not performed from this environment; the DB-layer proof exercises the exact RPCs the app calls.

2026-07-15 — Provisioning P5 ops + DoD close + named follow-ups. DoD MET: verify_all_tenants_provisioning() = all 5 tenants complete:true (ongoing health check — any complete:false = a tenant that can't fully transact); a tenant created through the REAL signup path reaches complete:true and completes sale + repair close + payroll disburse with balanced journals and zero manual seeding (P4); non-business signup regression-clean (P3a). OPS: registered pg_cron job verify_provisioning_daily (jobid 10, '0 5 * * *', active) — inserts an URGENT IN_APP notification to each tenant's ADMIN when that tenant is not complete; added a same-day not-exists dedup guard to the supplied SQL to prevent daily duplicate spam while a tenant stays incomplete. Registered MANUALLY via db query (NOT in migrations, like notif_detectors_daily — re-register on a fresh project). 6 cron jobs now (mv_refresh_15min, report_schedules_runner, reorder_recommendations_daily, notif_detectors_daily, approvals_escalation_hourly, verify_provisioning_daily). NAMED FOLLOW-UPS (deferred, not silent):
  1. ⚠️ FISCAL PERIOD ROLLOVER CLIFF (highest priority): provision_tenant seeds only the CURRENT-year OPEN period. On next Jan 1, every tenant needs a new period or ALL journal posting (sale/repair/payroll GL) stops tenant-wide. No rollover job exists today — real latent production cliff. Fix = fn_ensure_current_fiscal_period() on a daily/monthly cron (idempotent create-if-missing). Flagged, not built.
  2. ⚠️ ENUM LANDMINES: number_series_type_enum has 10 labels; golden seeds 8. JOURNAL_ENTRY + RECEIPT_VOUCHER are seeded by no tenant. Harmless today (nothing calls next_number() for them) but the day a feature does, it fails for EVERY tenant at once. Decide deliberately: seed them (JV-/RV-) into the golden set, or drop them from the enum. Don't leave two live landmines.
  3. Perm-trigger fragmentation (smell, not bug): 6 seed_*_perms_for_admin triggers fire on roles insert; each new module adds another. Cleanup = consolidate into one seed_module_perms_for_admin or fold perms into provision_tenant. Works as-is.
  4. CASHIER module perms: the 6 triggers seed ADMIN only; a new tenant's CASHIER gets the base matrix from handle_new_user. If CASHIER should see repair/reports/etc., that's a separate perm-design decision.
  5. inventory_settings deliberately NOT seeded (0 rows for every tenant incl. golden; lazily defaulted). Confirmed create_stock_adjustment path COALESCEs (does not assume a settings/stock row).
  6. Dead code: the superseded handle_new_user definition in migration 20260609000001_signup_provisioning (P0.7) is stale in the repo (live fn is the auth_full_schema:230 version + P3 wiring). Only one live handle_new_user exists; the old migration file can't be safely edited (history). Repo-hygiene note only — nobody should edit that file.
  7. Tenant onboarding UI (business details, logo, branches beyond Main) — separate from data provisioning, not built.
PROVISIONING RUNBOOK (P0–P5) COMPLETE: fault "new tenants get no CoA / cannot post GL" closed end-to-end, gate-proven live, with drift monitoring in place.

2026-07-15 — Fiscal period model corrected (migrations provision_tenant_fiscal_alignment + verify_provisioning_fiscal_granularity). RETRACTION: the P5 "fiscal rollover cliff" follow-up (#1) is WRONG and is withdrawn. current_fiscal_period(p_tenant uuid, p_date date) is the SOLE period creator — VOLATILE, SECURITY DEFINER (verified) — and lazily inserts the covering MONTHLY period (name to_char(date,'Mon YYYY'), start date_trunc('month'), end month-end, OPEN) whenever none exists. It self-heals on the first post of any date, so there is NO Aug-1 and NO Jan-1 posting freeze; the "Jul 2026" periods exist because this function made them. No rollover cron is needed. REAL DEFECT (introduced in provisioning P2): provision_tenant seeded its own period with date_trunc('year') → an ANNUAL Jan1–Dec31 bucket while the live model is MONTHLY → new tenants would run annual periods, existing tenants monthly (granularity/consistency divergence, not a freeze). FIX (Task 2): provision_tenant's whole "4. FISCAL PERIOD" insert block replaced by `perform public.current_fiscal_period(p_tenant_id, current_date)` — reuse the sole creator so the model can never diverge again; exactly one block changed, rest byte-for-byte. LIVE DATA (Task 3): swept for non-monthly periods (end_date <> month-end of start_date) → 0 found; the annual-period tenants (P3b signup test, P4 transaction test) were all rolled back and never persisted, so NO repair was needed and no live data was changed this phase. CHECKER HARDENED (Task 4): verify_tenant_provisioning now computes fiscal_period_monthly (the covering OPEN period's end_date must equal month-end of its start_date), surfaces it as a field, and folds it into `complete` — previously it only checked open_fiscal_period>0, which an annual period satisfies, so it could not have caught this class; now the verify_provisioning_daily monitor catches granularity drift, not just absence. GATE all green on linked prod: (1) verify_all_tenants_provisioning → 5/5 complete:true + fiscal_period_monthly:true; (2) provision_tenant ×2 on Demo Store → complete:true both, accounts 20 + periods 1 stable (no dupes, no 2nd period); (3) fresh scratch tenant (rolled back) → provision → "Jul 2026" 2026-07-01..07-31 monthly OPEN, is_monthly + verify_monthly + complete all true, NOT annual; (4) journal-in-period integrity across all tenants → 0 total journals, 0 null-period, 0 outside-period (no live posting data; nothing regressed). Files: 2 additive migrations. handle_new_user unchanged (calls provision_tenant, which now aligns fiscal via the sole creator).

2026-07-15 — Fiscal correction — Gate 4 re-run + P4-persistence honesty. Prior Gate 4 (journal-in-period integrity) was an EMPTY-SET false green (0 live journals → the check never ran); per standing doctrine "empty set ≠ pass, seed then measure" it was untested. Closed with a real rolled-back smoke: fire the wired path (auth.users business signup → handle_new_user → provision_tenant → monthly period), then post_journal (Dr 1000 100 / Cr 3000 100, p_gate=false) as that tenant's ADMIN → journal lands in the single "Jul 2026" 2026-07-01..07-31 OPEN period, journals_in_period=1, total_periods=1 (current_fiscal_period did NOT spawn a second period). Provisioning + posting proven to agree on a provision-created monthly period. HONESTY CORRECTION: P3b/P4 "real signup" were SQL simulations — direct auth.users inserts inside begin/rollback that fire the real on_auth_user_created trigger chain (handle_new_user → provision_tenant), NOT live Flutter/GoTrue app signups, and rolled back (never persisted). The exact wired trigger path is exercised, but the first genuine customer signup will be its first LIVE execution. PROJECT_STATE line corrected accordingly. Silver lining: the P2 annual-seed divergence existed only between P2 and this fix, no real tenant was born in that window (0 non-monthly periods live), so the fault was caught before the first customer — cheapest possible moment. Tenant provisioning DONE, honestly.

2026-07-15 — Settings S1 (migrations settings_payment_methods + provision_tenant_payment_methods): payment_methods §3.14 verbatim + RLS (tenant read / settings:update write); seeded 7 rows per tenant from payment_method_enum (CASH is_system; BANK_TRANSFER/MOBILE_WALLET/CHEQUE/CREDIT_NOTE requires_reference), all UNMAPPED (bank_account_id NULL). NEW resolve_payment_account(tenant, method, bank_account_id) = THE SOLE payment→GL resolver: 3-tier (explicit bank account → method's bank account → '1000' Cash fallback) via the schema chain payment_methods.bank_account_id → bank_accounts.chart_account_id → accounts.code. Fallback is byte-identical to today, so S2's rewrites are no-change for cash-only tenants. Corrected S0 findings on record: NO live misstatement (1000=0, 1010=0, all 7 payments CASH — a latent trap, not broken books); ledger_accounts is an AR/AP subledger, NOT a payment map. Dropped duplicate "bank tenant read" policy. provision_tenant seeds methods (block 8, byte-for-byte rest); verify checks 7 (folded into complete). GATE all green on linked prod (seed-then-measure, resolver's 3 tiers proven): 7 methods/tenant; TIER 3 unmapped → 1000 for CASH/CARD/null (today's behaviour preserved); rolled-back map of CARD→test bank (chart_account_id=1010) → TIER 2 card=1010 while cash stays 1000; explicit bank_account_id on CASH → TIER 1 =1010 (explicit wins); 0 leaked post-rollback (bank_accounts 0, mapped methods 0). Fresh-provision proof (rolled back): bare tenant → provision_tenant → payment_methods=7, verify complete=true. verify_all_tenants_provisioning() = 5/5 complete:true, payment_methods 7.

2026-07-15 — Settings S2a (migration settings_create_sale_payment_account): create_sale's unconditional "all paid amounts book to Cash 1000" debit replaced by per-payment resolution via resolve_payment_account, GROUPED by resolved account (split tender → one debit line per account, Σ = paid total; a cash change return is credited back to Cash so Σ nets to v_applied). Removes the M07 payment_method→account deferral for the SALE path. Only the GL payment-debit shape changed; revenue/tax/COGS legs, stock, IMEI, session guard byte-for-byte. Unmapped methods resolve to 1000 → cash-only tenants byte-identical (regression-gated). GATE (rolled-back, as Ahmad Store ADMIN, opening cost 0 to isolate payment debits): (A) unmapped CASH sale = single 1000 debit (=500 paid), balanced; (B) CARD→1010-mapped SPLIT sale (500 CASH + 1000 CARD) = 1000=500 + 1010=1000 debits summing to paid, dr==cr, trial_balance true. Different per-method amounts so mis-grouping would show.

2026-07-15 — Settings S2b/S2c (migrations settings_payment_rpcs_resolver + settings_vouchers_payroll_resolver): record_customer_payment / record_supplier_payment / create_expense / disburse_payroll_run / disburse_salary_advance all now resolve their money account via resolve_payment_account instead of four divergent hardcodes (unconditional 1000 / bank_account_id→1010 / method='CASH'→1010 / coalesce(arg,'1000')). ONE resolver across every money path. create_voucher unchanged (caller supplies p_lines — no account pick). Explicit p_pay_account still overrides the payroll default (COALESCE short-circuits — resolver only runs when the arg is null). Unmapped → 1000, so all existing gates (HR payroll cost-tie, repair close, sales) re-proved unchanged. GATE S2b (rolled-back, ADMIN): customer payment cash(no bank)=1000; bank mapped to chart 1010 → 1010; bank mapped to chart 1300 (≠1010) → 1300 — the money proof the hardcode is gone (old code would return 1010); supplier cash payment credits 1000; trial_balance true.
