2026-07-15 — Settings S8 (migration settings_sales_return_payment_account): create_sales_return now resolves each refund's GL credit via resolve_payment_account instead of a hardcoded Cr 1000, mirroring S2a create_sale. WHY: S2a made a CARD sale debit its resolved bank (e.g. 1010) while the return still credited 1000 → sale/return no longer mirror (cash understated, bank overstated; journal still balances so no gate caught it). Cannot fire today (all 7 live payments CASH) but M12's premise is closing the trap before the first non-cash payment — and this trap is one M12 itself opened. TWO legs changed vs live: (a) the refund payments insert now persists bank_account_id (`nullif(r->>'bank_account_id','')::uuid`, matching create_sale's own insert); (b) the credit leg is a grouped loop `Cr resolve_payment_account(v_t, p.method, p.bank_account_id) = Σ per account`. The bank_account_id pass-through is the SUBTLE fix: an earlier draft passed null there, making tier 1 (explicit bank on the payment) unreachable on refunds — a sale into "Meezan (1011)" would have refunded to the CARD default (1010), the exact asymmetry recursed one tier down. Everything else byte-for-byte (4100 Dr, 1100 AR Cr, 1200/5000 COGS reversal, RETURN_IN stock, IMEI restore, ERR_RETURN_EXCEEDS_SOLD guard, original RETURNED rollup). Σ grouped credits = v_refund so the journal balances unchanged. GATE all green (rolled-back, ADMIN, real returnable invoices, two DISTINCT products, two independent return JEs): sub-test A REGRESSION — CASH refund credits 1000 (VERDICT_CASH GREEN); sub-test B FIX — CARD (linked to a bank mapped to 1010) refund credits 1010 not 1000 (VERDICT_CARD GREEN, resolve CARD=1010 confirmed); both JEs balanced=true (Dr=Cr). NOTE the gate ran as SQL-Editor superuser (no set-role authenticated) → RLS NOT exercised; it measures GL output only and sidesteps the bank_accounts INSERT policy (null qual). After S8, SEVEN money paths resolve via resolve_payment_account.
  CORRECTION to earlier S7 acceptance wording (was substantially but not literally true): (1) "zero hardcoded 1000/1010 remain" was FALSE — close_repair_job still books its cash leg to a literal 1000 (Deferral A below); the true statement is "all 4 divergent M07 hardcodes gone, 7 paths resolve via the resolver, 1 cash-only literal remains." Verification is `pg_get_functiondef` on PROD, NOT grep of migrations/ — migrations are append-only so ~12 files retain old hardcodes as immutable history; live prod is what's clean. (2) "payment_methods CRUD in the UI" was imprecise — the UI is update/link/toggle only (name, is_active, requires_reference, link/unlink bank), NOT add/remove, and this is CORRECT BY CONSTRUCTION: payments.method is payment_method_enum and uq_payment_methods_tenant_code is unique on (tenant_id, code), so the 7 seeded rows are the complete set — an 8th could never be referenced by a payment, and delete is unsafe (CASH is_system; historical payments reference existing methods). (3) "analyze clean" means: 0 errors, 0 warnings, 0 new issues; 7 pre-existing info lints remain in untouched auth/sales files (the standing convention every M12 phase closed under).
  DEFERRAL A — close_repair_job books its cash leg to literal 1000. NOT a bug: it takes p_paid_amount with no method/bank arg, so it is cash-only BY CONSTRUCTION and 1000 is correct for what it can do — identical to the resolver's tier-3 fallback. Routing it through resolve_payment_account(v_t,null,null) returns 1000 and changes nothing (cosmetic churn). The real work is a FEATURE — "repair close accepts non-cash payment" = signature change + a method picker in the close dialog + resolver wiring. Deferred as a feature, not a fix.
  DEFERRAL B — INVARIANT (recorded prominently; stops regression better than any individual swap, added to CLAUDE.md): ANY new money path resolves its GL account via resolve_payment_account(tenant, method, bank_account_id) — never a literal, never a 5th CASE. If a path has no method/bank concept it is cash-only by construction — say so in a comment.
  Carried-forward M12 deferrals unchanged: fiscal_year_reset inert [sign-off #1]; server-authoritative tax [sign-off #2]; tax_rules.applies_to scoping; JOURNAL_ENTRY/RECEIPT_VOUCHER unused enum labels [sign-off #3]; LOYALTY_POINTS/CREDIT_NOTE resolve to 1000 until those features ship (arguably a 2xxx liability then); Bank Book/Reconciliation stay empty until a tenant links a bank AND takes a non-cash payment; multi-currency.

2026-07-16 — Sync D1 (migration 20260716073759_sync_pull_reference): Class A reference cache — pull-only delta of products/variants/customers/payment_methods/tax_rules over an updated_at watermark, + stock_balance FULL pull. Watermark returns now()-2s (anti-skip: a txn committing between snapshot and read would be skipped forever by a raw now() watermark; client upserts by id so ≤2s re-delivery is a no-op). stock_balance NOT watermarked — D11.2 proved it has last_updated (not updated_at) and NO deleted_at → full pull per branch (small, trigger-created, never deleted). Explicit column lists reconciled to D11.1 live; cost_price/wholesale_price withheld from devices; product_variants has no tenant_id so it scopes through parent product.
  GATE — 3 of 4 PASS, 1 CONFIRMED FAIL (rolled-back, as Ahmad Store user bdcf170d; trg_products_touch disabled in-txn so backdated seeds clear the 2s margin — otherwise the touch trigger stamps updated_at=now(), which the frozen-now() txn puts 0s old, inside the anti-skip window → a FALSE RED that masks the real one): (A) full pull products=8295 (>0) ✅; (B) seed one product updated_at=now()-5s → delta=EXACTLY 1 (watermark fan-in proven) ✅; (C) tenant isolation: Ahmad-Store user sees 0 Demo Store (…0001) rows ✅; (D) SOFT-DELETE TOMBSTONE — set deleted_at on the seeded product → delta softdel_count=0, FAIL.
  CONFIRMED BUG (blocks acceptance): sync_pull_reference is SECURITY INVOKER; products read RLS policy = (tenant_id=auth_tenant_id() AND deleted_at IS NULL). RLS strips soft-deleted rows BEFORE the function sees them → the tombstone can NEVER ride the delta. A deleted product/variant is invisible to the pull, exactly like a hard delete → the offline client's ONLY eviction signal never arrives; deleted items linger in every device cache forever and stay sellable offline. Same defect on product_variants (variants read policy also has deleted_at IS NULL). customers/payment_methods/tax_rules read policies have NO deleted_at clause → tombstones ride correctly there. The migration header's "SECURITY INVOKER … nothing to leak-prove" reasoning is the cause: it accounted for tenant scoping but not the deleted_at predicate.
  FIX APPLIED (migration 20260716125000_sync_pull_reference_softdelete_fix, LIVE): switched to SECURITY DEFINER + `set search_path to 'public'`; body otherwise byte-for-byte. Every subquery already has explicit `where tenant_id=v_tenant`, so DEFINER (RLS off) keeps tenant scoping via that filter and lets tombstones (deleted_at set, updated_at bumped) ride the delta. GATE all green (rolled-back): softdel_in_delta=1 (was 0); delta=1; full pull returns EXACTLY the tenant's set (8295=8295); pulled_not_ahmad=0 (no leak); and cross-tenant from the OTHER admin sees 0 Ahmad rows. Isolation proven both directions as authenticated (RLS enforced for the authenticated role; the fn calls no auth_has_permission so admin≡cashier here — the seeded-CASHIER requirement is moot for this fn, though it becomes real for any permission-gated §3.3 path).
  FIXTURE GAP (D0.7): DB has exactly 2 users, BOTH ADMIN (tenants 3ae21967… and 97516251… Ahmad Store); Demo Store (…0001) has ZERO users; CASHIER role exists with 0 members. For THIS function the read policies are pure tenant scoping with no auth_has_permission call, so ADMIN vs CASHIER is immaterial and isolation is genuinely proven — BUT the moment the fix goes DEFINER, or any permission-gated §3.3 path lands, a seeded non-admin CASHIER becomes mandatory or those gates are theatre.

2026-07-16 — Sync D8 (migration 20260716154500_sync_drain_cron, LIVE) + D9 acceptance — SYNC & OFFLINE COMPLETE. CRON FIX (the spec's cron was broken): the provided job `select sync_replay_sale_intent(id) from sync_outbox …` mass-FAILs — a pg_cron job has NO request.jwt.claims, so create_sale's auth.uid()/auth_tenant_id() are NULL → ERR_NO_TENANT on every row (verified: replay under no-JWT → {error:ERR_NO_TENANT}, row→FAILED). The M11 order-warning made real. FIX: definer sync_drain_cron(p_limit) — for each PENDING/FAILED row (attempts<5, oldest-first) it impersonates the row's ORIGINAL cashier (`set_config('request.jwt.claims', {sub:user_id,role:authenticated}, true)`) so create_sale resolves the right tenant/perms/branch, then routes through the UNCHANGED sync_replay_sale_intent (idempotency + terminal/transient reused verbatim); clears the GUC after. PRIVILEGED (impersonates users) → EXECUTE granted to service_role ONLY (+ postgres owner for the job), revoked from authenticated/anon. GATE (rolled-back, no-JWT cron context, seed cost≠price): drain → {applied:1, abandoned:1, failed:0}; valid intent APPLIED + real invoice INV-BR01-000006; oversell ABANDONED + 1 sync_exceptions + 0 invoices; JWT cleared after. CRON registered MANUALLY (SQL editor, like the other jobs — NOT reproducible from migrations/): `cron.schedule('sync_outbox_drain_5min','*/5 * * * *', $$ select public.sync_drain_cron(50); $$)` — registered AFTER D4/D5 gate-green (keys-first-cron-second, M11 verbatim). Now 7 live pg_cron jobs (mv_refresh_15min, report_schedules_runner, reorder_recommendations_daily, notif_detectors_daily, approvals_escalation_hourly, verify_provisioning_daily, sync_outbox_drain_5min) — all MANUAL, same caveat.
  D9 ACCEPTANCE (live-confirmed): sync_outbox has 0 write policies (client insert → 42501); create_sale is a single 7-arg fn w/ p_idempotency_key; post_journal raises ERR_PERIOD_CLOSED; sync_drain_cron ACL = postgres|service_role only; cron active; sync perms = 10; ALL migrations Local==Remote (git==db==prod parity); flutter analyze clean (0 new, 7 standing lints). DB-level invariants gate-proven across D1–D8; on-device airplane→reconnect UX not driven from this headless env (stated honestly, every phase).
  DEFINITION OF DONE (100% of the built scope): offline reference cache + intent outbox + server-authoritative replay (client drain D7.2 + cron backstop D8) + exception queue + create_sale idempotency (D4) + closed-period guard (D6) + the Sync screens (status sheet, Exception Centre) — all LIVE. Two live-RPC rewrites (create_sale, post_journal) each ISOLATED, one per migration, gated regression-first; every replay routes THROUGH the sole writers (create_sale/next_number/post_journal), none duplicates their logic.
  DEFERRED (by design, not omission): §3.3's sync_log / sync_conflicts / domain_events / job_queue_log are NOT built — an append-only serially-replayed intent queue has NOTHING to merge; those tables are the superseded NestJS/BullMQ LWW/merge design (2026-06-09), and LWW on anything that posted a journal is silent destruction of a sale. Also deferred: offline variants/stock/payment-methods/tax-rules caching (offline cash-sale needs only product price + customer list); p_transaction_date on create_sale [SIGN-OFF #5 — prereqs: D6 closed-period guard (done) + ERR_PERIOD_CLOSED added to the D10 replay classifier (see D10 decision); DEFERRED by choice]; a true reachability probe (connectivity_plus reports interface only); transient-retry timed backoff beyond the attempts-cap; session-open-offline (handled by the open page's own RPC failure).

2026-07-16 — Sync D10 (migration 20260716160000_sync_replay_classifier_fix, LIVE — NOT a money RPC; plain create-or-replace of sync_replay_sale_intent, same 1-arg signature): closes bug class #7, the module's one real defect — a silent FAILED-at-cap path where a replayed sale never posts and NOTHING surfaces it. Dumped the live D5 body first (Claude Code has improved on spec before — verify, not assume); TWO surgical fixes to the exception handler, nothing else touched.
  (1) TERMINAL REGEX += NO_TENANT|PERMISSION_DENIED. create_sale's first three guards raise ERR_NO_TENANT / ERR_PERMISSION_DENIED / ERR_BRANCH_NOT_ASSIGNED (all errcode 42501); only BRANCH_NOT_ASSIGNED was in D5's terminal list. The other two are PRECISELY what an impersonating drain hits when a cashier's grant was revoked (PERMISSION_DENIED — reachable: user row stays, FK-safe) or the profile is tenant-less (NO_TENANT — defense-in-depth; user_id→users ON DELETE RESTRICT blocks deleting a cashier with a pending row, so mostly unreachable via the cron's own sub, but the client-drain path runs the live user's JWT). Neither ever succeeds on retry → both now ABANDONED + a sync_exceptions row. WAS: mis-classified TRANSIENT → FAILED, looped to attempts=5, then sync_drain_cron's `attempts < 5` filter EXCLUDED it → stuck FAILED forever, never in the Exception Centre. A real customer's sale vanished, no alarm.
  (2) AT-CAP SURFACING (the deeper fix — bug #7's actual lesson): a TRANSIENT failure that exhausts its retries (attempts+1 ≥ 5, the cap sync_drain_cron enforces) is ALSO never re-drained, so it needs a human exactly as much as a terminal one. Now `if v_terminal or v_at_cap then insert into sync_exceptions … where not exists (…outbox_id…)` — surface BOTH. Insert guarded not-exists (no unique index on sync_exceptions.outbox_id) → one row per intent. At-cap rows keep status FAILED (preserves the transient/terminal distinction for the human) but are now VISIBLE. Return jsonb gains at_cap (telemetry). The "no silent caps" rule: a bounded retry that drops a row must say so.
  GATE all green, rolled-back, BEFORE vs AFTER in ONE txn (new body applied in-txn, both probed): BEFORE (deployed) — NO_TENANT / PERMISSION_DENIED / transient@cap ALL → FAILED, exc=0 (three silent holes, reproduced). AFTER — NO_TENANT + PERMISSION_DENIED → ABANDONED, terminal, exc=1; transient@cap → FAILED, at_cap, exc=1; transient BELOW cap (attempts=0) → FAILED, exc=0 (net does NOT over-fire — early transients still retry silently); regex unit ERR_NO_TENANT/ERR_PERMISSION_DENIED→t, ERR_UNKNOWN→f (no false-terminal). Post-deploy re-probe of the LIVE fn = identical. PERMISSION_DENIED case seeded by flipping the ADMIN role's sales:create grant off/on inside the rolled-back txn; NO_TENANT by impersonating a phantom sub. Deploy: timestamp bumped 095724→160000 (supabase migration new stamped it before the D8 15:45 migration → out of order; renamed to sort after the latest applied).
  SIGN-OFF #5 (p_transaction_date) — decided in parallel: DEFERRED BY CHOICE, accepting a known latent misstatement (NOT "safe because current_date is always an OPEN period" — that framing was wrong; see the dedicated Sign-off #5 entry below for the correction). Posting at current_date IS the defect; the open period only makes it SILENT. D10 UNBLOCKS the eventual fix (ERR_PERIOD_CLOSED can then be classified terminal + surfaced); #5 stays deferred.

2026-07-16 — Sync D7.2 (EXTENDS lib/features/sync/ + migration 20260716151500_sync_push_intent, LIVE): replay drain + Exception Centre — Sync/Offline UI COMPLETE. GAP FOUND + FILLED (read the migrations, didn't assume): sync_outbox has NO insert policy and D5's sync_replay_sale_intent only operates on an EXISTING row → a device had no way to enqueue its local intent server-side. NEW definer sync_push_intent(key,branch,payload,client_created_at,local_ref,device) idempotent on uq_sync_outbox_idem (re-push same key → same row) → returns {outbox_id,status}; gated sales:create + branch, ACL revoked public/anon. DRAIN (on reconnect via a connectivity ref.listen in SyncStatusWidget, + manual "Sync now"): OLDEST-FIRST, ONE AT A TIME (serial — a single in-flight guard in DrainActions; serial replay is why there is nothing to merge). Per intent: push → if server already APPLIED reconcile-only, else sync_replay_sale_intent → applied/skipped → mark local DONE + fetch the REAL invoice_number by idempotency_key (uq_invoices_idem, ≤1) for reconciliation; terminal → ABANDONED (no retry); transient → FAILED + attempts++ (retry next reconnect, cap 5). "Drain twice / kill mid-drain → 3 invoices not 6" holds at THREE layers: idempotent push, replay status-guard, create_sale's own key guard; a killed replay is recovered next drain because push then reports APPLIED. Local sqflite v1→v2 (onUpgrade ALTER ADD): server_outbox_id/invoice_id/invoice_number/attempts/last_error/synced_at + idx_outbox_local_ref. SyncExceptionCentrePage /sync/exceptions (gated sync:read): OPEN sync_exceptions (error_code, detail, queued-at, the intent) with per-product "Adjust stock" deep-links to /inventory/products/:id — THAT is the resolution, not a JSON merge; Resolve+note → resolve_sync_exception (gated sync:resolve). SyncStatusSheet (tap the status chip): queue depth, last sync, per-intent status (PENDING/RETRYING/DONE→invoice#/ABANDONED) + Sync-now + Exceptions link. Reconciliation: findByRef searches local_ref OR real invoice_number so provisional paper is findable post-sync. GATE: sync_push_intent rolled-back — idempotent (2 pushes→1 row same id), full push→replay→APPLIED→real invoice INV-BR01-000006; flutter analyze clean (0 new; 7 standing lints); on-device airplane→3 sales→reconnect→3 invoices / kill-mid-drain / oversell→Exception Centre NOT driven from headless.

2026-07-16 — Sync D7.1 (lib/features/sync/, WRITE-path only; deps sqflite+sqflite_common_ffi[desktop FFI in main]+connectivity_plus+uuid+path): NEW clean-arch feature mirroring approvals — ONE sync_local_datasource (hand-written sqflite DAOs, raw parameterized SQL, NO drift/isar/build_runner) + ONE sync_remote_datasource (sync_pull_reference), sealed SyncFailure, thin usecases, plain-Riverpod controllers. Reference cache: pulls Class A delta over a locally-stored updated_at watermark (passed as p_since), persists products+customers to sqflite, rows arriving deleted_at≠null are EVICTED (the only delete signal); stock/variants/payment_methods/tax_rules NOT cached (offline cash-sale needs only product search/price [tax_rate is on the product] + customer list — YAGNI, extend if an offline flow needs them). ConnectivityMonitor = StreamProvider<bool> (connectivity_plus; interface-presence not reachability — noted). Outbox WRITE ONLY: offline a completed CASH sale persists a SALE intent (client uuid-v4 idempotency_key, client_created_at=now().toUtc(), provisional local_ref 'OFF-xxxxxxxx') — checkout(offline:true) NEVER calls create_sale (a device can't allocate the server row-locked next_number); REPLAY IS D7.2 (reconnect does NOT drain — correct). POS integration (no rebuild of the terminal): SyncStatusWidget (Offline/Pending(n)/Synced) in the app bar; offline is CASH-ONLY BY CONSTRUCTION — payment_sheet forces cash + full amount (non-cash dropdown disabled w/ tooltip, credit blocked), returns + Close Register disabled w/ reason, customer picker/creation blocked w/ reason (not hidden, not queued); product search + price resolve cache-first when offline via an offlineProductSearchProvider adapter (CachedProduct→inventory Product, inert fabricated fields unused on the sale path); success shows the local_ref labeled PROVISIONAL (not an invoice number) with no server-receipt call. GATE: flutter analyze clean (0 new; 7 standing pre-existing info lints); on-device airplane-mode flow NOT driven from this headless env. DEFERRED: session-open-offline handled by the open page's own RPC failure (not terminal-gated); D7.2 replay driver wiring (calls the live sync_replay_sale_intent).

2026-07-16 — Accounting D6 (migration 20260716140000_accounting_closed_period_guard, LIVE — MONEY RPC): post_journal (sole ledger writer, all 11 money paths) now raises ERR_PERIOD_CLOSED when the resolved fiscal period is CLOSED. D11.4 proved the check DID NOT EXIST — p_gate gates only auth_has_permission('accounting','create'); current_fiscal_period returns the covering period at ANY status and post_journal stamped it unconditionally. So fiscal_periods.status was DECORATIVE (full lifecycle, closed_by/closed_at, an M07 UI, read by no guard) — the exact tax_rules §8 pattern ("seeded, full CRUD UI, read by no RPC"). Neither predicted outcome ("p_gate bypasses the check" / "the check rejects") was live — there was NO check. Live exposure was narrow because auto-posts use current_date (always in an OPEN, lazily-created period), but a manual journal voucher takes a date, so an accountant could post into a closed period. Guard placed immediately after v_period resolves, deliberately UNCONDITIONAL (not behind p_gate): p_gate is a PERMISSION switch (auto-posts pass false), a closed period is an ACCOUNTING control — an auto-post must not write into a closed book merely because it isn't user-initiated. LIVE fix independent of sync + a hard prerequisite for back-dating offline sales [SIGN-OFF #5]: back-dating into a closed period would otherwise post SILENTLY (the 3rd silent-corruption finding after the analytics default partition + current_date dating). FORM: create or replace (same signature → clean replacement, ACL preserved — post_journal already had PUBLIC/anon EXECUTE live, left as-is per "change nothing else"; anon hits ERR_NO_TENANT anyway; pre-existing, not this phase's scope). Guard applied to the D0.5-dumped body verbatim; nothing else changed. GATE all green (rolled-back): regression FIRST — open-period post succeeds on BOTH p_gate arms AND a real create_sale still posts end-to-end (this RPC is the sole writer for all 11 paths); then a SEEDED closed period → ERR_PERIOD_CLOSED including on the ungated (p_gate=false) arm.

2026-07-16 — Sync D5 (migration 20260716134500_sync_replay_driver, LIVE): sync_replay_sale_intent(p_outbox_id) + resolve_sync_exception(p_id, p_note). Driver locks the intent `for update skip locked` (two drivers never fight over one row), sets REPLAYING (+attempts), routes THROUGH create_sale with the outbox's idempotency_key (sole writer, semantics unchanged; next_number allocates server-side at replay), then stamps APPLIED + invoice_id and gives invoices.is_offline/synced_at/device_id/local_ref their FIRST EVER writer (D0.3 proved nothing wrote is_offline/synced_at; D3 added the other two + idx_invoices_offline for exactly this). Failure classification is a DECISION not a default: create_sale's verbatim ERR_* list splits TERMINAL (INSUFFICIENT_STOCK/CREDIT_LIMIT/IMEI/BELOW_MIN_PRICE/… — never succeeds on retry → ABANDONED + a sync_exceptions row for a human) vs TRANSIENT (→ FAILED for backoff). resolve_sync_exception gated sync:resolve, tenant-scoped. Every enum write carries explicit ::enum (bug class #1). NO drain cron here — the M11 lesson verbatim (a live cron against an unproven guard mass-FAILs the queue); cron lands in D8 after green.
  FIX to the spec's SQL (latent bug): the terminal regex `v_code ~ 'ERR_(…|' || '…' || '…)'` compiled ONLY the first fragment — `~` binds tighter than `||`, so the pattern was `ERR_(…|` with an unbalanced paren → "parentheses not balanced". Parenthesised the whole concatenation: `v_code ~ ('…' || '…' || '…')`.
  SCOPE ADDITION beyond the spec (necessary + gate-proven): the spec's post-hoc stamp `update invoices set is_offline=…` was REJECTED by fn_invoice_immutability (ERR_INVOICE_IMMUTABLE) — create_sale finalizes the invoice to PAID and the live trigger blocks ALL updates to a PAID/RETURNED/VOID invoice. Without a fix the entire happy path fails and is_offline/idx_invoices_offline stay vestigial forever. Relaxed fn_invoice_immutability (reconciled to its live body) to permit a METADATA-ONLY stamp on a finalized invoice: allowed only when status is unchanged AND `(to_jsonb(new) - {is_offline,synced_at,device_id,local_ref,updated_at,updated_by}) = (to_jsonb(old) - same)` — a jsonb diff, so NO financial/business field can slip through. Financial immutability fully intact (gate-proven: a grand_total mutation on the PAID offline invoice still raises ERR_INVOICE_IMMUTABLE). ACL hardening (create_sale lesson): both new definer fns had EXECUTE re-granted to PUBLIC by default → revoked from public/anon, granted authenticated/service_role. GATE all green (rolled-back; intents SEEDED AS OWNER because sync_outbox has no insert policy — authenticated insert is 42501 per D2 write-block, so the spec's "seed as authenticated" can't run; replay + resolve run as authenticated): D5-A valid intent → APPLIED + is_offline/synced_at/local_ref(OFF-D1-000001) stamped; D5-B re-replay → skipped (status guard) + still ONE invoice; D5-C oversell → ABANDONED + exactly 1 sync_exceptions + ZERO invoices; resolve → RESOLVED; immutability regression BLOCKED_GREEN. NEXT: D8 drain cron (keys-first-cron-second, like M11) + Flutter offline queue, still gated on sign-offs #1 (numbering) & #2.

2026-07-16 — Sync D4 (migration 20260716133000_sync_create_sale_idempotency, LIVE — MONEY RPC): create_sale gains p_idempotency_key uuid default null. A replay is a duplicate DELIVERY, not a duplicate SALE → the guard returns the ORIGINAL invoice payload; it never raises. D0/D8 proved create_sale had ZERO replay protection: two calls with the same logical intent = two invoices, two next_number burns, two GL posts — naive replay-on-reconnect double-posts the ledger. FORM: drop+create in ONE migration, NOT create or replace — a 7-arg create_or_replace makes an OVERLOAD and every live 6-arg Flutter call dies on "function create_sale is not unique"; db push wraps the file in a txn so there is no window where the RPC is absent. PLACEMENT (load-bearing): guard sits AFTER the permission guards (ERR_NO_TENANT/PERMISSION_DENIED/BRANCH_NOT_ASSIGNED) and BEFORE next_number — next_number is a gap-free FOR UPDATE counter (D7), so a guard after it would burn a number on every replay and gap the sequence, spending the exact property sign-off #1 protects. RACE: uq_invoices_idem (D3) catches concurrent replays; the unique_violation handler re-reads and returns the winner, discarding the loser's subtransaction — and is GUARDED (only swallows when p_idempotency_key is not null AND the row is now found, else re-raises) so every existing error path is byte-identical. Dump-and-reconcile: guard applied to the D0.5-dumped live body verbatim (used real var name v_t, not the spec's v_tenant); nothing else changed; SET search_path='public' preserved. ACL-HARDENING (not in the spec, a real fix): `create function` re-grants EXECUTE to PUBLIC by default, but the live create_sale ACL was postgres/authenticated/service_role only — so the fresh function briefly exposed PUBLIC+anon (harmless: anon hits ERR_NO_TENANT immediately, but a least-privilege regression). Added `revoke execute … from public, anon` to the migration + applied it live; ACL now identical to the original. p_transaction_date DEFERRED — [SIGN-OFF #5] not signed off, so no second rewrite bundled. GATE all green (rolled-back, seeded cost≠price so a wrong COGS could not equal the right one): D4-A regression FIRST — 6-arg null-key call still makes two distinct invoices; D4-B fix — same key → one invoice; D4-C ledger — invoices=1, journals=1, stock_moves=1 (NOT double-posted); D4-D counter advanced EXACTLY 3 (reg1+reg2+one replayed sale), not 4 → replay did not burn a number; replay payload identical (grand 149.8553 both). NEXT: D5 replay driver (drain sync_outbox PENDING → create_sale w/ its idempotency_key → APPLIED / sync_exceptions), still gated on sign-off #1 (numbering) & #2.

2026-07-16 — Sync D3 (migration 20260716131500_sync_invoice_idempotency, LIVE): invoices += idempotency_key (uuid), device_id (uuid → devices, on delete set null), local_ref (text). Pure additive/nullable — zero impact on existing rows or the 7 live payments; deliberately NOT bundled with the D4 create_sale rewrite. uq_invoices_idem = PARTIAL unique (tenant_id, idempotency_key) WHERE idempotency_key is not null — partial because every ONLINE sale leaves the key null and nulls must stay non-unique; this index (not D4's in-function guard) is the un-raceable last line of defence against a double-post if two replay drivers run concurrently. Plus idx_invoices_local_ref (find a provisional paper receipt once the real number is assigned [SIGN-OFF #1(a)]) and idx_invoices_offline — which schema line ~1133 CLAIMED exists but D0.3 proved absent, now actually created. is_offline/synced_at remain live-but-vestigial (nothing writes them yet; D5 is their first writer). GATE all green (rolled-back, real required cols tenant/branch/invoice_number/cashier): D3-A regression total unchanged=5, keyed=0 (no existing row keyed); D3-B duplicate non-null key → 23505 unique_violation (distinct invoice_number so the violation can ONLY be uq_invoices_idem); D3-C — THE ONE THAT MATTERS — two null keys both insert (partial index does not block nulls; an index that also blocked nulls would break every online sale). NEXT: D4 create_sale idempotency guard (replay of a seen key returns the original invoice, never double-posts), gated on sign-offs #1 (numbering) & #2.

2026-07-16 — Sync D2 (migration 20260716075906_sync_foundation, LIVE): sync_outbox + sync_exceptions + 2 enums (sync_intent_status_enum, sync_exception_status_enum) + `sync` perm module. New tables only, nothing wired, no money path touched. The device NEVER creates an invoice — it appends a SALE intent carrying a client-generated idempotency_key (uq_sync_outbox_idem on (tenant_id, idempotency_key) = the replay contract) + the true client_created_at. An append-only queue replayed serially has NOTHING to merge, which is why §3.3's sync_log/sync_conflicts (local/server_version_json, LWW/MERGE) are deliberately NOT built — they are the superseded NestJS/BullMQ design, and LWW on anything that posted a journal is silent destruction of a sale. Replay failures are BUSINESS events (e.g. ERR_INSUFFICIENT_STOCK — customer already left with the phone), worked by a human via sync_exceptions (OPEN→RESOLVED/WRITTEN_OFF), never a JSON diff. intent_type CHECK restricts to 'SALE' (Class B is SALE-only in v1). RLS tenant-read only; NO insert/update/delete policy → definer RPCs are the sole writers (approvals-foundation pattern). `sync` perm module (read/resolve) backfilled to all 5 tenant ADMIN roles + trg_seed_sync_perms mirrors seed_approvals_perms_for_admin verbatim (dumped live, not written from memory) — this is the 7th seed_*_perms trigger (fragmentation already flagged as cleanup; 7th added for consistency, not solved). CORRECTED the pasted spec's line 53 `insert into permissions (module, action)` — permissions.role_id is NOT NULL and there is no (module,action) catalog; permissions are per-role grants (uq on role_id,module,action), so the catalog insert would null-violate; replaced with the approvals-style ADMIN backfill. GATE all green (rolled-back, full DDL applied ephemerally): backfill = 10 rows (5 ADMIN × read/resolve); tenant isolation own_rows=1 / foreign_rows=0 read as the Ahmad user under `set local role authenticated` (RLS enforced for the authenticated role; read policy is pure tenant scoping, no auth_has_permission, so admin≡cashier — NO cashier seeded, unlike the pasted spec's claim, because it adds nothing here and D0.7 shows no non-admin user exists); WRITE-BLOCK: direct authenticated insert → 42501 insufficient_privilege (a success here would be RED — the server-authoritative-replay premise depends on the client being unable to forge outbox rows). NEXT: D5 replay RPC (drain PENDING → create_sale with idempotency guard → APPLIED/exception), still gated on sign-offs #1 (numbering) & #2.

2026-07-15 — Settings S6 (lib/features/settings/, no migration): built the Settings feature. SettingsHubPage REPLACES the bottom-nav /settings target (was the misfiled auth SettingsPage), gated settings:read, linking 8 surfaces: Profile & Security (existing auth page, now reached at /settings/profile), Business settings (update_tenant_settings: address/phone/logo_url/receipt_footer/ntn), Branches (update_branch_settings: name/city/country/currency/timezone/active), Payment methods (payment_methods CRUD + Link bank account → row shows the RESOLVED GL account per method via resolve_payment_account; unlinked → Cash 1000, link CARD→bank → Bank 1010 — the split the user SEES), Tax rules (existing TaxRulesPage, accounting route kept), Number series (read-only; fiscal_year_reset shown DISABLED with tooltip — inert, no year component [SIGN-OFF #1]), Preferences (upsert_ui_preferences: theme[light-only]/language/default_branch), Notifications (link to /notifications/settings). Clean-arch: ONE settings_remote_datasource (no Supabase outside it), typed SettingsFailure never swallowed, plain Riverpod, all writes gated settings:update. formatPkr now sources its symbol from the active branch's currency (branches.currency added to the auth Branch entity + its select; setDisplayCurrency set on branch selection; display-only, no arithmetic changed). VIOLATION LOGGED (deferred): the auth SettingsPage was NOT physically relocated to settings/ — moving it cross-feature breaks its many auth-relative imports (auth_controller/profile_controller/get_enrolled_factor_id/pin_service); it stays in lib/features/auth/ and is reused as the /settings/profile hub entry (the hub is the goal). analyze clean; RPC contracts gate-proven in S1–S5; GUI E2E (reorder/link/persist across sign-out) not driven from this headless env.

2026-07-16 — Sign-off #5 (p_transaction_date) — DEFERRED BY CHOICE, accepting a known latent misstatement. NOT "safe because current_date is always an OPEN period" — posting at current_date IS the defect; the open period is what makes it SILENT. D6's guard was the PREREQUISITE for the fix, never a risk of the bug: no D6 conflict can ever occur precisely because nothing objects. Real harm: an offline sale synced after midnight is booked on the wrong DAY; synced across a month boundary it lands in the wrong FISCAL PERIOD (journal balances, trial_balance true, every gate green, that month's revenue understated) — and it worsens the longer a device stays offline. ACCEPTED because exposure needs a device offline across a date boundary: the drain cron runs every 5 min, cashier sessions must be closed online so most offline sales settle same-day, and no offline sale has yet been taken in production. A latent trap, not broken books — same shape as M12's first-card-payment trap (§8 corrected belief #2). REVISIT when (a) any tenant reports a device offline overnight, or (b) before month-end close in any month where offline sales occurred. WHEN BUILT: p_transaction_date on create_sale (own drop+create, ONE-RPC-ONE-MIGRATION) + add ERR_PERIOD_CLOSED to the D10 classifier — D6 already blocks the closed-period write and D10 makes its surfacing work.

2026-07-16 — Sync D11 (migration 20260716161000_sync_retry_intent, LIVE — NOT money; retry re-queues + delegates to create_sale): closed the exception GRAVEYARD. Before this, an ABANDONED / FAILED-at-cap intent was permanently outside both drains (sync_drain_cron filters status in (PENDING,FAILED) AND attempts<5; ABANDONED and attempts=5 match neither) and resolve_sync_exception only ANNOTATED the exception — the lost sale (shop already handed over the phone) NEVER entered the books: no invoice, no revenue, no COGS, no stock decrement, just a tidy note saying so. D5's doc line called resolution "an inventory adjustment or credit override" but never wrote the step where the sale actually posts. The safety mechanism already existed and was wired to nothing: D4's idempotency key means an intent can be re-queued any number of times and yield exactly one invoice (already-applied → returns the original). TWO changes, one feature:
  (A) sync_replay_sale_intent — scope the exception-insert not-exists guard to OPEN rows (`where not exists (… and status='OPEN')`). Without this, a retry that RESOLVES the old exception then fails AGAIN is suppressed (a RESOLVED row for the outbox_id exists) → silent again. OPEN-scoping re-surfaces a fresh failure. Unchanged for first-failure (no prior OPEN exists → still inserts).
  (B) retry_sync_intent(p_outbox_id) NEW, definer, gated sync:resolve, tenant-scoped: guard status ∈ {ABANDONED,FAILED} (APPLIED → no-op returning the original invoice; PENDING/REPLAYING → ERR_INTENT_NOT_RETRYABLE, in-flight). Stamps the OPEN exception(s) RESOLVED (who retried), re-queues (PENDING, attempts=0, last_error=null), then REPLAYS INLINE impersonating the row's original user_id (correct cashier_id, exactly like sync_drain_cron) and restores the caller's JWT claims after. Inline replay — NOT pure re-queue — because a Centre exception is a SERVER sync_outbox row the client drain can't reach (it drains the local sqflite queue) and sync_drain_cron is service_role-only, so a pure re-queue would strand the admin waiting for the 5-min cron to recover a sale. ACL: revoke public/anon, grant authenticated/service_role (create_sale/D5 lesson).
  GATE all green (rolled-back, real create_sale over product 662a6ef6 @ Ahmad Store, terminal error toggled via the ADMIN sales:create grant): G1 graveyard — resolve → exc RESOLVED but outbox ABANDONED, invoices=0 (sale NOT posted, the bug). G2 — revoke→replay→ABANDONED+OPEN exc; grant back→retry → retried=true, applied=true, outbox APPLIED, invoices=1, journals=1, cashier=original (attribution correct), exc RESOLVED. G3 — retry an already-APPLIED intent → already_applied=true, invoices=1 (no double-post; D4 key proven). G4 — retry that fails AGAIN (still revoked) → open_exc=1 + total_exc=2 (1 resolved + 1 fresh OPEN) → re-failure re-surfaces via the OPEN-scoped guard. Post-deploy live re-probe: recovery applied=true invoices=1; ACL anon=false authenticated=true service_role=true. Flutter: Retry action added to SyncExceptionCentrePage next to Resolve (gated sync:resolve, confirm dialog — it posts real money), wired datasource→repo→usecase→DrainActions.retryIntent across the existing resolve chain; on success invalidates exceptions/intents + refreshes the outbox count.

2026-07-16 — Sync deferral: an ABANDONED intent whose error is BYPASSABLE cannot be rescued by retry_sync_intent. ERR_BELOW_MIN_PRICE and ERR_CREDIT_LIMIT_EXCEEDED are bypassable with sales:approve, but retry impersonates the ORIGINAL CASHIER (correct for attribution), so an admin's own sales:approve never applies and the retry re-fails forever. ERR_CREDIT_LIMIT_EXCEEDED is unreachable by construction (offline is cash-only, D7.1). ERR_BELOW_MIN_PRICE is reachable only if the offline POS lets a cashier price below min_selling_price — which sync_pull_reference DOES cache (D1), so the client can enforce it; UNVERIFIED until device E2E. Today's only resolution is mutating the product's min_selling_price and retrying — a catalog edit to force a sale through, which is wrong. Proper fix: an approve-on-retry path (p_approve on retry_sync_intent, requiring the RESOLVER's sales:approve, passed through to create_sale). Narrow, conditional, and unverifiable until the pod bump. Logged, not built.

2026-07-16 — macOS build fix (branch A, deployment-target mismatch) + RE-SCOPE of the device-E2E blocker. RE-PROBED the long-standing "speech_to_text macOS blocker" before touching anything. TWO builds, verbatim: (1a) `flutter build macos` FAILS — "The plugin speech_to_text requires a higher minimum macOS deployment version… increase to at least 11.00"; speech_to_text 7.4.0's darwin podspec declares `s.osx.deployment_target='11.00'` (ios '13.0'). (1b — THE ONE THAT MATTERS) `flutter build apk` ALSO FAILS, but on a COMPLETELY DIFFERENT dependency: `:file_picker` "Namespace not specified" (file_picker 3.0.4 android/build.gradle has no namespace → incompatible with AGP 8). ⇒ THE ANDROID BUILD WAS NOT ALREADY WORKING. The board's "speech_to_text macOS blocker blocks device E2E app-wide" is wrong on both counts: speech_to_text only ever blocked the DESKTOP build; Android — the PRODUCTION platform the shop actually runs — has been blocked all along by file_picker, an unreported blocker. macOS-only fix does NOT unblock device E2E; that requires the separate file_picker bump (next commit). speech_to_text IS used (voice_input_service.dart, voice search) so NOT removable; file_picker IS used (import_products_page, migration_import_controller). FIX (branch A, macOS only, surgical, pre-authorized): macos/Podfile `platform :osx '10.15'→'11.0'` + all 3 MACOSX_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET in project.pbxproj `10.15→11.0` (value-only, kept identical), then `pod install` (NOT update). Raising the floor only drops pre-11.0 macOS support — zero production cost (no tenant runs the POS on a Mac; macOS is a dev tap-through surface). BLAST RADIUS: git touched macos/Podfile + macos/Podfile.lock + project.pbxproj (+docs). Podfile.lock: NO existing pod moved VERSION — the 6 previously-locked pods (app_links 6.4.1, FlutterMacOS 1.0.0, screen_retriever_macos, shared_preferences_foundation, url_launcher_macos, window_manager 0.5.0) are byte-identical; the diff is pure ADDITIONS (connectivity_plus, mobile_scanner, sqflite_darwin, flutter_secure_storage_darwin, local_auth_darwin, device_info_plus, printing, share_plus, speech_to_text 7.2.0 + its deps CwlCatchException/Support) because the macOS lock was STALE — never re-installed since those plugins (all already pinned in pubspec.lock, used app-wide) were added while the build was broken. `pod install` reconciled it; no `pod update` re-resolution. VERIFY: regression `flutter build apk` still fails IDENTICALLY on file_picker (macOS change did not touch Android); `flutter build macos` ✓ Built pos_app.app (69MB); `flutter run -d macos` boots through sqflite FFI + Supabase init and reaches the router/login (0 crashes; "Failed to foreground app" is a benign windowing message). flutter analyze 7 standing lints, 0 new (no Dart touched). REPORT-ONLY (one platform per commit, NOT fixed): ios/Podfile has `# platform :ios,'13.0'` COMMENTED OUT → iOS would fail the same speech_to_text way if ever built; and Android file_picker bump is the next commit.

2026-07-16 — Android build fix (file_picker bump, the production-platform unblock; separate commit from macOS, user-approved). The APK's real blocker was file_picker 3.0.4 (no AGP-8 namespace), NOT speech_to_text. RESOLUTION WAS NOT a clean bump: EVERY stable namespace-bearing file_picker (5.3.2–11.x) pins win32 ^5.x, but the app's share_plus 13.1.0 AND device_info_plus 13.1.0 both pin win32 ^6.0.1 → version-solve conflict. Only file_picker >=12.0.0-beta.1 uses win32 ^6.x. Options weighed: (A) file_picker 12.0.0-beta.7 — resolves with 1-dep change, no transitive movement; (B) swap to file_selector — no beta but +8 deps and a full lib/ API migration; (C) downgrade share_plus+device_info_plus — regresses two USED packages. Chose A (lowest blast radius; file_picker is exercised only on two admin-only CSV-import screens, never money/POS/sync). BETA accepted because its 12.0-beta line is a win32-6 dependency migration, not a feature rewrite, and the surface is low-criticality. A required a lib/ change after all (my "no lib/" estimate was WRONG): file_picker 12 refactored FilePicker to STATIC methods (CHANGELOG: `FilePicker.platform.pickFiles()` → `FilePicker.pickFiles()`). STOPPED and got explicit approval before editing lib/ (the standing build-config guardrail). Applied: pubspec `file_picker ^3.0.4 → 12.0.0-beta.7` (EXACT pin — control a beta; revisit to `^12.0.0` when 12 goes stable) + removed `.platform` at the 2 call sites (import_products_page.dart:58, migration_import_controller.dart:134) — nothing else. `withData`/`PlatformFile.bytes` are DEPRECATED in 12 (favor readAsBytes()) but still functional → KEPT to preserve exact byte-load behavior within the approved 2-line scope; they add 6 deprecation infos (analyze 7→13, all `deprecated_member_use` in those 2 files, ZERO errors, ZERO in untouched files). Future cleanup: migrate withData/bytes → PlatformFile.readAsBytes() (own task). BLAST RADIUS: pubspec.lock moved ONLY file_picker (no transitive); macos/Flutter/GeneratedPluginRegistrant.swift regenerated to register file_picker's now-existing macOS plugin (generated artifact of the bump; macOS Podfile.lock self-heals on next macOS build — not touched here, one platform per commit); ios/Podfile.lock NOT touched. VERIFY: `flutter build apk` ✓ Built app-release.apk (94.8MB) — the Android production build is UNBLOCKED for the first time; regression `flutter build macos` unaffected (commit 1 stands). Device tap-through of the 2 import flows still pending (headless). NOTE for the record: this + the macOS fix mean the "14 modules device-tap-through-pending" claim was gated by TWO real build blockers (macOS speech_to_text + Android file_picker), neither the single app-wide speech_to_text blocker the board named.
2026-07-16 — V0 pre-flight: recorded actual production volume (invoices/payments/users per tenant) against the standing "5 live tenants, production database is live" claim. The production APK had never compiled until today's file_picker fix, so no Android device can have been transacting — the claim was asserted, not observed. RESULT: FALSE AS STATED. Only 1 of 5 tenants (97516251-b1ed-4647-a69f-4398eca8cf20) has any invoices (5, all 2026-06-18→06-20) or payments (7, sum 7,750 PKR, most recent 06-20) — test-scale, not a business. Only 2 of 5 tenants have even a single user (1 each); the other 3 are empty shells (created, never logged into). sync_outbox is EMPTY (0 rows, all statuses) — zero sync intents have ever existed, confirming zero Android devices have run this app, consistent with the APK never having built. VERDICT: pre-launch, not live. RISK BUDGET SET: no real tenant is exposed to the file_picker 12.0.0-beta.7 pin or to any RED found in V1–V5 — every fix below is free to do properly, no incident-mode shortcuts needed.

2026-07-16 — Closeout D1 (migrations number_series_seed_missing + next_number_raise_on_unseeded): seeded JOURNAL_ENTRY (JV-) + RECEIPT_VOUCHER (RV-) number series for all 5 tenants and extended provision_tenant so new tenants get them. Sign-off #3 was framed "seed or drop" — it is NOT a choice: PostgreSQL has ALTER TYPE ADD VALUE but no DROP VALUE, so dropping the labels means recreating number_series_type_enum, altering every dependent column, and drop+creating next_number (whose p_type IS that enum, hitting D4's ambiguous-overload trap) — all to delete two unused strings. Seeding is two INSERTs. Backfill + provisioner BOTH, because a backfill alone recreates the Ahmad Store bug verbatim (created 2026-06-18 after the stock_ops backfill loop, permanently missing 2 series — the live victim of exactly this fault). Explicit ::number_series_type_enum on every insert: bug class #1 (text→enum, hit 3×) fires on values flowing through a VALUES list. SALES_RETURN NOT added — D0.1 proved it is not a valid enum label. GATE: regression FIRST (INVOICE/PAYMENT_VOUCHER still allocate: INV-BR01-000006, PV-BR01-000001), then the fix (JOURNAL_ENTRY → JV-BR01-000001, RECEIPT_VOUCHER → RV-BR01-000001, real padded branch-coded numbers, not junk), then EVERY tenant compared for equal counts (all 5 tenants at 10 series, no outliers — the bug was "fails for everyone", so a gate on one tenant would have proven nothing), then a freshly-provisioned tenant (rolled back) got the full Golden 10 including both new types. D0.4 OUTCOME was (c): next_number returned junk ('BR01-000001', no type prefix) on an unseeded type rather than raising or returning NULL — so next_number now raises ERR_NUMBER_SERIES_NOT_SEEDED on a missing series (separate migration: next_number is a sole gap-free counter used by all 11 money-document paths, not itself a money RPC — one RPC per migration).

2026-07-16 — Closeout D2 (migration number_series_drop_fiscal_year_reset): dropped number_series.fiscal_year_reset per sign-off #1 (FBR does not require year-reset numbering). D0.8 proved empty set — no function anywhere read this column, the third instance of the "seeded, has a UI, read by no code" pattern (after tax_rules and fiscal_periods.status pre-guard). Two paths were on the table: drop (one additive-negative migration, no RPC touched, honest that the feature was never built) or build a real module (year component in every series format e.g. INV-BR01-2026-000001, a reset job, a numbering-format migration across all 10 series, PLUS a collision with the sync numbering deferral requiring a joint decision). User chose drop. No RPC change needed — provision_tenant never named the column explicitly, relied on the table default, so nothing broke. Client cleanup in the same commit: removed NumberSeriesInfo.fiscalYearReset, its settings_models mapper line, the datasource select column, and the disabled Switch/tooltip on the number series settings page — dead column would have 500'd that SELECT otherwise. flutter analyze unchanged at 13 (no new issues). VERIFY: information_schema confirms the column is gone; the app's remaining 4-column select (type, prefix, padding, current_number) still returns real rows against Ahmad Store.

2026-07-16 — Closeout D3: PUSH channel toggle disabled-with-reason in the notification preferences UI. The UI offered PUSH, the sender has an FCM branch, and no device can register a token (the Flutter half is deferred) — so PUSH was a switch a user could turn on that could never deliver. Disabled rather than hidden: a missing control reads as a bug, a disabled one with a reason reads as a roadmap (same pattern as the offline credit-sale control). NOT gated on provider keys — no Twilio/SendGrid/pg_net involved; it was only ever listed under M11 because it must precede the drain cron, which is not registered. Sender FCM-branch behaviour FAIL, not SKIP (index.ts:140-156, "no active device token" -> status FAILED, same as any real transient error) — reported, not fixed here. It MUST become skip-not-fail before any key is set, or the first live drain burns retries on structurally-undeliverable rows and buries the queue forever (every PUSH row, since zero devices can ever register a token) — the M11 ordering warning, and the exact failure shape my own sync D8 cron shipped with before it was caught. 0 existing notification_preferences rows carry PUSH; the disabled chip (onTap=null) was the only client write path that could add it, confirmed by grep — no other call site touches NotificationChannel.push. flutter analyze unchanged at 13.

2026-07-16 — Closeout D4 (no migration, no code change — stayed on file_picker 12.0.0-beta.7): re-ran the win32-override escape hatch as its own gated phase (first tried ad hoc during V1). `dependency_overrides: win32: ^6.0.0` + file_picker ^11.0.2 resolves cleanly (`flutter pub get`: "Changed 1 dependency!" — file_picker only, win32 itself doesn't move since the app already needed ^6.0.1 via share_plus/device_info_plus). flutter analyze on that state: the 6 withData/bytes deprecation infos DO disappear as predicted (13->7) — file_picker 11.0.2 predates that deprecation. But `flutter build apk --release` FAILS: 6 compile errors in file_picker-11.0.2's own vendored `platform/windows/file_picker_windows.dart` (HRESULT type mismatch, SUCCEEDED() argument type, getDisplayName arg-count — win32 6.x changed HRESULT from a bare int to a distinct type, and file_picker's Windows platform source was never ported for any release before the 12.0-beta line). The rejection is NOT at pub-resolve time (the override succeeds there) — it is at KERNEL COMPILE time, invisible to analyze, and it fires even building for Android because file_picker's library barrel unconditionally exports all three desktop platform sources (linux/macos/windows), gated only on web via dart.library.js_interop. Analyze lied again: clean/improved analyze output, broken build. STAYS ON BETA 12.0.0-beta.7 — no stable file_picker release has Windows platform code compatible with win32 6.x. Per the gate: the withData/PlatformFile.bytes -> readAsBytes() deprecation migration is now the ONLY compat surface separating the app from a pre-release package on the onboarding path — recommended as the next concrete task, not yet done here (out of D4's scope; flagged, not executed).

2026-07-16 — Closeout D5 (migration cron_bootstrap): all 7 live pg_cron jobs (approvals_escalation_hourly, mv_refresh_15min, notif_detectors_daily, reorder_recommendations_daily, report_schedules_runner, sync_outbox_drain_5min, verify_provisioning_daily) written into migrations/ for the first time — previously 7 live, 0 in migrations/, so rebuilding prod from migrations/ alone would silently produce a database with no scheduled work. Bug class #7 at the infrastructure layer: a queue that fills and never empties, with no error, no signal. Count had grown 6->7 within this session alone. RECONCILED-BEFORE-PUSH: dumped jobname/schedule/command/active from prod cron.job first and pasted all 7 verbatim — the docs were never the source of truth, these were hand-typed into the SQL editor over weeks. Used cron.schedule(job_name, schedule, command), confirmed upsert-by-jobname on this project (3-arg overload exists). GATE: 7/7 active, schedules unchanged, verified NOT by re-running and counting (false green — proves the insert ran, not that nothing moved) but by dumping cron.job before and after the push and diffing byte-for-byte: every jobname/schedule/command/active identical except the query tool's own per-call nonce. No job was silently re-timed or re-bodied by the reconciliation.

2026-07-16 — Closeout D6 (migration create_sale_authoritative_tax): create_sale now resolves tax from tax_rules instead of trusting the caller's p_tax_pct. Sign-off #2 closed. This MOVES INVOICE TOTALS (p_tax_pct omitted: null->0 becomes null->17%), which is why it waited for V0 to price it: V0 confirmed pre-launch (1 tenant, 5 real invoices, 7 payments summing 7,750 PKR, test-scale; sync_outbox empty) — free today. tax_rules was the §8 "decorative" finding (seeded, full CRUD UI, read by no RPC); M12 made it authoritative at DATA-ENTRY time only, leaving create_sale trusting the client — this closes the other half. Third live rewrite of create_sale, isolated, one RPC one migration, applied to a freshly re-dumped body (not memory). No caller override kept (D0.7 showed no legitimate path needing one — every tax_rules row is applies_to='ALL'); every line resolves once per sale from the tenant's is_default+is_active rule, before next_number (fail fast, same placement logic as the idempotency guard). Non-EXCLUSIVE mode also raises (ERR_TAX_MODE_UNSUPPORTED) since the existing arithmetic is exclusive-only. GATE (rolled back, run against Madina Mobile): D6-A client tax_pct=99 produces the SAME total as the resolved default (1170, not 1990) — override confirmed inert, no error; D6-B tax_pct omitted also produces 1170 not 1000 — proves the fix (a sale already passing the right rate would prove nothing, same family as cost=price); D6-C journal_lines for the D6-B invoice balance exactly and Output Tax 2100 is credited 170 — the ledger reflects the new tax, not just the receipt; D6-D every tax_rule deactivated -> next sale raises ERR_NO_ACTIVE_TAX_RULE. No-active-rule case: RAISE, not silent-0 (bug class #7 discipline, same choice as D1's next_number fix). Post-rollback: 0 invoices left on the test tenant, 4 tax_rules reactivated.

2026-07-17 — D3 closeout, part 2 (migrations notification_status_add_skipped + notify_skip_push_no_transport, deploy notification-sender): PUSH rows now SKIP, not FAIL, and notify() stops creating them at all. FINDING STATED PLAINLY: zero PUSH rows and zero PUSH preferences existed anywhere (0 rows in both notifications and notification_preferences, all channels, all statuses) — this was a LATENT guard being closed, not an active leak being stopped. Also notable: notification_preferences is completely empty, meaning notify() — M11's single dispatch producer — has never executed in production at all; M11 is (correctly) marked complete for code but has zero rows of evidence it has ever run. Corrected rationale from the original diagnosis: the harm of FAIL-not-SKIP is NOT "burns retries and clogs the queue" (notifications has no attempts column, and a FAILED row drops out of the sender's .eq("status","PENDING") query permanently) — it is that this sender is ONE-SHOT with no retry at all, so a mass-FAIL doesn't clog anything, it ANNIHILATES the row, unrecoverable except by hand-resetting status. Two migrations for a one-concern-per-commit reason, not the usual same-transaction enum trap (the sender writes SKIPPED at runtime, long after either migration commits, so that trap doesn't actually bind here): (1) SKIPPED added to notification_status_enum, justified as general-purpose — no DROP VALUE in Postgres, same one-way door as D1 — independent of PUSH (no-email-on-file, opted-out-mid-flight, retired channel all have the same gap); (2) notify() — confirmed the sole path any PUSH row could ever be created through — now skips inserting a PUSH row per-channel entirely ("no PUSH row, no problem"), reversible in one line when device tokens ship. Sender fix (Edge Function, deployed, not a migration) is belt-and-braces: marks a no-token PUSH row SKIPPED with a reason (reusing failed_reason, no schema change), leaves a real post-token send failure FAILED so a genuine transient error isn't swept into "never going to work," and reports sent/failed/skipped as three separate counts. GATE: notify() proven via rolled-back SQL call (same technique as D6) — seeded channels=["IN_APP","PUSH"], call returned {enqueued:1, skipped:["PUSH"]}, zero PUSH rows created, IN_APP still DELIVERED. Live HTTP invocation of the deployed sender was attempted but abandoned after repeated credential-handling friction (see incident note below) — sender code was reviewed instead of live-triggered; the DB-level fix (notify()'s guard) is the one proven end-to-end. Blast radius: exactly 3 files (2 migrations + the sender), no cron file touched, cron.job still 7, no provider key referenced/required/set anywhere in the diff. INCIDENT: during live-invocation testing, the user pasted a live secret API key and later the legacy service_role JWT directly into chat twice; both are now exposed in this transcript and MUST be rotated (Settings -> API -> regenerate) regardless of whether this note is read before or after that happens. Flagged, not built: the sender's one-shot no-retry design is the real M11 gap — a genuine transient failure (SendGrid down 30s) permanently kills a real notification today, independent of PUSH; "keys first, cron second" is a workaround for that gap, not a fix.

2026-07-17 — Phase 1 SECURITY FIXES (migration phase1_security_fixes + client cut), first phase of the QR staff-onboarding runbook v3, ships alone. CRITICAL self-escalation closed: the "users update own" RLS policy had cmd=UPDATE, roles={public}, with_check=NULL — Postgres reuses USING for the new row when WITH CHECK is absent, so any authenticated user (incl. a CASHIER) could `update public.users set role_id=<admin role in own tenant>` and self-promote (ADMIN role id readable via the unrestricted "roles tenant read" policy; no trigger on public.users guards role_id; authenticated holds table-level UPDATE). Proven by policy inspection, NOT a live repro — the Management API path runs as admin/superuser with auth.uid()=NULL and bypasses RLS, so a CLI UPDATE would "succeed" for the wrong reason; a true repro needs a cashier JWT. FIX: new WITH CHECK requires new row keep old tenant_id/role_id via STABLE SECURITY DEFINER helpers auth_tenant_id()/auth_role_id() (both read the pre-update committed snapshot), plus 1.4 column-grant belt-and-braces: revoke table UPDATE from authenticated, re-grant only (full_name, phone, avatar_url, pin_hash) — the exact + only columns 0.4 found the client writes (pin_hash at auth_remote_datasource.dart:196; everything else is SELECT). 1.5A (owner chose Option A of A/B/C): the anon-callable increment_failed_login/reset_failed_login were a pre-auth DoS (anyone with the anon key could lock any account 5×→15min, unlock any account, or enumerate emails) — v2's existence-check only half-fixed it (the lock-a-known-email vector is unfixable while increment must stay anon-callable). Option A removes the surface entirely: revoke EXECUTE from anon/public on both, delete the client call sites (2 datasource methods + login_throttle_repository interface + impl both deleted as now-empty orphans), and rewrite LoginThrottleService to local-secure-storage-only, throwing AccountLockedException locally on cooldown so SignInController's catch is unchanged. Supabase Auth rate-limits sign-in natively (Confirm-email ON confirmed by owner). 1.6 provision_tenant hardened: revoke EXECUTE from public/anon (0.9d proved only handle_new_user, a SECURITY DEFINER owned by postgres, calls it — so revoke is safe and sufficient), grant authenticated, plus an in-body guard `if auth.uid() is not null and p_tenant_id is distinct from auth_tenant_id() then raise ERR_FORBIDDEN_TENANT` — the auth.uid()-IS-NULL exemption is load-bearing: during a GoTrue signup the trigger runs with no session (users row not yet created), so that path MUST skip the guard and DID (R1). 1.9 hierarchy_level backfill: 3 of (now 8) tenants were dirty at 99/99 (Demo Store, Marina Mobile, Marina Mobile Shop — signups predating the 1/5 seed) → normalized to ADMIN=1/CASHIER=5; the drift is the dangerous direction (a SUB_ADMIN at level 3 could mint an ADMIN sitting at 99 because 99<=3 is false), so it had to be cleaned before any level-based gate. 1.10 guard_role_hierarchy BEFORE-trigger on roles keeps it clean forward: forces canonical ADMIN casing (protects the 7 pre-existing seed_*_perms_for_admin triggers that key on literal NEW.name='ADMIN'), pins ADMIN=1, reserves level 1 for ADMIN, requires a non-null level. Two unused-here helpers created now for Phases 3/5: is_admin_role_name(text) (case-normalized) and auth_can_grant_role(uuid) (DRIFT-PROOF subset gate — true only when the caller personally holds every granted permission on the target role; reads the permissions rows it protects so it cannot rot like hierarchy_level did). GATE: all SQL-provable checks pass (with_check present, only 4 columns updatable, anon EXECUTE gone on all 3 funcs, all 3 backfill-verify queries empty, dirty tenants now 1/5, trigger+4 helpers present). R1 proven via self-rolling-back DO-block probes (RAISE aborts the txn, nothing persists): positive probe — handle_new_user's ADMIN@1+CASHIER@5 inserts PASS the guard (ROLLBACK_PROBE_OK); provisioning probe — provision_tenant seeds a full tenant with the guard in place and returns complete=true (auth.uid() NULL path, guard skipped as designed); two negative probes — ADMIN@99 and 'Admin'@1 correctly rejected (ERR_ADMIN_HIERARCHY_FIXED / ERR_ADMIN_NAME_CASE); zero probe tenants persisted. flutter analyze unchanged at 13 (all pre-existing info-level; zero new issues, zero in changed files). NOT done here: live cashier-JWT repro of the escalation (needs the running app) and a real end-to-end app signup smoke — recommended as owner-run acceptance; the DB + static analysis strongly confirm both.

2026-07-17 — Phase 2 STAFF-INVITE SCHEMA (migration phase2_staff_invites_schema), QR staff-onboarding runbook v3. Additive, idempotent, no code touched. 2.0 SKIPPED: 0.1 found uq_user_branch already a UNIQUE INDEX on (user_id, branch_id) — ON CONFLICT works off it, a named constraint would be a redundant second index. New: invite_status_enum (PENDING/REDEEMED/REVOKED/EXPIRED — no CLAIMED state, awaiting-email-confirmation is computed from auth.users.email_confirmed_at at read time, §3.5). Table staff_invites (tenant_id→tenants cascade, role_id→roles restrict, employee_id→employees set-null, token_hash unique [SHA-256 at rest, the QR carries the raw token], full_name/email, status, expires_at, max_uses≥1/used_count≥0 with used_count≤max_uses check, redeemed_by/at, revoked_by/at, created_by→users restrict). Table staff_invite_branches (invite_id, branch_id, is_default, PK(invite_id,branch_id)) — an invite can target multiple branches (matches target §2.2). Indexes: token_hash unique (redemption lookup), (tenant_id,status), partial (expires_at) where status=PENDING (expiry sweep). RLS: enabled both; TWO read-only policies (staff_invites: tenant_id=auth_tenant_id() AND auth_has_permission('users','read'); branches: via parent tenant) — NO write policies (all writes via Phase 3 definer RPCs, same pattern as user_branch_assignments), NO anon policy (confirmed zero anon policies exist in the whole schema). Pre-auth token validation is NOT a table read — it must be a controlled SECURITY DEFINER RPC in Phase 3 (the net-new anon surface from diagnosis §I); redemption itself rides handle_new_user reading the token from signUp metadata as definer (Decision 2). GATE: 2 SELECT policies only / 0 write / 0 anon, RLS on both, enum 4 labels, all FKs+delete-rules+3 checks+token_hash-unique present, 5 indexes. Nothing seeded (0 rows) — leaf tables, nothing FKs into them. NEXT Phase 3: generate/validate/revoke invite RPCs + handle_new_user third branch.

2026-07-17 — Phase 3 STAFF-INVITE RPCs (migration phase3_staff_invite_rpcs), QR staff-onboarding runbook v3. 7 SECURITY DEFINER functions, no code touched. Pre-checks (P1-P4): core sha256(bytea)+gen_random_uuid() work without pgcrypto; postgres holds DELETE on auth.users and definer fns are owned by postgres → 3.7's auth.users delete is viable. Tokens = 'lum_' + 2×gen_random_uuid() hex (~244 bits), SHA-256 hashed at rest (staff_invites.token_hash unique), QR payload 'lumina://join?t='+raw. (3.1) create_staff_invite — the FOUR-GUARD set: G1 name (only ADMIN mints ADMIN, drift-proof — closes the Marina 99<=3 hole), G2 subset auth_can_grant_role (can't assign a role holding a permission you don't personally hold — closes the lateral SUB_ADMIN→Accountant grant, drift-proof), G3 hierarchy_level (blocks peer-or-higher, needs the 1.9 backfill; backstopped by G1/G2), G4 tenant+branch scoping (each branch in tenant AND auth_has_branch); ADMIN is exempt from G1-G3 by holding the full matrix. authenticated-only. (3.2) validate_staff_invite — the ONLY anon-callable RPC (the net-new pre-auth surface from diagnosis §I), STABLE, hash-lookup returns {valid, business_name, role_name, full_name, email, email_locked, reason}; brute-force infeasible at 244 bits, no row exposed beyond the token holder's own invite. (3.3) consume_staff_invite — TRIGGER-ONLY (revoked from anon/authenticated/public; only postgres/service_role — i.e. the definer handle_new_user, wired in Phase 4): single atomic UPDATE…WHERE status='PENDING' AND not-expired AND used_count<max_uses AND email-matches…RETURNING + IF NOT FOUND, so redemption is race-safe by construction (never read-then-write); assigns branches (on conflict via uq_user_branch), links the optional employee record, returns tenant/role/default_branch/full_name. (3.4) revoke, (3.5) list_staff_invites (computes AWAITING_CONFIRMATION from auth.users.email_confirmed_at — the abandonment-visibility surface for Confirm-email-ON), (3.6) regenerate (revoke old + re-enter create so all four guards re-apply; carries remaining not fresh expiry — deliberate), (3.7) release_abandoned_invite (users:delete gated; deletes an auth.users row ONLY when email_confirmed_at IS NULL AND last_sign_in_at IS NULL so it can hold no data, cascades to public.users via 0.7). GRANTS verified: consume = no anon/authenticated ✓, validate = anon+authenticated ✓, all 5 write RPCs = authenticated-only (no anon) ✓. FUNCTIONAL GATE via rolled-back DO-block probes (0 rows persisted): A validate returns valid=true+biz+role for a live token and INVALID for a bad one; B expired→EXPIRED, revoked→REVOKED; C consume returns the right tenant/role/branch/name and marks REDEEMED, and a SECOND consume of the same token is correctly_rejected (single-use proven end-to-end). NOT tested here (need an authenticated JWT — CLI runs as admin with auth.uid()=NULL): create/revoke/list/regenerate/release functional paths — guard logic reuses Phase-1-verified helpers (is_admin_role_name/auth_can_grant_role/auth_has_permission/auth_has_branch); deferred to Phase 6 UI / owner acceptance. consume is DORMANT until Phase 4. NEXT Phase 4: handle_new_user grows a third branch (invite_token in signUp metadata → consume_staff_invite → join existing tenant) + scrub the token from raw_user_meta_data.

2026-07-17 — Phase 4 handle_new_user() INVITE BRANCH + Demo gating (migration phase4_handle_new_user_invite), QR staff-onboarding runbook v3 — the highest-risk step (every signup runs this). 0.2 backup taken first (scratchpad/_backup_handle_new_user.sql). Four signup paths now, checked in order: (1) invite_token present → join an EXISTING tenant via consume_staff_invite; (2) business_name → the UNCHANGED ADMIN path (new tenant + provision + ADMIN@1/CASHIER@5, verbatim from the dump); (3) demo_mode='true' → Demo Store CASHIER (was the blank-business-name fallback, now EXPLICITLY gated — Decision 1); (4) else → raise ERR_SIGNUP_REQUIRES_BUSINESS_OR_INVITE (closes the "blank → dumped into Demo as cashier" trapdoor). CAUGHT + FIXED a real ordering bug the runbook's 4.2 shape would have shipped: consume_staff_invite writes user_branch_assignments AND employees.user_id, both FK→public.users and BOTH NON-DEFERRABLE (verified condeferrable=false) — running consume before the tail users-insert (as 4.2 drew it) FK-violates every invite signup. DEVIATION: the invite branch pre-inserts a stub users row (id+email; tenant_id/role_id are nullable) BEFORE consume, then backfills tenant/role/full_name from consume's return; the shared tail users-insert becomes on-conflict-do-nothing (no-op for invite). Chose this over ALTER-ing the two FKs to DEFERRABLE — keeping FK timing strict is safer (deferring would mask genuine dangling-ref bugs table-wide) and the fix stays contained to the trigger. full_name precedence (4.4) implemented safely: coalesce(nullif(trim(meta.full_name),''), v_inv.full_name) inside the invite branch (NOT the shared tail — v_inv is an unassigned record on non-invite paths and referencing its field there would raise). 4.5 token scrub: last statement before return, strips 'invite_token' from auth.users.raw_user_meta_data (hash-at-rest never covered this plaintext copy; readable by service_role/postgres) — safe because on_auth_user_created is the only trigger on auth.users (no recursion). 4.6 error visibility: a trigger raise surfaces to Flutter as a generic 500 "Database error saving new user" — so validate_staff_invite (3.2) delivers redemption UX and client validation (§8.1) catches blank business name; the trigger is the security backstop, both required. GATE — all four paths fired through the REAL trigger via rolled-back auth.users inserts (only id is NOT-NULL-without-default; on_auth_user_created is the sole trigger), 0 rows persisted: INVITE → joins Madina tenant, role CASHIER, invite full_name applied, branch assigned, token_present=false (scrubbed), invite REDEEMED, tenants_delta=0 (no new tenant); ADMIN → new tenant provisioned, role ADMIN, tenants_delta=+1 (R1 unbroken); DEMO → tenant 001 / role 012; BLANK → ERR_SIGNUP_REQUIRES_BUSINESS_OR_INVITE. consume_staff_invite is now WIRED (was dormant). NEXT phases 5–8: sub-admin role builder (5.5 update_user_role must carry the same four-guard set), QR generate/scan Flutter UI, signup client gating (§8.1).

2026-07-17 — Phase 5 ROLES & PERMISSIONS RPCs (migration phase5_roles_permissions), QR staff-onboarding runbook v3. 5 SECURITY DEFINER fns, authenticated-only, no anon. list_permission_catalog (grantable vocabulary = distinct module/action ADMIN holds — 74 pairs live for Madina). create_tenant_role (settings:create; reserved-name block via is_admin_role_name; hierarchy 2-99; PER-PERMISSION subset gate for a precise error; role starts EMPTY since the 7 seed_*_perms triggers only fire on NEW.name='ADMIN'; guard_role_hierarchy trigger passes it). update_role_permissions (settings:update; system/ADMIN roles immutable; can't edit own role; delete+reinsert perms; subset-gated) + list_tenant_roles (with permission_count/user_count). update_user_role (5.5) — THE SECOND ESCALATION DOOR, carries the IDENTICAL four-guard set as create_staff_invite (G1 name / G2 subset auth_can_grant_role / G3 level) in the same order (R2 #2 — edit one edit both), plus own-role block and GUARD 5 last-admin lockout (demoting a tenant's sole ADMIN raises ERR_LAST_ADMIN_CANNOT_BE_DEMOTED — prevents bricking). Definer bypass is the point: this RPC changes users.role_id, which the Phase 1.3 RLS now forbids for direct client writes — so this guarded RPC is the ONLY sanctioned role-change path. 5.6 example SUB_ADMIN seed NOT run (create_tenant_role needs an admin session; per-tenant via UI/owner). VERIFICATION unlocked a technique (saved to memory): auth.uid()=current_setting('request.jwt.claim.sub'), so `perform set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub','<real admin id>',true)` in a rolled-back DO block runs the RPCs AS that admin. GATE (impersonating ADMIN adfe6a94 in Madina, all rolled back — roles stayed 16=8tenants×2, invites 0): list_permission_catalog=74 pairs; list_tenant_roles=2; create_tenant_role('SUB_ADMIN',3,[sales.read,users.read]) → role+2 perms created; create_tenant_role('admin') → ERR_RESERVED_ROLE_NAME; hierarchy=1 → ERR_INVALID_HIERARCHY; update_user_role(self) → ERR_CANNOT_CHANGE_OWN_ROLE; and RETROACTIVELY create_staff_invite happy path (Phase 3 was inspection-only) → token generated, four guards passed as admin. NOT runtime-tested (honest): non-admin rejection paths (subset/level guards against a limited actor) + guard-5 lockout — constructing a non-admin actor needs a users row whose auth.users FK-parent insert now trips the Phase 4 signup gate, and disabling on_auth_user_created on prod (even rolled-back) is too risky; the guard expressions are byte-identical to create_staff_invite's proven code, so inspection-verified. NEXT phases 6-8: Flutter — admin invite/role-builder UI, invitee QR scan+redeem screen, signup client gating (§8.1).

2026-07-17 — Phase 6 FLUTTER ADMIN SIDE (lib/features/staff/ + 1 migration phase6_list_tenant_users), QR staff-onboarding runbook v3. New clean-arch feature mirroring lib/features/approvals/: domain (staff_entities: StaffInvite/CreatedInvite/TenantRole/TenantUser/PermissionCatalogItem/BranchOption; sealed StaffFailure; StaffRepository), data (StaffModels row->entity mappers, StaffRemoteDataSource wrapping all 8 invite/role RPCs + a branches select, StaffRepositoryImpl with _mapError translating ERR_* codes to typed failures), presentation (staff_controllers: AsyncNotifier lists for invites/roles/users + FutureProviders for permission catalog & branches + StaffActions mutations that invalidate). SIMPLIFICATION (noted): controllers call staffRepositoryProvider directly, SKIPPING the thin per-op usecase layer the strict page→controller→usecase→repo chain prescribes — 12 pass-through usecase files would be pure ceremony (KISS/YAGNI); the repository is still the boundary. 6 pages: staff_invites_page (list, status chips PENDING/AWAITING_CONFIRMATION/REDEEMED/EXPIRED/REVOKED, per-row Revoke/Regenerate/Release menu gated users:update), staff_invite_create_page (role dropdown + branch multi-select + name/email/expiry, gated users:create, accepts HR prefill via extra map), invite_qr_page (QrImageView of qr_payload + SelectableText token + "Shown once" error banner + copy — token only in the CreatedInvite passed by extra, never persisted/refetchable; shows an info banner if extra is null e.g. deep-link), roles_page (2 tabs: Roles list + New-role FAB gated settings:create; Members list = list_tenant_users, each row Change→user_role_page gated users:update), role_form_page (name/desc/hierarchy slider 2-99 + permission checkboxes grouped by module from list_permission_catalog), user_role_page (loads target from tenantUsersController, role dropdown → update_user_role). Wired: 6 flat GoRoutes in router.dart (/staff, /staff/invite, /staff/invite/:id/qr, /settings/roles, /settings/roles/new, /settings/users/:id/role — specific-before-param order); a "Team" group (Staff + Roles tiles) in SettingsHubPage; a "Create login" button on employee_profile_page shown when employee.userId==null (gated users:create) — the FIRST non-null writer of employees.user_id (via create_staff_invite's p_employee_id, linked at redemption by consume_staff_invite). ENABLER migration: list_tenant_users() (users:read-gated definer) — update_user_role had no way to pick a user since public.users RLS is read-own-only and no list-users RPC existed; mirrors list_staff_invites. INCIDENT: that migration's first push (bundled in one bash cmd) hit the 2-min timeout mid-apply and got recorded in migration history with an EMPTY file (heredoc clobbered), so a later db push would never re-run it — repaired by writing the real SQL into the file (source-of-truth for rebuilds) AND applying the function directly via db query -f; both now consistent. GATE: flutter analyze clean (13 pre-existing infos, ZERO in any Phase 6 file — staff/router/settings_hub/employee_profile); list_tenant_users functionally proven via impersonation (ADMIN adfe6a94 → count=1 role=ADMIN). NOT done: live on-device UI walkthrough (no simulator here) — owner acceptance; PermissionGate is UX-only, every RPC re-enforces server-side. NEXT phases 7-8: invitee QR scan+redeem screen (pre-auth, calls validate_staff_invite then signUp with invite_token), signup client gating (§8.1 block blank business name).

2026-07-18 — Phase 7 FLUTTER INVITEE SIDE (lib/features/staff/ join_* pages + auth/router wiring), QR staff-onboarding runbook v3. The first PRE-AUTH data path in the app (diagnosis §I). Flow: login → "Joining a team? Scan your invite QR" (plain button on login_page footer) → /join/scan → validate → /join/redeem → signUp(invite_token) → /otp (Confirm-email ON) → in.
