2026-07-20 — AUTH-LOGIN-THROTTLE fix (TC-AUTH-LOGIN-004, login_page.dart only, no schema/service change). Root cause of "cooldown stuck at Xs, never decrements": _submit's throttle pre-check (LoginThrottleService.canAttempt → locked) set a STATIC error string "Too many attempts. Try again in Xs" and early-returned WITHOUT calling _startCooldownCheck() or setting _cooldownSeconds — so on any locked-state entry (app restart while locked, prior timer cancelled, re-nav to LoginPage) the button stayed enabled (disabled = isLoading || _cooldownSeconds>0, still 0), label stayed "Log in", and the frozen "Xs" never ticked. The live 1s ticker only ever started from the recordFailure-throw path (AccountLockedFailure in _onStateChange), never from the pre-check path. Fix: guard now calls _startCooldownCheck(email) → seeds _cooldownSeconds via immediate _tick() + Timer.periodic(1s); decrements because canAttempt recomputes lockDt.difference(now); auto re-enables when lock deletes at remaining<=0. Edge case #2 fixed same change: _tick read the LIVE _emailController.text, so editing the email box mid-cooldown made canAttempt(otherEmail)→(true,0) and cancelled a still-active lockout; now the locked email is captured in _cooldownEmail at cooldown-start and _tick reads that. NOT changed (deliberate, not defects): lockout triggers at attempt >maxAttempts(5) not the catalog's loose "3+"; baseCooldown=30s not the catalog's illustrative 60s; no 15-min rolling attempt-window decay (counter is permanent until a successful login resets it — stricter, intended). OWED runtime: device re-run of TC-AUTH-LOGIN-004 (needs 6 real failed sign-ins to arm the lock, then observe live countdown + re-enable) — no widget test infra for flutter_secure_storage + timers in this repo yet.

2026-07-20 — AUTH-RECOVERY fix (TC-AUTH-OTP-002 + RESET-001; router.dart + reset_controller.dart, no schema change). Bug: forgot-password → enter recovery OTP → /reset NEVER shown, user drops straight into /dashboard. Root cause = a race between the recovery session and the auth-lifecycle handler: supabase.auth.verifyOTP(type: recovery) creates a real session (recovering user's uid, previously null while logged out); that fires onAuthStateChange; _GoRouterRefreshStream saw newUid != _lastUid and ran resetUserScopedState() → RecoveryState.instance.reset(), forcing stage back to none. OtpController sets stage=codeVerified right after its await, but the auth-state-change stream event lands as a SEPARATE event and clobbers codeVerified back to none; _redirect then sees recovery==none && loggedIn==true and falls through to /dashboard — the codeVerified→/reset gate is defeated. The lifecycle handler was treating the transient recovery sign-in as a cross-user login. Fix 1 (router.dart): the uid-change branch now skips BranchRouterState.reset()+resetUserScopedState() when RecoveryState.instance.isRecovering (the recovery sign-in is part of the flow, not a login; nothing user-scoped is loaded yet, so skipping is safe). Normal login/signup-OTP/logout all have isRecovering=false → reset runs unchanged. Fix 2 (reset_controller.setNewPassword success): signOut() the transient recovery session FIRST (stage still codeVerified keeps redirect pinned to /reset — no /dashboard flash), THEN RecoveryState.reset() → redirect resolves to /login (loggedIn=false, recovery=none, and /reset is not in authRoutes so the logged-out branch returns /login). This matches the catalog's documented "reset success → /login" and eliminates the "logs directly into the app" surprise; user must re-authenticate with the new password. Edge cases: Start-over button is gated on 'reset session expired' (dead session already) → existing reset+go('/forgot') still correct, untouched; OTP Back before verify has no session → fine. flutter analyze clean. OWED runtime: device re-run of full recovery (real email OTP) — no widget/integration test infra for Supabase auth sessions in this repo yet.

2026-07-20 — PURCHASING PO-form validation fix (purchase_order_form_page.dart only, no schema change). Symptom (screenshot): New PO with a line Qty 15 / Unit Cost 1000 showing "Line: PKR 15,000.00" was rejected by "Add at least one line with a product, quantity > 0 and cost ≥ 0." Root cause = discoverability + silent-drop, NOT a validation bug: the line had no product bound (productId null — the small blue "Select product" link was never tapped/couldn't be), but the computed line total made the line look complete; _save filtered `where(productId != null && qty>0 && cost>=0)` and, when that left zero valid lines, showed the generic error — and in the multi-line case it SILENTLY DROPPED any data-filled-but-productless line from the created PO. Fix: (1) validate every line individually with precise messages — product required ("Select a product for this line before saving." / "…for every line, or remove the empty one(s)."), then qty>0, then cost≥0 — so a filled line is never silently discarded; (2) the per-line product selector is now a full-width required FIELD (fieldFill container, inventory icon, chevron — proper tap target) that renders a destructive-red border + "Product required" helper when a save is attempted with productId still null (_saveAttempted flag). Picking a product clears the highlight. Edit path (products preloaded) unaffected. flutter analyze clean. OWED runtime: device re-run of the New-PO save/validation path.

2026-07-20 — AUTH PIN brute-force hardening (TC-AUTH-PIN-002, P0 SECURITY; pin_lock_screen.dart + main.dart, no schema change). Two bypasses that made the PIN lock ineffective. Bypass 1 (reported): on the 3rd wrong PIN the screen called PinService.clearPin() + PinLockState.instance.unlock() — a WRONG PIN cleared the lock and dropped the guesser straight into /dashboard on the victim's already-authenticated session (the catalog's own "Expected: unlock → /dashboard" was itself the vulnerability). Fix: _lockOut() now supabase.auth.signOut()s instead of unlocking — SIGNED_OUT → router resetUserScopedState() clears the lock and the !loggedIn redirect (which precedes the pin-lock check) sends the now-logged-out user to /login; full email+password re-auth required. signOut clears the local session even if the server revoke is unreachable; on the off chance it throws we keep the screen locked (reset attempts, show "Please sign in again") and NEVER fall through to access. PIN is intentionally NOT cleared — the failed guesses didn't compromise it and it reconciles from server on re-login; clearing was also pointless UX churn. Bypass 2 (related edge case, found while fixing): PinLockState.locked is in-memory and app.dart only arms it on AppLifecycleState.resumed, so a cold start (force-quit + relaunch) with a restored session skipped /pin-lock entirely and landed on /dashboard — force-quitting bypassed the PIN, which also made Bypass-1's fix moot (just restart instead of guessing). Fix: main.dart arms PinLockState.instance.lock() synchronously after Supabase.initialize (session already restored) and before runApp, guarded on `currentSession != null && await hasPin()` — first frame renders /pin-lock, no bypass window and no flash. Guarded on the LOCAL pin hash (same check the resume path uses) so a device that never set/reconciled a PIN is never stranded on an unverifiable lock, and a FRESH login (no session at launch) is not re-prompted for PIN right after entering the password. flutter analyze clean. OWED runtime: device re-run — 3-wrong-PIN → /login, and force-quit-relaunch → /pin-lock (both need a real device with a set PIN + biometric hardware).

2026-07-20 — UI REDESIGN to LUMINA design system (frontend-only, in progress). Adopted the Claude-Design "LUMINA" system for the app UI, starting with the Auth module. Source = a React/TS export + tokens.css (kept in scratchpad as VISUAL SPEC ONLY — React cannot be imported into Flutter; every screen is hand-rebuilt). Key decisions: (1) DARK MODE is now first-class — reverses CLAUDE.md's prior "light mode only / no darkTheme" convention (user chose light+dark with a toggle); every future feature must support both themes. (2) Architecture to add dark without breaking the 12+ features that share tokens: kept AppColors as light-only static consts (remapped to Lumina, new accent #2C6BFF) so nothing breaks, and ADDED a `LumColors` ThemeExtension (full light+dark) read via `context.lum` — reskinned widgets/pages read context.lum (dark-aware), legacy pages keep static AppColors (light) until reskinned; dark rolls out screen-by-screen. (3) Three brand fonts bundled locally (Clash Display + Satoshi from Fontshare, JetBrains Mono from JetBrains GH release) in assets/fonts/ — NOT google_fonts (Clash/Satoshi aren't on it). (4) Claymorphism (inset shadows) built with a CUSTOM PAINTER (clay.dart / ClayContainer) because Flutter BoxShadow has no inset support (and the prior flutter_inset_box_shadow burn — see 2026-06 dep lesson); paints outer drops → fill → inner shadows clipped to the RRect. (5) flutter_svg added for the logo glyph. (6) Shared-widget public APIs kept IDENTICAL during reskin so no feature outside auth needed edits. (7) Added CLAUDE.md guardrail: frontend/UI tasks touch ONLY presentation/ + core/design/, never backend/db/routing/auth/data. Done so far: L1 foundation (tokens/theme/clay/6 widgets) + L2 primitives (glyph/wordmark/checkbox/AuthHeroScaffold) + L2 Login pixel-close, all flutter-analyze clean + macOS run exception-free. Remaining: 16 auth screens' bespoke L2 rebuild. VERIFY OWED: on-device visual eyeball (agent env can't screenshot — macOS screen-recording perm denied; Flutter headless-web CanvasKit snapshots blank).

2026-07-20 — macOS fixes surfaced during UI redesign QA. (1) "No internet" on the environment-check screen despite working network: macOS entitlements (macos/Runner/DebugProfile.entitlements + Release.entitlements) had com.apple.security.network.server but NOT com.apple.security.network.client, so the App Sandbox blocked ALL outbound connections — the env check's `supabase.from('tenants').select()` failed → "No internet" (Session still showed "Ready" because currentSession reads the cached token locally, no network). Added com.apple.security.network.client=true to both; macOS builds can now reach Supabase (pre-existing gap, unrelated to the redesign). (2) AuthHeroScaffold card hugged the left on mid-size desktop windows: rewrote the layout to explicitly Center the card in both the single-column and two-column (_FormSide) paths, and raised the hero breakpoint 900→1024 so windows between 900–1024 use the centered single column instead of a cramped ink-hero split. Entitlement changes require a full rebuild (no hot reload). flutter analyze clean.

The reskinned /splash was never rendering: _redirect's env-check gate (`if !EnvCheckState.passed → /env-check`) fired on the very first eval of initialLocation '/splash', rewriting it before a frame drew. Added SplashState (ChangeNotifier singleton, app_flow_state.dart) + a gate at the TOP of _redirect: `if !SplashState.shown && recovery==none → hold on /splash`. splash_page starts a 1600ms Timer in initState that sets SplashState.shown=true → redirect (SplashState is in refreshListenable) advances to the env-check gate. shown persists for the app lifetime (one splash per launch; resets on cold start/hot restart). A live recovery deep-link skips the splash (recovery!=none). flutter analyze clean. Also this session: reworked AuthHeroScaffold ink hero glow (was one bright centered spotlight) to layered subtle auroras — blue top-right (.28) + warm bottom-left (.14) over #10131C + faint prism rays, matching the design's radial-gradient + Prism treatment.

Sign out" escape on the post-login gates (branch-select/workspace-init/mfa-challenge) which were app-kill-only dead-ends (supabase.auth.signOut() → redirect to /login); removed the dead "Use PIN instead" on cold login (/pin-lock isn't in the unauth allow-set); MFA "use another method" now signs out instead of looping through workspace-init→MFA; branch-select Retry + neutral empty + per-tile select spinner; mfa-challenge verify spinner + start-failure Retry; workspace-init wrapped in AuthHeroScaffold to keep the desktop hero continuous mid-flow + dropped a debugPrint leaking the session UID; guarded the /otp `state.extra` cast (deep-link safe); mfa-challenge route switched to the _authFadePage cross-fade; raw `error.toString()` fallbacks replaced with a generic user-safe message. 2026-07-20 -- FIX: invoice-detail duplicate-page-key crash (Navigator '!keyReservation.contains(key)'). Repro: dashboard -> Today's Sales KPI -> /dashboard/drilldown (a root page above the shell) -> tap invoice row -> context.push('/sales/invoice/:num'). That route lived INSIDE the StatefulShellRoute sales branch; go_router 17 cannot push a StatefulShellRoute descendant from a page sitting above the shell without re-emitting the shell page with its fixed key -> duplicate-key assertion (red screen). Root cause = shell-descendant leaf reached via cross-shell push, not the drilldown itself. Fix: moved GoRoute '/sales/invoice/:invoiceId' out of the sales StatefulShellBranch to a TOP-LEVEL route, so all 4 entry points (sales history, dashboard recent-sale tile, drilldown, repair-close) push a plain root page that pops back to origin -- no shell duplication. Idiomatic go_router: leaf detail pages reachable from multiple branches belong at root, not in a branch. Trade-off: invoice detail no longer shows the bottom-nav bar (it is a pushed detail page now) -- fine. analyze clean; macOS debug build clean. SEPARATE pre-existing issue seen in same log: flutter_secure_storage write throws -34018 'A required entitlement isn't present' on macOS (device/branch/pin persistence) -- keychain entitlement gap, NOT related to this crash; flagged, not fixed.

2026-07-20 -- FIX: flutter_secure_storage -34018 on macOS (blocked PIN setup, spinner stuck). Root: the macOS Runner is ad-hoc signed (CODE_SIGN_IDENTITY = '-', no development team). flutter_secure_storage v10 defaults to the data-protection keychain (kSecUseDataProtectionKeychain), which needs a keychain-access-groups entitlement; ad-hoc signing REJECTS that entitlement (adding it failed the build: 'entitlements that require signing with a development certificate'). So every Keychain write threw -34018, and PinSetup._save awaited setPin() with no try/catch -> unhandled throw left _loading true forever -> infinite 'Confirm your PIN' spinner. Fix: set MacOsOptions(usesDataProtectionKeychain:false) on ALL FlutterSecureStorage instances -> legacy file keychain, which sandboxed apps reach via the implicit app group, no entitlement/cert needed. Five scattered sites (pin_service, device_service, login_throttle_service, app_flow_state, branch_controller) -- initial patch missed the latter two (searched only core/services); a signed-in run proved the 3 patched sites stopped erroring while branch_controller still threw, then patched it too. Entitlements left unchanged. Also hardened PinSetup._save with try/catch: a storage failure now shows a retry banner instead of hanging. NOTE drift risk: 5 identical consts -- keep in sync (or centralize) when adding a 6th. analyze + macOS build clean.

2026-07-21 -- UI REDESIGN: dashboard feature + navigation (frontend-only). Second Claude-Design export, SAME design-system UUID as the auth zip (lumina-pos-design-system-5cd3f8f0...), so tokens/theme/clay were already implemented and were NOT touched -- this was purely a consumer-side reskin. Process followed the new docs/UI_REDESIGN_PLAYBOOK.md end to end. Decisions: (1) NAV IS NOW RESPONSIVE -- >=900px renders a 244px left rail (wordmark, 44px clay-soft active items, Settings split below a hairline, profile user block), <900px keeps a bottom bar but with the design's 46x30 accentSoft pill behind the active icon, replacing M3 NavigationBar (whose indicator can't express that). bottom_nav_shell.dart lives in core/widgets -- OUTSIDE the CLAUDE.md UI-only allowlist -- so this was taken as an explicit user-approved exception; file path, class name and constructor kept identical so router.dart needed zero edits, and branchMap / goBranch / purchase:read+sales:read gating were lifted verbatim. Shared chrome, so all 5 tabs get the new nav; no tab's page CONTENT changed. (2) DECORATIVE SEARCH OMITTED -- the export's header carries a non-functional 'Search... /K' field; shipping a dead control repeats the 'Remember me' mistake corrected on login in the auth UX pass, and no global search exists to wire. (3) lucide_icons_flutter ^3.1.15 ADOPTED (user chose fidelity over the zero-dep Material mapping); all 31 needed symbols were grepped out of the package and confirmed to exist BEFORE any widget depended on them; package has zero transitive deps. Icon language is now mixed (dashboard+nav Lucide, ~40 other screens Material) -- accepted, migrate opportunistically. (4) CHART SPLIT -- sales trend HAND-ROLLED (7 Expanded columns + per-bar ScaleTransition): the design needs a gloss gradient on the latest bar only, a 55ms staggered scaleY entrance and value labels above bars, all cheaper as plain widgets than as fl_chart config; the payment donut KEEPS fl_chart PieChart (centerSpaceRadius + a Stack-centered label is exactly its strength and the existing code already worked). Both honour MediaQuery.disableAnimations. (5) THE DELTA PROBLEM -- the design shows per-KPI trend deltas ('12.4% vs yesterday'), but DashboardSummary carries NO comparison fields; those numbers are mock. Rather than fabricate them or add backend fields (which would break the UI-only boundary), only today_sales gets a real delta, derived in the presentation layer from the salesTrend series already in the payload (last vs previous point, suppressed when <2 points or yesterday==0); the other 9 KPIs render honest static sub-labels ('on hand', 'cleared balance', 'below reorder point', 'at cost'). The design's semantic colour rule (down-on-receivables is GREEN, up-on-low-stock is RED) is kept in the tile API for when real comparison data lands. (6) EDIT MODE MOVED INLINE -- the separate _EditRow list is gone; each tile grows an eye-toggle + up/down chevron footer calling the SAME kpiLayoutProvider.toggle/move, and hidden tiles stay visible at 0.42 opacity so they can be toggled back. (7) dashboard_page.dart split 772 lines -> ~260-line composition root + 10 files under presentation/widgets/; dashboardEditingProvider deliberately LEFT declared in dashboard_page.dart because two widgets import it from there. Fixed en route (both real pre-existing bugs, not cosmetic): the KPI grid's hardcoded 2-up width math (MediaQuery.width - padding)/2 inside a Wrap rendered TWO GIANT TILES on any desktop window -- now a real GridView at 5 cols wide / 2 narrow; and the dashboard + nav were still on light-only static AppColors so they were broken in dark mode -- both now read context.lum. New shared widgets: core/design/widgets/app_pill.dart (AppPill + AppPillTone, the design's dot+label status pill) and app_money_text.dart (muted 0.62em currency prefix + tabular mono number); format.dart gained formatAmount()+displayCurrency and formatPkr now delegates to them (byte-identical output) rather than duplicating the grouping loop. NotificationBell and SyncStatusWidget restyled in place with logic untouched -- note SyncStatusWidget now also renders in the dashboard header (user-accepted), so its drain-outbox-on-reconnect listener can fire from the dashboard, not just POS. GATE: flutter analyze = 13 pre-existing infos / 0 new; macOS debug builds and boots clean (profile loaded, WORKSPACE-STATE completed=true, zero render/overflow exceptions); flutter test shows the SAME 2 failures as clean HEAD (Supabase-not-initialized in widget_test + kpi_layout_persistence_test) -- proven by running the suite in a detached HEAD worktree, so NOT caused by this work. VERIFY OWED: on-device visual eyeball -- the agent env still cannot screenshot (screencapture returns 'could not create image from display'); unverified visually are the 900px rail/bottom-bar boundary, the 5/2-col grid, dark mode, edit-mode persistence across a restart, all 6 drilldown kinds, and the permission-gated nav variants. SEPARATE pre-existing bug seen in the same run log, untouched: LoginThrottleService.reset still throws -34018 from FlutterSecureStorage.delete on macOS despite the MacOsOptions(usesDataProtectionKeychain:false) fix -- delete apparently does not honour mOptions; needs its own slice.

2026-07-21 -- DASHBOARD UI PARITY FIXES + GLOBAL SEARCH. Follows the dashboard/nav reskin entry above; triggered by the user diffing the running app against the reference renders. FOUR REAL BUGS, all mine: (1) AVATARS RENDERED AS SHADOW ONLY -- ClayVariant.lumen paints no fill of its own (the doc comment on ClayContainer.color says as much: "Null keeps the painter transparent (caller supplies its own gradient/fill)"), and I passed no colour to the welcome-card and nav-rail avatars, so they came out as pale halos instead of solid Lumen discs. AppButton was the existing correct precedent all along (color: lum.accent on the lumen variant). Lesson: when a variant is documented as caller-fills, grep an existing consumer before using it. (2) KPI TILES HAD DEAD SPACE -- I had hand-guessed childAspectRatio constants (1.32/1.45), which set tile HEIGHT independently of content and left ~45px of blank card under every value. Replaced with a LayoutBuilder that computes cellWidth from real constraints and divides by a documented content-height constant (120, or 173 in edit mode), so tiles are exactly as tall as what they contain at any width or column count. Lesson: never hardcode an aspect ratio to hit a height -- derive it. (3) THE DONUT WAS MISSING ENTIRELY, the user's loudest complaint. Two compounding causes: I dropped each chart card when its data was empty (`paymentBreakdown.isEmpty ? null : ...`), AND dashboard_summary's payment_breakdown is scoped to TODAY only (`p.created_at >= v_from and <= v_to`, migration 20260714105051 line 76) -- so a tenant with no sales today loses the card completely, and because _Pair collapses to its non-null side, the sales-trend card silently expanded to full width, hiding that anything was gone. Both charts now ALWAYS render with an in-card empty state ("No payments recorded yet" / "No sales in the last 7 days"). This was a UI bug, not a data bug: the RPC is behaving as designed and the donut appears as soon as a sale is recorded today. Lesson: a conditionally-omitted section in a two-up row reads as a layout bug, not as absent data -- prefer an empty state. (4) Sales-trend value labels floated at the top of the chart area rather than sitting on each bar; the bar column is now measured with LayoutBuilder so the label sits directly above its bar. Also ADDED `gradient` to ClayContainer (additive param; _ClayPainter now prefers a shader over the flat fill, shouldRepaint updated) so KPI tiles and the welcome card carry the design's linear-gradient(180deg, surface, g50) wash -- previously flat. GLOBAL SEARCH (reverses the earlier "omit the decorative search field" decision -- the user chose to wire it for real rather than ship it dead or leave the gap): new lib/features/search/, scope = products + customers + invoices, presented as an overlay panel under the header field (command-palette feel) rather than a /search page. Built as a PRESENTATION-LEVEL AGGREGATOR (FutureProvider.autoDispose.family) over the modules' existing use cases -- searchProductsUseCaseProvider, loadCustomersUseCaseProvider, loadInvoicesUseCaseProvider -- so there is NO new datasource, NO new repository and NO SQL migration. Deliberately calls the USE CASES, never the feature controllers: customersProvider/productsProvider are non-autoDispose singletons whose _query state is shared with the Customers page and POS picker, so driving them from search would corrupt those screens. Only backend-shaped change: an optional `search` param threaded through loadInvoices datasource->repo->usecase applying .ilike('invoice_number', ...) -- purely additive, every existing caller unchanged, no index needed at POS volumes. SECURITY NOTE: read RLS on products/customers/invoices is tenant-scoped ONLY (no auth_has_permission check), so each section is gated CLIENT-SIDE with canProvider(('inventory','read')) / ('customers','read') / ('sales','read') and never queried when denied -- without that gate the server would happily return rows the role shouldn't see. CMD+K / Ctrl+K is the app's FIRST keyboard shortcut (no Shortcuts/Actions precedent existed anywhere): CallbackShortcuts in BottomNavShell targeting a GlobalKey<GlobalSearchFieldState>, no-op when the dashboard isn't mounted; Esc dismisses. 300ms debounce matching the five existing hand-rolled copies (still no shared debounce helper -- a candidate for extraction if a sixth appears), 2-char minimum, 5 hits per section, field shown on wide layouts only (the design has no mobile search). Platform hint label uses defaultTargetPlatform, NOT dart:io Platform, so web builds don't break. GATE: analyze 13 pre-existing infos / 0 new; macOS builds and boots clean with zero render/overflow/unhandled exceptions. VERIFY OWED: on-device eyeball of all of the above incl. the search overlay -- agent env still cannot screenshot.

2026-07-21 -- UI REDESIGN: sales module (frontend-only). Third Claude-Design export ("LUMINA Sales", 8 screens x desktop/mobile x light/dark), SAME design-system UUID as the auth and dashboard zips, so tokens/theme/clay/fonts were already implemented and were NOT touched -- consumer-side reskin only, per docs/UI_REDESIGN_PLAYBOOK.md. All 11 sales screens were still on light-only static AppColors (zero context.lum references), so the module was broken in Counter mode; it now has zero static-colour references. Decisions: (1) NAV IS MODULE-AWARE -- inside /sales/* bottom_nav_shell.dart swaps its destination list for the design's sales rail (Point of sale / Sales history / Returns / Session) and puts the other modules below a hairline; narrow layouts render the same 4 tabs plus a leading Modules tab that opens a sheet. The export's rail contains ONLY the 4 sales items because the export only ever renders sales -- shipping that literally would trap the user inside the module, so the escape hatch is an addition, flagged to and accepted by the user. bottom_nav_shell.dart is in core/widgets, outside the CLAUDE.md UI-only allowlist, so this is the same user-approved exception taken for the dashboard reskin; file path, class name and constructor are unchanged, so router.dart needed zero edits, and branchMap / goBranch / permission gating / the CMD+K binding were lifted verbatim. Sales destinations navigate with context.go because they all live inside shell branch 3; the Session item targets /sales/open, which already renders the already-open state itself, so the shell needs no session provider. (2) POS CATALOGUE IS A GRID WITH CATEGORY CHIPS -- the design replaces the search-result list with a card grid plus a category filter row. Chips are fed by the EXISTING inventory categoriesProvider (a presentation-level join, no new query) and are HIDDEN OFFLINE because CachedProduct carries no category at all; showing an empty or lying filter row offline was the alternative and was rejected. A missing StockLevel now reads as "Stock not cached" rather than "Out of stock", because offline the stock cache is empty and the old code would have dimmed the entire catalogue. Tiles keep the design's gradient placeholder + cpu glyph: the export itself is image-light and the POS payload has no product image, so no Image.network and no new dependency. Grid childAspectRatio is derived in a LayoutBuilder from the measured cell width against a documented content-height constant (kpi_grid.dart pattern) -- never hand-picked. (3) CLOSE SESSION SHOWS ONLY REAL FIELDS -- the render breaks the drawer down as Opening float / Cash sales / Card sales / Digital wallets, but close_cashier_session returns only expected_float, closing_float, cash_variance, total_sales, total_transactions, and the design's three numbers are hardcoded constants in the export's own JS. Rather than fabricate a split or add a datasource query (which would break the UI-only boundary), the card shows opening float / total sales / transactions before closing and expected-in-drawer / counted cash / variance after. There is also no live pre-close variance strip, because expected-in-drawer is computed server-side and cannot be derived client-side from total_sales (which includes card). (4) PAYMENT STAYS A ROUTE -- the design layers payment as a bottom sheet over the POS; /sales/payment is a real go_router page and converting it would be a routing change, so the page is restyled to READ as the sheet (amount-due hero figure, TENDER rows, sticky Complete sale) with the route and its extra contract byte-identical. The design's "Exact" chip was adopted -- it fills a tender with the outstanding balance, which is real behaviour, not decoration. OTHER HONEST DEVIATIONS: history rows drop the design's "n items" (the invoices list query has no item join); invoice items render description + price x qty with no SKU line (invoice_items has neither sku nor product name); the receipt slip shows the real branch name but not a terminal id (none exists); the return screen KEEPS its reason chips even though the design has no reason control, because create_sales_return requires one; the sync chip, notification bell and header avatar are wired to the real SyncStatusWidget / NotificationBell / profile initials instead of the export's hardcoded "3 queued" and "FA". Two bugs visible in the export's own renders were deliberately NOT reproduced: the rail keeps a hardcoded white glass fill in dark mode, and its desktop rail items compute an active state they never paint. New shared widgets in core/design/widgets: app_qty_stepper.dart, app_filter_chips.dart (AppFilterChips + AppFilterChip), app_section_card.dart, app_money_field.dart -- all additive, no existing widget's public API changed. New sales widgets: sales_scaffold.dart (SalesScaffold + SalesHeader + SalesAvatar, replacing 11 hand-rolled AppBars), sales_rise.dart (the lum-rise entrance, static under reduce-motion), sales_empty_state.dart. pos_terminal_page.dart went from 932 lines to a composition root plus presentation/widgets/pos/. No new dependencies. GATE: flutter analyze = 10 issues, 0 new against the 13-issue baseline (the 3 pre-existing POS infos died with the rewrite); macOS debug builds clean; flutter test shows the same 2 failures (widget_test smoke + kpi_layout_persistence) as clean HEAD, re-proven this session in a detached HEAD worktree. VERIFY OWED: on-device visual eyeball -- the 900px rail/bottom-bar boundary, grid column counts, dark mode across all 11 screens, the offline pass (chips hidden, cash-only payment path), the mobile cart sheet, and a screenshot diff against the reference renders.

2026-07-21 -- SALES ROUTE TRANSITIONS CROSS-FADE (follows the sales reskin entry above; raised by the user seeing the same "sliding screen" problem the auth hero screens had). Every sales screen renders the SAME persistent chrome -- the rail (>=900px) plus the header -- so go_router's default slide made that static chrome appear to travel with the pane that was actually changing. All 8 in-shell sales routes plus the top-level /sales/invoice/:invoiceId now use pageBuilder with the EXISTING cross-fade helper rather than a second implementation; that helper was renamed _authFadePage -> _fadePage (private, 12 call sites in router.dart, no behaviour change) because it now serves auth and sales both. The invoice route keeps state.pageKey, which is exactly the key the default page would have used, so the go_router 17 key-reservation assertion documented above it is unaffected. This is a ROUTER edit, outside the CLAUDE.md UI-only boundary -- taken because the user asked for it directly; only transition wrappers changed, every path, builder body, extra contract and redirect is untouched. NOTE the design's own lum-rise entrance (opacity + 14px translate) is still applied per-screen via SalesRise on the card screens (open/close session, success, receipt, return-done) because it is what the export specifies; it is a settle inside the pane, not a page slide, and can be made fade-only by dropping the Transform.translate in sales_rise.dart if that reads as movement too.

2026-07-21 -- macOS FILE PICKER FIX (root cause: missing sandbox entitlement, NOT a Dart bug). Symptom: tapping Choose CSV on either import screen did nothing on the macOS build. Investigated through the plugin source rather than guessing: file_picker 12.0.0-beta.7's MacOSFilePickerHandler.handleFileSelection calls checkEntitlement(requiredMode:.readOrWrite) and RETURNS A FlutterError BEFORE it ever constructs NSOpenPanel, so the dialog never appears; the check reads com.apple.security.files.user-selected.read-only / .read-write via SecTaskCopyValueForEntitlement. Both macos/Runner/*.entitlements had App Sandbox enabled and declared NEITHER key -- the 2026-07-16 file_picker 3.0.4 -> 12.0.0-beta.7 bump updated GeneratedPluginRegistrant and Podfile.lock but not the entitlements (the pre-12 plugin had no such check, so the gap was invisible until now). The plugin's OWN example app declares user-selected.read-write in both files, which is the reference that confirmed it. FIX: added com.apple.security.files.user-selected.read-write to DebugProfile.entitlements and Release.entitlements. Chose read-write over read-only even though the CSV flows only read, because the printing / share_plus receipt paths write to a user-selected destination and would hit the same wall (.requireWrite) later. SECOND DEFECT, why it was invisible: neither call site caught the resulting PlatformException -- FilePicker.pickFiles() was awaited bare, so a hard platform rejection surfaced as a silent no-op. Both now catch PlatformException and surface it (import_products_page -> _error banner, migration_import_controller -> state.failure), so any future entitlement/permission refusal is legible instead of dead. VERIFY: rebuilt and read the entitlements back OUT of the signed binary -- `codesign -d --entitlements :-` on the built .app shows com.apple.security.files.user-selected.read-write present, which is the exact key the plugin's runtime check reads. analyze 10 issues / 0 new. NOT verified: the actual pick-a-CSV-and-import tap-through (headless env cannot drive the panel) -- owed on device.

2026-07-21 -- UI REDESIGN: repair module (M09), fourth Claude-Design export ("Repair feature screen map", 5 screens x desktop/mobile x light/dark). SAME design-system UUID (5cd3f8f0-66ab-4668-b735-c252b51735e1) as the auth, dashboard and sales zips, so lib/core/design tokens/theme/clay/fonts were already correct and were NOT re-derived -- consumer-side reskin per docs/UI_REDESIGN_PLAYBOOK.md. Repair was the last module still on light-only static AppColors (zero context.lum references), so it was broken in Counter mode; it now has none. Decisions: (1) REPAIR MOVED INTO THE NAV SHELL -- the export renders repair as a first-class module with the rail/bottom nav, but the five repair routes were top-level GoRoutes outside StatefulShellRoute, so repair had no nav chrome at all. The user was asked and chose the move: a new StatefulShellBranch APPENDED as index 5 (after Settings) so branch indices 0-4 -- and the _kSalesBranch = 3 const -- never shift, gated in branchMap on repair:read. All five routes switched from builder to pageBuilder + the existing _fadePage helper, the same cross-fade sales got, because every repair screen now carries identical chrome; /repair/:repairId stays DECLARED LAST so the three literal sub-paths keep winning. The three inbound deep links (inventory hub tile, approvals entity link, notification tap) switched push -> go so they land inside the branch instead of stacking a shell route outside it; the approvals one branches on the /repair prefix because purchasing and HR are still outside the shell and must keep pushing. Intra-module drill-downs (intake, job detail) deliberately KEEP push so back returns to the board. This is a router + core/widgets edit, outside the CLAUDE.md UI-only boundary, taken only because the user explicitly chose it. (2) SALES CHROME PROMOTED, NOT COPIED -- repair needed the same header, so sales_scaffold.dart moved to core/widgets/module_scaffold.dart as ModuleScaffold/ModuleHeader/ModuleAvatar and sales_scaffold.dart became three typedefs plus kSalesWideBreakpoint, so all 9 sales pages compile untouched (playbook section 6 backward-compat pattern). bottom_nav_shell.dart's sales-only _SalesDest/_SalesRail/salesIndex generalised to _ModuleDest/_ModuleRail/(dests, destIndex, moduleLabel); repair's rail destinations are Repair jobs / Workload / History, and the export's own rail has no way back out of the module, so the Modules escape hatch built for sales carries over. (3) NO BOARD/LIST TOGGLE -- the export ships an explicit Board/List segmented control; the user asked instead for the screens to be responsive like the earlier reskins, so the layout stays width-driven (board >=900px, status-grouped list below) with no manual switch. Board column width is DERIVED in a LayoutBuilder -- columns share the available width when the seven fit and fall back to a 268px minimum with horizontal scroll -- never hand-picked. (4) BOARD SEARCH IS REAL -- the mock's "Search jobs..." header field is wired client-side over the already-loaded list (job number / device / brand / model / customer / IMEI), the same idiom repair_history_page already used; no datasource, query or controller change. (5) HONEST DATA, deviations from the mock and why: the export's six columns include an awaiting_parts state that does NOT exist in repair_status_enum, and omits our qc / cancelled -- the board keeps the real 9-state machine and the existing repairBoardStatuses; the cost summary reads Parts / Estimate / Final instead of the mock's Parts / Labour / Total because there is no labour field anywhere in repair_jobs (deriving it from final minus parts would silently mislabel margin); part rows show product name + qty x unit cost, no SKU (repair_parts joins only products(name)); the status-history timeline resolves changed_by (a user UUID) against the technicians already loaded and shows the timestamp ALONE when the id is unknown, never an invented author; the customer picker drops the mock's "12 jobs" note (no such count); the technician workload sub-line does NOT carry the mock's "n high/urgent" -- repairJobsProvider is a FILTERED list (this very page sets a technician filter), so deriving the count there would under-report whenever a filter is active; the real delivered / avg-turnaround figures the mock lacks are kept instead. Product rows show a stock line only when Product.qtyOnHand is actually non-null. (6) ADDITIVE TOKEN/PRIMITIVE GAPS ONLY -- LumColors gained transit/transitSoft/transitText (light values already existed as AppColors statics; dark from the export's colors.css --transit: #A07CFF) for the warranty pills, AppPillTone gained transit, and AppButton gained AppButtonSize (md default 50px, new sm 38px) for the compact toolbar/card-header buttons. No existing field, enum value or default changed, so no call site anywhere moved. repair_detail_page.dart (1274 lines, then 1796 after the reskin) was split with part/part of into repair_detail_page.dart + repair_detail_cards.dart + repair_detail_dialogs.dart to stay near the 800-line ceiling while keeping the private widgets private. Every raw AlertDialog in the module moved onto one showRepairSheet helper (centred modal >=900px, bottom sheet below). No new dependencies. GATE: flutter analyze = 8 issues, 0 new (baseline 10 -- two pre-existing infos died with the rewritten files); macOS debug builds clean; flutter test = the same 2 failures as clean HEAD (widget_test smoke + kpi_layout_persistence). VERIFY OWED: on-device eyeball -- the 900px rail/bottom-bar boundary, board column counts and drag-to-status, dark mode on all five screens, the bulk-select flow, close & invoice / warranty paths, and a screenshot diff against the reference renders.

2026-07-21 -- DARK MODE DEEP AUDIT across auth/dashboard/sales/repair (4 parallel read-only audits, user-requested "deep diagnosis"). Dashboard (13 files) and sales (24 files) came back fully clean -- zero AppColors/hardcoded-color regressions, confirming the "zero static-colour references" claims in PROJECT_STATE.md. Two real bugs found and fixed: (1) LumColors.dark.ink was identical to dark.paper (both 0xFF0B0E16) -- surfaced independently by two audits: LuminaWordmark (lumina_brand.dart) defaults its text colour to lum.ink with no override, so "LUMINA" rendered black-on-black on every mobile/stacked auth screen (login, signup, forgot/reset, OTP, MFA, branch-select, workspace-init) in Counter mode; separately the repair kanban bulk bar (repair_kanban_page.dart) fills its floating pill with lum.ink, so it had zero tonal separation from the dark scaffold behind it, visible only via its drop shadow. Fixed by giving dark.ink the existing `surface` value (0xFF161B28) -- the same one-step-lighter-than-paper delta every other "raised" dark-mode surface already uses -- rather than inventing a new hex. (2) signup_page.dart's _PasswordStrengthHint was the one isolated AppColors holdout in the whole auth module (destructive/warning/success/fieldFill) -- switched to context.lum, matching the rest of the theme-aware signup card. Auth's splash screen and MFA-enroll QR panel's hardcoded literals were investigated and CONFIRMED intentional (both explicitly commented in code as "always dark ink hero" / "QR needs a fixed white quiet-zone"), not bugs -- left untouched. GATE: analyze 8 issues, 0 new.

SAME design-system UUID (5cd3f8f0-66ab-4668-b735-c252b51735e1) as the auth, dashboard, sales and repair zips, so lib/core/design tokens/theme/clay/fonts were already correct and were NOT re-derived -- consumer-side reskin per docs/UI_REDESIGN_PLAYBOOK.md. Settings was the last module still on light-only static AppColors (31 refs, zero context.lum), so it was broken in Counter mode; it now has none. The export's Settings.dc.html is a device/rail/bottom-nav harness that duplicates chrome we already ship in bottom_nav_shell.dart and was ignored; SettingsBody.dc.html plus the Toggle and Dropdown component specs are the actual spec. Decisions: (1) SUB-SCREENS MOVED INSIDE THE NAV SHELL -- the design draws every settings detail screen with the rail still present and an in-page back button, but the six /settings/* routes were top-level GoRoutes outside StatefulShellRoute, so they rendered full-screen with their own AppBar and no nav. The user was asked and chose the move: they are now siblings of /settings inside the EXISTING settings StatefulShellBranch, switched from builder to pageBuilder + the shared _fadePage (same cross-fade sales and repair got, because every settings screen now carries identical chrome). No branch was added or reordered, so no index -- including _kSalesBranch = 3 -- moved; paths, page classes and the hub's seven context.push call sites are byte-identical. This is a router.dart edit, outside the CLAUDE.md UI-only boundary, taken only because the user explicitly chose it. (2) THE AUTH PROFILE PAGE IS IN SCOPE -- the design's "Profile & security" screen is lib/features/auth/.../settings_page.dart, not a settings-module file; the user chose to include it rather than have the hub's first tile open a differently-styled page. Presentation only: every provider, PinService/LocalAuthentication call, permission key and push target is unchanged. (3) FOUR NEW SHARED PRIMITIVES, all in lib/core/design/widgets/ and all theme-aware, reduce-motion aware and >=44dp: app_toggle.dart (the 46x28 inset pill switch -- Material's Switch cannot express an inset track, so it is hand-rolled rather than themed), app_dropdown.dart (the 50px clay-inset well, built on the stdlib MenuAnchor so outside-tap dismissal, focus traversal and keyboard handling come from the framework instead of a hand-rolled overlay), app_settings_row.dart (AppSettingsRow + AppSettingsGroup -- the icon-tile row and the label-over-hairline-separated-card grouping, shared by the hub, the profile screen and the payment cards), and app_detail_scaffold.dart (back button + eyebrow + display title + description, 860px centred). Adopted in SETTINGS ONLY by the user's choice -- the ~15 raw Switch/SwitchListTile sites in inventory/accounting/hr/notifications/approvals/reporting are left for their own reskins, so the icon and control language stays mixed for now. (4) THE SHEET HELPER WAS PROMOTED, NOT COPIED -- settings needed the modal-or-bottom-sheet behaviour repair already had, so repair_sheet.dart moved to core/design/widgets/app_sheet.dart as showAppSheet/AppSheetHeader and repair_sheet.dart became a delegating function plus a typedef, leaving all five repair call sites compiling untouched (playbook section 6 backward-compat pattern). Likewise AppTextField gained maxLines (the receipt-footer textarea) and helperText (the logo-URL note) as purely additive params with unchanged defaults, so none of the 12+ features that use it moved. (5) HONEST DATA, deviations from the mock and why: the profile screen's security rows show NO meta counts -- the mock's "3 devices" and "2 active" have no source on that page and deriving them would mean loading two more controllers to decorate a row, so they are omitted rather than invented; the payment GL line reads "Posts to Cash (1000)" from resolvedAccountCode alone because no account NAME exists anywhere in the payload (the mock's "1010 - Cash on hand" is invented); payment-method icons are mapped from the method's code with a generic wallet fallback rather than guessing a payment rail; the branch "Default" badge is the real BranchConfig.isMain; the hub footer carries the REAL PackageInfo.version (new dep package_info_plus, chosen by the user over a static string) instead of the mock's v3.2.0; and Preferences keeps Theme as a single "Light" option because that is genuinely all UiPreferences offers -- dark mode lives on the profile screen's Counter-mode toggle, which is exactly where the design puts it too. (6) BRANCH DROPDOWNS WITH A CAPABILITY GUARD -- the user chose the export's fixed 3-option country/currency/timezone dropdowns over the previous free-text fields. To stop that silently truncating real data, AppDropdown renders a value the caller's option list does not cover as its own synthetic option, so a branch stored on e.g. Asia/Dhaka still displays Asia/Dhaka instead of reading as unselected. Both branch sheets moved onto showAppSheet and gained the design's Cancel/Save pair with save disabled while the name is empty. (7) PERMISSION FILTERING MOVED OFF PermissionGate ON THE PROFILE SCREEN -- inside AppSettingsGroup a gated-out row still occupies a slot, so its hairline would render as a stray double rule; the four admin rows are therefore filtered against permissionMatrixProvider before the group is built, and the whole group collapses when none survive. The permission keys (settings:update, settings:read, accounting:read) are identical to before, as is the hub's PermissionGate(settings, read) around the Workspace group. GATE: flutter analyze = 8 issues, 0 new (baseline 8); macOS debug builds and boots clean; flutter test = the same 2 failures as clean HEAD (widget_test smoke + kpi_layout_persistence), re-proven this session in a detached HEAD worktree, so NOT caused by this work. 2026-07-22 -- SUPPLIERS MODULE UI RESKIN (sixth design export, consumer-side only). The export's design-system id is 5cd3f8f0-66ab-4668-b735-c252b51735e1, identical to the auth/dashboard/sales/repair/settings zips, so lib/core/design tokens were NOT touched -- this was a pure consumer reskin per docs/UI_REDESIGN_PLAYBOOK.md. Suppliers was the last module still on light-only static AppColors (zero context.lum reads), so the whole feature was broken in Counter mode; it now has none. Decisions: (1) SUPPLIERS MOVED INSIDE THE PURCHASING SHELL BRANCH (user-approved router edit, same precedent as repair and settings) -- the four /suppliers* routes left the top-level list and became siblings of /purchasing in the existing StatefulShellBranch (index 2), on pageBuilder + the shared _fadePage. Paths, page classes and constructor args are byte-identical and no branch index moved. bottom_nav_shell.dart gained _purchasingDests, wired into the switch that already served sales and repair. (2) THE MODULE RAIL CARRIES ONLY IN-BRANCH DESTINATIONS -- Purchasing (/purchasing) and Suppliers (/suppliers). Purchase orders / invoices / returns / reorder are still ROOT routes; putting them on the rail would `go` outside the shell and the rail would vanish mid-module. Moving them in is deliberately deferred to its own slice. (3) THE TWO INBOUND LINKS SWITCHED push -> go (purchase hub, inventory hub) because a cross-branch push would strand the screen outside its own shell; intra-module drill-downs (list -> detail, list -> form) keep push so back returns to the list, and the list itself therefore has NO back button -- it is a rail destination now, like the hub. (4) TWO PROMOTIONS TO THE DESIGN SYSTEM -- repair_states.dart became core/design/widgets/app_states.dart (AppEmptyState / AppErrorState); the repair file is now two typedefs so its call sites are untouched, and AppErrorState gained additive `icon` and `retryLabel` params defaulting to repair's old wifiOff / "Try again". New app_search_field.dart is the design's 48px inset search well with a clear button (the POS keeps its own copy because it also hosts the scan button). (5) HONEST DATA -- the opening balance arrives from supplier_ledger() as a SCALAR, not an entry, so its synthesised ledger row shows an em-dash where the mock puts a date rather than inventing one, and it renders only when the value is non-zero; "Settled" appears only once payablesAgingProvider has actually resolved, because claiming a settled account off a failed aging call would be a lie; the amount suffix follows supplier.currency via formatAmount instead of the mock's hardcoded PKR; and the ledger card is NEVER conditionally dropped -- an empty ledger renders an in-card "No transactions yet." (6) A SECOND "DETAILS" CARD WAS ADDED beyond the mock's four-field identity grid (tax number, currency, bank, account, address, tags, notes). The form writes all of them and the mock's detail screen shows none, which would have left them write-only; every value is a real stored field and empty ones read as an em-dash. (7) THE MOCK'S DECORATIVE CHROME WAS DROPPED -- its fake Cmd-K topbar search, static "Synced" chip, bell and avatar are replaced by ModuleHeader's real SyncStatusWidget / NotificationBell / ModuleAvatar; its dead rail and bottom-nav items and its unsortable ledger column headers carry no tap target. Record payment keeps the REAL push to /purchasing/payments/create rather than the mock's toast-only stub. The mock's single role==="owner" boolean is ignored in favour of the three existing PermissionGate keys (purchase:create/update/delete). (8) The delete flow moved off a raw AlertDialog onto showAppConfirm and off SnackBar onto showAppToast; the remove() call and the pop are unchanged. GATE: flutter analyze = 8 issues, 0 new (baseline 8); flutter test = the same 2 pre-existing failures as clean HEAD (widget_test smoke + kpi_layout_persistence). VERIFY OWED: on-device eyeball -- the agent env cannot screenshot, so unverified visually are the 900px rail/bottom-bar boundary, all four list states, dark mode on all three screens, the ledger card with and without entries, and a screenshot diff against the reference renders.

2026-07-22 -- BANK-OPENING bug fix. Bank account create was plain client CRUD: opening_balance was written to its column but current_balance (defaults 0) was never seeded and no journal was posted, so the list card (shows current_balance) read Rs 0 on every new account, every running balance stayed off by the opening amount, and the balance sheet never saw the opening cash. Fixed by mirroring create_expense/create_voucher -- a gated security-definer create_bank_account RPC that inserts with current_balance=opening_balance and, when opening<>0, posts via post_journal Dr(bank's linked chart account)/Cr(Owner Equity 3000), sign-flipped for a negative/overdraft opening. Opening journal posts with a NULL branch (create flow carries no branch; next_number + journal_entries.branch_id both allow null) so NO UI change was needed. The "bank gated insert" RLS policy was dropped so the GL invariant can't be bypassed by a raw client insert; gated update stays (edits don't touch the GL). Datasource insertBankAccount now calls the RPC then re-selects the row, keeping repo/controller/usecase/entity/UI untouched. NOT fixed (out of scope): editing opening_balance after creation still updates only the column, not current_balance or the GL -- opening balance shouldn't really be editable post-create. GATE: flutter analyze clean on the changed datasource; migration not yet pushed.

(2) CHARTS KEEP fl_chart, RESTYLED -- NOT hand-rolled (user choice). New module widgets wrap fl_chart theme-aware: ReportingBarChart (rounded rods, single-accent fill, value labels rendered via always-on tooltips [showingTooltipIndicators + transparent getTooltipColor] so each label tracks its own rod rather than being pinned to the container, hairline baseline, no gridbox, no y-axis) and ReportingLineChart (2.5px strokes, 10% belowBar area fill, optional dashed projection series + beamSoft VerticalRangeAnnotation future region + dashed VerticalLine split). No new dependency (fl_chart 1.2.0 was already the module's charting standard). (3) HONEST DATA -- every mock value maps to a real entity field with nothing fabricated: inventory KPIs = sum(totalValue)/sum(retailValue)/count(belowReorder)/row count (retailValue is a real InventoryValuationRow field); products table+Top-8 bars = unitsSold/revenue/profit/invoiceCount; aging KPIs+buckets+top-balances = totalBalance/b90plus/the five bucket fields/maxDaysOverdue with an Overdue pill past 60 days; trends KPIs = sum(totalRevenue)/sum(totalProfit); forecast = a 7-day trailing moving average over the last 60 days (honest info banner "a guide, not an ML forecast", flat projection, projected-next-7 = avg x 7); insights = recommendationType/confidenceScore/reasoning. Only recommendation types the backend actually emits render (each mapped to a pill tone + Lucide badge via reporting_ui.insightMeta) -- the mock's 4-type spread (REORDER/PRICE/SLOW_MOVER/CASHFLOW) is NOT fabricated. The hub's "Financial . opens in Accounting" rows KEEP real navigation to /accounting/reports/{trial-balance,profit-loss,balance-sheet,cash-bank-book} (the mock only toasts) with an arrow-up-right chevron as the leaves-the-module cue. (4) STAT CARDS/GRID -- new ReportingStatCard (clay-raised tile, surface->g100 gradient wash, tinted icon, display-face value or AppMoneyText) laid out by ReportingStatGrid whose column count is DERIVED from available width against a min-tile-width, never a hand-picked childAspectRatio (tiles size to their own content height). (5) PAGES ONTO DS WIDGETS -- hub sections became AppCard row groups; the scheduled-reports create/edit form moved from a bespoke showModalBottomSheet+raw DropdownButton+Material Switch onto showAppSheet + AppDropdown + AppToggle + AppTextField; metric/range selectors moved from raw ChoiceChip to AppFilterChips; insight actions use AppPill+AppButton; empty/error states use AppEmptyState/AppErrorState; SnackBars moved to showAppToast. The existing ReportExportButton was restyled to the design's clay "Export" button + PDF/CSV popup menu with its public constructor (title/headers/rowsBuilder) UNCHANGED so its 4 page call sites + the aging view keep compiling. The hardcoded aging bucket Color(0xFFFF6B30) was retired to lum.beam. No core/design edits, no new deps, no domain/data/controller/model edits. (6) SIDE EFFECT -- pull-to-refresh is dropped on the report lists (ModuleScaffold/AppDetailScaffold have no RefreshIndicator slot); the autoDispose FutureProviders refetch on revisit, and a manual refresh is not in the design. New module widgets under lib/features/reporting/presentation/widgets/: reporting_ui.dart (abbreviate, insight badge/tone, confidence colour, aging pill), reporting_stat_card.dart, reporting_bar_chart.dart, reporting_line_chart.dart. 2026-07-23 -- REPORTING NAV PROMOTION + BACK-BUTTON FIX (follow-up to the reporting reskin, user-requested). The initial reskin kept reporting push-based (router untouched); the user then asked for it in the nav bar and reported "most reporting screens have no back button". Root cause: (a) the 9 /reports* routes lived OUTSIDE the StatefulShellRoute, so entering /reports left the app with no rail/bottom-bar and no way back to the dashboard (macOS has no back gesture); (b) three screens -- the hub, smart_insights and scheduled_reports -- used ModuleScaffold, which renders NO back button, so once pushed onto them the user was stranded (the other 6 sub-pages already used AppDetailScaffold, which has a clay back). Fix: (1) PROMOTED REPORTING TO A NAV-SHELL BRANCH (index 7, appended after customers=6 so no earlier index shifts) -- the 9 routes moved into a new StatefulShellBranch on pageBuilder + the shared _fadePage; bottom_nav_shell.dart gained _allItems[7] = Reports (LucideIcons.barChart3), _kReportsBranch=7, and `if (hasReports) 7` in branchMap gated reports:read, slotted just before Settings (which stays last, below the rail divider). Reporting has NO in-branch sub-destinations (its sub-reports are drill-downs, not rail siblings), so it deliberately gets the normal module rail like Dashboard/Inventory -- no _reportsDests, no switch case, no module sub-rail; the Reports item stays lit on every /reports* location. (2) THE HUB STAYS ModuleScaffold as the branch root (the design's hub route has no back; the rail/bottom-bar is the exit), exactly like the Customers list / Dashboard / Inventory hub. (3) smart_insights AND scheduled_reports MOVED FROM ModuleScaffold TO AppDetailScaffold (eyebrow 'Reports' + title + description + clay back to the hub), matching the design's ReportBody header which shows a back chevron on every sub-route (showBack = route !== 'hub') and consistent with the other 6 sub-pages; their bodies changed from a self-scrolling ListView to a plain Column since AppDetailScaffold already provides the scroll + centered max-width, and the scheduled "New" action moved into AppDetailScaffold.actions. (4) INBOUND LINKS push->go: dashboard quick_launch's Reports tile (c.push('/reports') -> c.go('/reports')) and the dashboard stock-value KPI, whose deep route changed from '/reports/inventory' to '/reports' and is navigated via go -- kpi_grid now routes any '/reports*' target with context.go and everything else (e.g. '/accounting/reports/*', still top-level) with context.push. The KPI points at the hub rather than the inventory sub-report on purpose: a go'd branch sub-page has no route beneath it in its own Navigator, so an AppDetailScaffold back there would be dead; landing on the hub (Inventory is its first row) keeps back sane. The hub's own rows keep context.push -- intra-branch pushes (/reports/*) pop back to the hub, and the "Financial . opens in Accounting" rows push the still-top-level /accounting/reports/* pages above the shell. No permission keys, no data/controller/domain changes; the /purchasing/orders/create seed contract is unchanged. GATE: flutter analyze = 8 issues, 0 new (baseline 8); macOS debug build succeeds. VERIFY OWED (agent env can't foreground/screenshot): the runtime nav -- Reports appears in the rail (>=900px) and bottom bar (<900px) for a reports:read user and is lit across all 9 screens; tapping a sub-report and using its back returns to the hub; the dashboard quick-launch tile and stock-value KPI land on the hub with the branch switched; and no go_router key-reservation assertion fires when entering /reports from the dashboard (this pattern mirrors the proven customers branch promotion).
2026-07-23 -- BANK-OPENING follow-up: null-branch next_number bug (create_bank_account crashed with "null value in column entry_number of journal_entries"). The 2026-07-22 fix posts the BANK_OPENING journal with a NULL branch on the assumption next_number tolerated it -- it did not. For any series with include_branch_code=true (the auto-created JOURNAL_ENTRY row defaults to true), next_number ran `select coalesce(code,'') into v_code from branches where id = p_branch_id`; with p_branch_id NULL that matched zero rows, and PL/pgSQL sets the INTO target to NULL on no-row SELECT (not the initial ''), so `prefix || v_code || lpad(...)` collapsed to NULL and entry_number (not null) rejected the row. Vouchers/expenses were unaffected only because they pass a real branch_id; bank-opening was the first null-branch caller. Root-cause fix in the shared next_number (migration 20260723110000): guard the branch-code lookup with `and p_branch_id is not null` so v_code stays '' -- covers every present and future null-branch post, not just banks. create_bank_account unchanged. GATE: migration pushed to prod (supabase db push OK); grants preserved by create-or-replace.

2026-07-23 -- BANK RECONCILIATION NOW CORRECTS THE BOOKS (P0-MONEY fix, TC-ACC-BANK-004; user-approved treatment). Pre-existing bug (RPC from 2026-07-12, NOT the reskin): complete_bank_reconciliation only stamped status/reconciled_balance/reconciled_by/at and posted NO journal, so a statement-vs-book difference was recorded but never fixed -- the control was cosmetic (Complete succeeded, ledger unchanged, no adjustment entry). Chosen treatment (of three offered): the CALLER names the adjustment account (no blind plug to a guessed literal; the seeded 15-account CoA has no bank-charges/suspense account, and standard practice books the real cause -- bank charges/interest/error -- to its own account). Migration 20260723130000_bank_reconciliation_adjustment (pushed): complete_bank_reconciliation gains p_adjustment_account_code; on completion it recomputes the live delta = reconciled_balance - the bank chart account's current_balance, and when non-zero posts a balanced RECON_ADJUSTMENT journal via post_journal (delta>0 -> Dr bank / Cr contra; delta<0 -> Cr bank / Dr contra) that moves the bank chart account exactly onto the reconciled balance; the journal id is stored on bank_reconciliations.adjustment_journal_id (new nullable column). post_journal called p_gate=false because this RPC already enforces accounting:approve (the established auto-post pattern); the journal_entries period-open trigger still blocks a closed period, and idempotency comes free from post_journal's unique (tenant, reference_type, reference_id) index keyed on the recon id (Complete also blocks re-completion via ERR_ALREADY_COMPLETED). Not via resolve_payment_account -- this is not a payment; the bank side is the reconciliation's own bank chart account (deterministic), the contra is caller-supplied. Raises ERR_ADJUSTMENT_ACCOUNT_REQUIRED when delta<>0 and no code, ERR_BANK_HAS_NO_CHART_ACCOUNT when the bank has no linked chart account. Client chain threaded additively (datasource/repo interface+impl/usecase/controller add adjustmentAccountCode); the controller now also invalidates chartOfAccountsProvider + journalEntriesProvider after completion so the ledger balance + journal list reflect the adjustment immediately. UI (bank_reconciliation_page): when the reconciliation shows a difference, an "Adjustment account" picker (shared new acct_account_picker.dart, extracted from the manual-voucher line picker -- both now use it) is required before Complete; the difference posts to it. Worked example (the test): books 10000, reconciled/statement 9980 -> delta -20 -> Cr 1010 Bank 20 / Dr <chosen> 20 -> 1010 ledger + accounts.current_balance = 9980, a RECON_ADJUSTMENT journal appears in /accounting/journal. GATE: flutter analyze 8 issues 0 new; macOS debug build succeeds; migration pushed clean. VERIFY OWED: user re-runs TC-ACC-BANK-004 in-app (adjustment entry appears, 1010 ledger moves to 9980, bank shows reconciled). Note: bank_accounts.current_balance is a separate denormalised figure the journal does NOT touch (the BankCard balance vs the chart-account ledger can still diverge) -- pre-existing dual-balance design, out of scope here.

NAV PROMOTION (user-approved router edit): the 12 top-level /purchasing/* GoRoutes (orders, orders/create, orders/:id[/edit|/receive|/invoice], invoices-list, payments/create, reorder, returns, returns/create, returns/:id) moved from above the shell INTO the existing purchasing StatefulShellBranch (index 2 -- already held hub + the 4 suppliers routes; NO branch index shifts) on pageBuilder + the shared _fadePage, preserving every extra/path-param contract byte-identical (record-typed extra for orders/create, Map for payments/create + returns/create with the poId `as String` non-null cast). CRITICAL EXCEPTION: /purchasing/invoices/:id stays a TOP-LEVEL route (pageBuilder+_fadePage, exactly like /sales/invoice/:invoiceId) because the top-level /dashboard/drilldown page pushes it from ABOVE the shell, and go_router cannot push a StatefulShellRoute descendant from a page above the shell without tripping the `!keyReservation.contains(key)` assertion -- so this one leaf pushes cleanly from anywhere (loses the rail on that screen only, accepted). All other inbound pushes originate inside the shell (reporting/suppliers/approvals branches + intra-purchasing) so they render with the rail after the move. bottom_nav_shell.dart (_purchasingDests, _kPurchaseBranch=2, _purchasingIndexFor, purchase:read gating) byte-identical; no inbound push/go call site changed. CHROME: hub = ModuleScaffold (branch root, no back) + a LayoutBuilder width-derived clay tile grid (6 tiles, tone per spec, icon chip in tinted clay square; NEVER a hand-picked GridView aspect ratio -- a Wrap of intrinsic-height tiles). The 13 other screens = AppDetailScaffold (clay eyebrow/title/back). Lists (orders/invoices/returns) = AppFilterChips + AppPill status + AppMoneyText + app_states empty/error, cards inline per page. Detail (po/invoice/return) = AppSectionCard groups + two-column LayoutBuilder (>=760) with a right totals/actions column; a card is NEVER conditionally dropped from a shared row (empty-state text rendered instead). Forms (po-form/grn-receive/invoice-match/payment/return-form) = AppSectionCard + AppTextField/AppDropdown/AppMoneyField, product + supplier pickers via showAppSheet (retiring raw showModalBottomSheet), showAppToast (retiring ScaffoldMessenger), reorder Checkbox->AppCheckbox; AppDetailScaffold owns the scroll so pinned bottom bars became inline submit buttons. PO cancel AlertDialog -> a reason-carrying showAppSheet body (showAppConfirm has no text field). FILTER COUNTS: chips carry NO count badge -- status filtering is server-side (the notifier refetches per status), so there is no unfiltered set to count client-side; a real number was not fabricated. HONEST DATA -- every displayed value maps to a real field: supplier name(.city) via suppliersProvider, product names via productsProvider/getProductUseCase, PO/invoice/return totals+status+dates from entities, invoice-match variance = REAL InvoiceCreateResult.matchVariance (NOT the mock's fixed +4200), return-detail IMEI pills = REAL purchase_return_items.imei_ids_json (the mock generated them), reorder stock/reorder-point/cost from Product, supplier-picker payable from payablesAgingProvider.bySupplier. OMISSIONS (no backing field, stated not invented): po-list "· N lines" (loadPurchaseOrders returns PO headers only, no items -- avoided an N+1), po-detail GRN "N units" (loadGrns returns the GRN header only). Invoice rows/titles use the vendor supplierInvoiceNumber because the schema has NO internal invoice number. Reorder "+N to reorder" is a transparent derived figure (reorderPoint - onHand), not a fabricated restock policy. Payment method options + the cash-only GL construction (record_supplier_payment with p_bank_account_id: null) left byte-identical -- no fabricated GL-backed methods. NEW MODULE WIDGETS (presentation/widgets/, context.lum, no core/design edits, no new deps -- lucide_icons_flutter already present): purchasing_ui.dart (poStatusPill/invoiceStatusPill/returnStatusPill -> (AppPillTone,label) mirroring the design's PO_ST/INV_ST/RET_ST tables + ymd(DateTime)); purchasing_imei_capture.dart (one shared IMEI/serial capture block -- header+counter+removable mono list+add field+add button+optional Scan -- consumed by BOTH grn-receive and return-form, which each hand-rolled it before; caller passes `met` since GRN allows captured>=target and returns require ==target). Per-page list/detail cards kept inline (single-consumer, YAGNI). The three duplicated status-helper functions (poStatusLabels/poStatusColor/fmtPoDate on purchase_orders_page and returnStatusColor/returnStatusLabels on purchase_returns_page) were kept transiently during the phased build then DELETED once po-detail/invoice-detail/return-detail migrated to purchasing_ui. The 5 form pages were reskinned by 5 parallel subagents against a shared spec; each self-verified analyze-clean and preserved all provider/controller/usecase/RPC-param/validation/navigation/PermissionGate(purchase:read/create/update/approve/delete) bindings byte-identical. NO backend/router-redirect/controller/usecase/repository/datasource/domain/supabase edits (the router change is additive route relocation only). GATE: flutter analyze = 8 issues, 0 new (baseline 8, all pre-existing: auth null-aware x2, import_products file_picker deprecations x3, migration controller file_picker deprecations x3); macOS debug build succeeds (exit 0). VERIFY OWED (agent env can't foreground/screenshot): all 14 screens light + dark, the rail/bottom-bar persists on every purchasing screen EXCEPT invoice-detail, the /dashboard/drilldown -> invoice-detail push still resolves (keyReservation exception), hub tiles + working Suppliers tile, order lifecycle (submit/approve/receive/create-invoice/return/cancel-with-reason), GRN receive + IMEI capture, invoice 3-way match + variance result, record payment, returns form + detail (IMEI pills), reorder select -> create PO, and a screenshot diff vs the 13 reference renders.

'update' : 'create'), resolveTaxRateUseCaseProvider default, barcode scan, the ProductType/Status string maps, and every variant/image/tier data-map key + save call; its 3 AlertDialog editors became showAppSheet _EditSheet bodies.
