2026-07-24 -- CUSTOMER RLS GATE MADE REAL (migration 20260724070303_drop_ungated_customers_policy). AUDIT FINDING correcting the 2026-07-11 claim that the CASHIER-can't-edit gate was "real at the DB": it was NOT. The original customers_tenant policy from 20260618114258_sales_foundation.sql has NO `for` clause -> it applies to ALL commands (select/insert/update/delete) with tenant isolation but ZERO permission check. The 20260710134809 mutation-permissions migration ADDED four gated policies (insert->customers:create, update->customers:update, delete->customers:delete, plus tenant SELECT) but never dropped customers_tenant. Postgres OR's permissive policies per command, so the ungated policy still permitted UPDATE/DELETE for any authenticated tenant user regardless of permission -- a CASHIER could edit/delete customers directly via PostgREST. Forward migration drops customers_tenant; the four gated policies become the sole authority. Post-drop: SELECT via "customers tenant read" (tenant-scoped, unchanged); INSERT still requires customers:create (CASHIER HAS it -> POS quick-add + sales createCustomer path unaffected); UPDATE requires customers:update, DELETE requires customers:delete (CASHIER has neither -> now correctly blocked). NOTE: softDeleteCustomer is an UPDATE (sets deleted_at) so it hits the gated UPDATE policy (needs customers:update) while the detail-page delete button gates UI on customers:delete -- a role with delete-but-not-update would pass the UI and fail at the DB; harmless for ADMIN (all) / CASHIER (neither), pre-existing 20260710134809 policy split, not introduced here. Lesson: a later additive RLS migration that "adds a gate" must DROP the pre-existing ungated policy for the same command, or the OR makes the gate dead -- same class of bug as the bank_accounts dead-policy (checks GRANT before RLS) but the inverse (RLS OR too permissive).

2026-07-24 -- CUSTOMER LEDGER `outstanding` KEY RESTORED (migration 20260724070304_customer_ledger_outstanding_key). BUG: customer_ledger() (20260711141631) built its return jsonb with customer_id/opening_balance/current_balance/entries but NEVER an `outstanding` key, while CustomerLedgerModel.fromJson, the credit-summary UI, the Collect-payment CTA gate (customer_detail_page.dart:155 -> `ledger.outstanding > 0`) and the POS remaining-credit chip (customerOutstandingProvider -> pos_cart_panel) all read it. The model's _num() defaulted the missing key to 0, so outstanding was ALWAYS 0: the Collect-payment CTA on customer detail NEVER appeared and the POS credit chip always showed 0 -- silent degradation, no error. Forward migration create-or-replaces the function (body byte-identical to 20260711141631 except the added key). `outstanding` = guard-basis receivable = greatest(sum(invoices.balance) for non-VOID invoices of the customer, 0) -- invoices.balance = grand_total - paid_amount maintained by the sales RPCs; clamped >=0 so overpayment never shows negative outstanding (current_balance still carries the true signed net). No Dart change needed -- entity/model already expected the key. Lesson: an RPC and its consuming model share an implicit jsonb contract; a defaulting fromJson (_num->0) hides a missing key as a plausible zero instead of failing loud.

2026-07-24 -- Duplicate migration version 20260723140000 resolved (recon_adjustment renamed to 20260724070302). Two local files shared version 20260723140000: fix_trial_balance_asof (applied to remote under that version) and recon_adjustment_not_bank_account (the P0-MONEY guard rejecting a bank-reconciliation contra equal to the bank's own chart account -- a no-op adjustment that reported COMPLETED while the ledger never moved). recon_adjustment was stranded below remote HEAD and `db push --include-all` failed on schema_migrations_pkey (version 20260723140000 already existed). Fix: `git mv` recon_adjustment to 20260724070302 (fresh unique version, chronologically before the customer migrations) -> plain `db push` applied all three (070302 recon guard, 070303 drop ungated customers policy, 070304 customer_ledger outstanding). Safe because complete_bank_reconciliation is defined only at 20260723130000 and re-touched by nothing after, so re-applying the guarded create-or-replace now yields the same final state. Lesson: never reuse a migration version prefix across two files -- schema_migrations PK is the version alone, so the second file can never be recorded; the CLI silently applied whichever sorted/ran first and stranded the other.

2026-07-24 -- WhatsApp PDF receipt after every sale (6 migrations 20260724130000-130500 + new receipt-sender edge fn + notification-sender edit). NOT YET PUSHED (needs Twilio provisioning + Vault secret first). Architecture: SERVER-SIDE PDF so online, walk-in-with-phone, and offline-synced sales all send uniformly (offline sales replay through create_sale server-side with no app in the loop -> a client-side PDF approach would miss them). Flow: create_sale enqueues one communication_logs row (channel WHATSAPP, template_code SALE_RECEIPT, invoice_id, recipient=customer.phone) in a best-effort swallowed sub-block before the final return -- fires once per invoice (idempotent replay guard early-returns above it); walk-in/no-phone -> SELECT yields nothing -> no row. A pg_cron (*/2 min via pg_net + Vault-stored service key) hits the new receipt-sender edge fn, which per PENDING SALE_RECEIPT row: calls receipt_render_data(invoice_id) (SECURITY DEFINER, tenant resolved from the invoice, revoked from authenticated), renders a branded A4 PDF (pdf-lib, pure-JS, no browser), uploads to a new PRIVATE receipts bucket (<tenant>/<invoice>.pdf), mints a 300s signed URL, and sends via Twilio as a WhatsApp document (ContentSid approved template in prod; free-form Body+MediaUrl only works in the Twilio sandbox). attempts column added (retry budget 3 before FAILED). KEY TRAPS HANDLED: (1) communication_logs.channel CHECK was ('SMS','EMAIL') -- widened to include WHATSAPP or the enqueue silently no-ops; (2) notification-sender already drains communication_logs with NO template filter -> once its cron is wired it would send SALE_RECEIPT rows as JSON.stringify garbage text -- added .neq('template_code','SALE_RECEIPT'); (3) NOTHING ever drained the comms queues (delivery was deferred to "M11") -> first cron would blast months of stale PENDING rows -> a quarantine migration flips all existing PENDING (communication_logs/report_deliveries/notifications) to FAILED BEFORE the cron migration, plus a 30-min created_at freshness window in both senders; (4) the create_sale enqueue must swallow unique_violation too, else it bubbles to the function's outer `when unique_violation` handler and gets misread as an idempotency race, returning a wrong result for a valid new sale. This also REVIVES repair-status SMS/email + scheduled report deliveries (dead since built -- nothing triggered the sender). GO-LIVE DEPS (external): provision a Twilio WhatsApp Business sender, submit + get approval for a UTILITY SALE_RECEIPT document-header template (ContentSid), set secrets TWILIO_SID/AUTH/WHATSAPP_FROM/SALE_RECEIPT_CONTENT_SID, and create the Vault secret service_role_key out-of-band (never in a migration -- would leak into git). LIMITATION: pdf-lib does no Arabic/Urdu shaping -- Latin fields only in v1; Urdu header needs a pre-rendered image (future). No Flutter changes -- the on-device ReceiptPage/ReceiptPdfService stay as-is. Lesson banked (again): a queue with no drainer is a silent bug; and widening a CHECK to enable a new channel must be paired with excluding that channel's rows from any pre-existing generic drainer.

2026-07-24 -- Barcode label templates now seeded at tenant provisioning (migration 20260724140000). Root cause: provision_tenant() seeded sms/email templates but NEVER barcode_templates, so a fresh tenant had zero label templates and a non-technical user hitting "New template" had to hand-author raw Layout JSON -- unusable. Fix (two parts): (1) added section 8 to provision_tenant() seeding 3 label templates -- 'Standard Barcode (CODE128)' 50x25 (the default), 'Retail EAN-13' 50x30, 'QR Label' 40x40 -- with layout_json {showName,showBarcode,showPrice}=true matching what LabelPdfService reads; plus a set-based backfill for all existing tenants. barcode_templates has NO unique constraint so idempotency is name-guarded (not on conflict); is_default is set on CODE128 only and only when the tenant has no default yet (not-exists guard prevents a second default). EAN13 needs a 13-digit code -- LabelPdfService already falls back to CODE128 rendering otherwise, no crash. (2) Frontend: replaced the "Layout (JSON)" textarea in barcode_template_form_page.dart with three plain toggles (Product name / Barcode-QR / Price) + reused the same toggle for Default -- extracted a _toggleRow helper; removed dart:convert. So a layman never sees JSON: three ready templates exist out of the box, and creating a new one is toggles only. QR generation + QR scanning were already fully built (label_pdf_service qrCode(), mobile_scanner reads QR) -- only the seed + the JSON-field UX were missing. analyze 8 issues/0 new. Migration NOT yet pushed.

2026-07-24 -- Scan-to-jump on the products page (presentation-only, products_page.dart _scanForSearch). Scanning a product's barcode/QR now looks it up and, on a SINGLE exact match (barcode==code OR sku==code), navigates straight to /inventory/stock/:id (same target as tapping the card); 0 or >1 exact matches fall back to the prior behaviour (fill the search box + run the filtered search), and a truly empty result shows a warning toast. Reuses the existing searchProductsUseCaseProvider (its datasource already does an exact barcode.ilike + substring sku/name), so exact matching is filtered client-side to exclude fuzzy name hits -- zero backend/datasource/domain changes, no new deps. Every product is scannable even with no barcode set because LabelPdfService encodes barcode ?? sku, and the lookup matches SKU too. QR generation + scanning were already built; this closes the last gap (scanned code previously only filled the search field).

2026-07-24 -- Fixed '!keyReservation.contains(key)' crash (duplicate shell page key) on the inventory stock-detail page. Root cause: ProductStockDetailPage is a TOP-LEVEL route (/inventory/stock/:productId, kept above the shell because it is pushed from drilldown/notifications/search/sync), but its action buttons pushed SHELL-descendant routes -- onPrint->/inventory/labels, onAdjust->/inventory/adjustments/create, onOpening->/inventory/stock/movement. go_router cannot push a StatefulShellRoute descendant from a page above the shell without re-injecting (duplicating) the shell page key -> the assertion, thrown building the root MaterialApp navigator. Confirmed via stack trace (_debugCheckDuplicatedPageKeys on the root navigator) + the router's own pre-existing comments documenting this exact failure for /sales/invoice/:invoiceId and the 2 detail routes. Fix: promoted all three targets (/inventory/labels, /inventory/adjustments/create, /inventory/stock/movement) from the inventory StatefulShellBranch to TOP-LEVEL routes (declared after the shell; stock/movement placed BEFORE stock/:productId so the literal segment wins the match), keeping their _fadePage transitions. A root route pushes cleanly from BOTH the in-branch list pages and the root stock-detail page, so every call site (products_page, stock_levels_page, adjustments_page, product_stock_detail_page) works. Side effect (accepted, user-approved): these three forms/actions no longer show the left rail / bottom nav while open -- they are focused full-screen flows with an AppDetailScaffold back button, consistent with the other top-level detail routes. This partially reverses commit d71472f's "promote sub-routes into the branch" for these 3 leaves only; the list/browse sub-routes stay in-branch with the rail. Surfaced by the new scan-to-jump feature which makes the stock-detail page a hot destination. analyze 8 issues/0 new; on-device verification owed (print/adjust/opening from stock detail, + from the in-branch list pages).

2026-07-24 -- Fixed "This application does not support printing." on macOS. The printing plugin is registered (PrintingPlugin in GeneratedPluginRegistrant) but the sandboxed macOS app lacked the com.apple.security.print entitlement, so Printing.layoutPdf could not reach the print system. Added <key>com.apple.security.print</key><true/> to BOTH macos/Runner/DebugProfile.entitlements and Release.entitlements. Entitlements are applied at build/sign time -> requires a FULL rebuild (stop + flutter run -d macos), not hot reload/restart. Windows/mobile unaffected (no sandbox entitlement needed). This was the last blocker after the shell-route key-collision fix; label print (barcode + QR) now works end to end on desktop.

2026-07-24 -- Label PDF: embed a Unicode font. Product names with non-Latin1 glyphs (e.g. "LCD Unit — Huawei..." with em-dash U+2014) triggered dart_pdf "Helvetica has no Unicode support / Unable to find a font to draw" warnings and rendered a blank .notdef box, because LabelPdfService built pw.Document() with the default Latin-1 Helvetica. Fix: pass theme: pw.ThemeData.withFont(base: PdfGoogleFonts.openSansRegular(), bold: PdfGoogleFonts.openSansBold()) -- the exact pattern already used by receipt_pdf_service.dart (DRY). Open Sans covers Latin-extended + em-dash/punctuation (Urdu/Arabic still needs a Noto font -- future, same limitation noted for receipts). PdfGoogleFonts fetches+caches from Google Fonts (needs network on first use), consistent with the receipt service. Note: the "stuck spinner" on macOS is separate -- Printing.layoutPdf awaits the native print panel; "Failed to foreground app; open returned 1" in the log means the panel can open behind the app window under the experimental merged-thread mode. analyze clean.

2026-07-24 -- Scan a product QR/barcode from an IMAGE (desktop scan path). Live camera scanning (barcodeScanSupported) is iOS/Android-only, so macOS had no scan entry point at all. Added scanBarcodeFromImage() in barcode_scan_page.dart: FilePicker.pickFiles(FileType.image) -> MobileScannerController().analyzeImage(path) -> first barcode rawValue/displayValue; returns barcodeNoCodeFoundSentinel if the image has no readable code, null on cancel/refusal (PlatformException). New barcodeImageScanSupported flag (iOS/Android/macOS; excludes web + windows/linux where mobile_scanner has no analyzeImage). products_page: extracted the lookup+jump into _handleScannedCode(code); the scan icon now shows whenever camera OR image is supported and _onScanTap offers a camera/image chooser sheet (_ScanSourceSheet) on mobile (both) or goes straight to the image picker on macOS. Reuses the existing searchProductsUseCaseProvider lookup + /inventory/stock/:id jump, and the macOS user-selected read entitlement already present for CSV import covers reading the picked image. No new deps (file_picker + mobile_scanner already in pubspec). analyze 8 issues/0 new; on-device verify owed on macOS (pick a printed-QR screenshot -> jumps to product) + mobile (chooser -> camera and image both work).

2026-07-24 -- Product scan extended to POS + dashboard; scan logic unified. Extracted scanProductCode(context) into barcode_scan_page.dart (camera/image chooser via _ScanSourceSheet where both supported, camera-only, or image-only on macOS) so Products/POS/Dashboard share ONE entry point instead of duplicating the chooser. products_page._scan now calls it (removed its local _ScanSourceSheet). POS (pos_terminal_page._scanBarcode): now camera OR image; on a single exact barcode/SKU match it ADDS the product to the cart directly (_addLine + SnackBar), else fills the search — online lookup via searchProductsUseCaseProvider, offline via offlineProductSearchProvider(code).future, so it works in offline cash-sale mode too. Dashboard (quick_launch): new permission-gated 'Scan product' tile (inventory:read), shown only where barcodeScanSupported||barcodeImageScanSupported; scans then jumps to /inventory/stock/:id on exact match (SnackBar on no match). All reuse the exact-match rule (barcode==code||sku==code) and the top-level stock route. analyze 8/0. On-device verify owed: macOS POS (pick QR image → adds to cart), mobile POS (chooser), dashboard tile both platforms.

2026-07-24 -- Scan on Windows (+ fix desktop POS scan button). Root cause the scan button never showed on Windows: barcodeImageScanSupported was gated to iOS/Android/macOS (mobile_scanner has NO Windows plugin — analyzeImage throws MissingPluginException), and separately pos_search_bar gated its scan button on barcodeScanSupported (mobile-only), so POS scan was hidden on ALL desktop incl. macOS. Fixes: (1) added zxing2 + image packages; scanBarcodeFromImage now branches — mobile_scanner.analyzeImage where a plugin exists (mobileScannerAnalyzeImageSupported = iOS/Android/macOS, full formats), else a pure-Dart zxing2 decode (_decodeQrPureDart: image.decodeImage → Int32List pixels → RGBLuminanceSource → HybridBinarizer → QRCodeReader) on Windows/Linux. LIMITATION: zxing2 reads QR/DataMatrix only, not 1D (CODE128/EAN13) — acceptable since the feature is QR-centric; 1D-from-image stays macOS/mobile only. (2) barcodeImageScanSupported widened to !kIsWeb so the icon/tile appears on every desktop. (3) pos_search_bar scan button now shows when barcodeScanSupported||barcodeImageScanSupported (was hidden on all desktop). New deps justified: pure-Dart, no native build (a POS must run on Windows without a C++ toolchain); zxing2 chosen over flutter_zxing to avoid native platform builds. analyze 8/0; on-device verify owed on Windows (pick a QR image → Products jump / POS add-to-cart / dashboard jump).

2026-07-24 -- Moved product-scan entry to the page HEADERS (user request). POS: scan action added to the SalesHeader/ModuleHeader `actions` slot (top-right, before sync chip/bell) → runs the existing add-to-cart scan; the search-bar scan button was reverted to its original barcodeScanSupported gate (header is now the single desktop entry). Dashboard: scan HeaderIconButton (LucideIcons.qrCode) added to DashboardAppBar before edit-layout, with _scanAndJump moved there; the QuickLaunch "Scan product" tile added earlier was removed. Both gated on barcodeScanSupported||barcodeImageScanSupported so they appear on every native platform (incl. Windows via the zxing2 image path). analyze 8/0.

2026-07-24 -- Voice search added to POS + dashboard; enabled on macOS (Phase 1, native). speech_to_text supports iOS/Android/macOS/web but NOT Windows/Linux. Phase 1: voice_support now exposes voiceSearchNativeSupported (iOS/Android/macOS), voiceSearchCloudSupported (windows/linux, for Phase 2) and voiceSearchSupported (either). Products/POS/dashboard mics gate on voiceSearchNativeSupported. POS: mic in the ModuleHeader actions (next to scan) → VoiceInputService live partials fill the POS search. Dashboard: _DashboardVoiceButton (StatefulWidget) in DashboardAppBar, shown when isWide && native, dictates into the global search via new GlobalSearchFieldState.setQuery(). macOS enablement: added NSMicrophoneUsageDescription + NSSpeechRecognitionUsageDescription to macos Info.plist and com.apple.security.device.audio-input to both entitlements (iOS already had the strings). Phase 2 (NOT built): Windows/Linux cloud STT — record plugin captures audio → a transcribe Edge Function (verifies JWT, calls Whisper, holds OPENAI_API_KEY) → text; a unified VoiceSearch service routes native vs cloud and all 3 modules flip their gate to voiceSearchSupported. Blocked on provider choice + API-key secret + edge deploy (untestable until provisioned, same pattern as the WhatsApp receipt work). analyze 8/0.

2026-07-24 -- Windows/Linux voice search via Voxa (self-hosted STT), replacing the planned Whisper/OpenAI cloud path. Voxa (RaoAhmadRaza/VOXA) is a local FastAPI service: POST /transcribe (multipart audio, ?format=txt → plain text) + GET /health, default http://127.0.0.1:8000, fully offline/private, no API key/cost — ideal for a POS. Client: new VoxaStt (core/services/voxa_stt_service.dart) — records mic via `record` (wav 16kHz mono) to a temp file, ensureRunning() health-checks and optionally app-launches the sidecar (Process.start of --dart-define VOXA_CMD; VOXA_URL overrides base), then POSTs the clip and returns the transcript. Added deps record 6.2.1 + path_provider + http. All 3 voice mics (products search-row, POS header, dashboard header) now branch: native speech_to_text (iOS/Android/macOS, live partials) vs Voxa (Windows/Linux, tap-to-start/tap-to-stop, no partials); gates flipped to voiceSearchSupported. Deployment = app-launched local sidecar (user's choice); set VOXA_CMD to the bundled voxa exe or "python -m voxa.server", or run Voxa separately (health-check reuses it). NOT verified on-device — needs Voxa reachable on the Windows machine. analyze 8/0. No secret/edge-function needed (local, not cloud).

2026-07-24 -- Voxa STT sidecar auto-starts on desktop app launch. main() fires VoxaStt.ensureRunning() (unawaited) in the isDesktop block when voiceSearchCloudSupported (Windows/Linux), warming the server before the user taps the mic; app.dart kills it on AppLifecycleState.detached (best effort; health-check reuse handles any orphan). VoxaStt._resolveLaunch() defaults to the BUNDLED exe next to the app: <dir of Platform.resolvedExecutable>/voxa/voxa[.exe], and sets env VOXA_HOST/VOXA_PORT (parsed from VOXA_URL) + VOXA_MODEL (default base) + HF_HOME=<appdir>/voxa/models so pre-bundled weights load offline; started with ProcessStartMode.detached. --dart-define VOXA_CMD overrides the exe (e.g. "python -m voxa.server"). REQUIRES packaging (user-provided, not in repo): a self-contained voxa.exe built with PyInstaller --noconsole placed at voxa/voxa.exe beside the app, plus the HF model cache at voxa/models, both shipped by the Windows installer. Not verifiable here (Windows + bundled exe). analyze 8/0.

2026-07-24 -- Voxa Windows build tooling added (tools/voxa/). Verified my Flutter client matches Voxa's real api.py: POST /transcribe multipart field 'file', ?format=txt → text/plain body, GET /health 200, env VOXA_HOST/PORT/MODEL — no client changes. A Windows .exe cannot be cross-compiled from macOS (PyInstaller must run on Windows), so the repo carries only build tooling, not the binary: tools/voxa/{build_windows.ps1, voxa.spec, run_voxa.py, README.md}. build_windows.ps1 (run on Windows) makes a venv, installs Voxa + PyInstaller, pre-downloads the model into <RunnerRelease>/voxa/models (staged HF cache for offline), runs PyInstaller (--noconsole) → voxa.exe, and stages it at <RunnerRelease>/voxa/voxa.exe — exactly where VoxaStt._resolveLaunch() looks. The 8MB VOXA-main.zip (7MB test audio) is git-ignored, not committed. voxa.spec is best-effort (ctranslate2/onnxruntime/av are PyInstaller-fiddly — collect_all used; may need hiddenimports tweaks on first real build). Installer must copy the voxa/ folder beside pos_app.exe. NOT verified on a real Windows build.

2026-07-24 -- Fix: approved PO stayed stuck at SUBMITTED. Root cause: the approval engine is entity-agnostic — act_on_approval only sets approval_requests.status=APPROVED; nothing advanced the underlying purchase_order, so completing the chain in the Approvals tab was a dead end (the PO's own manual Approve button, approve_purchase_order, was the only path — redundant/hidden once a workflow exists). Fix (migration 20260724150000_po_advance_on_approval): AFTER UPDATE OF status trigger on approval_requests → advance_po_on_approval() advances the PO (status/approved_by=final approver/approved_at/version, same 4-col update approve_purchase_order does — no GL/stock side effects to share) when a purchase_orders request reaches APPROVED. The approval chain IS the authorization, so it bypasses the manual purchase:approve gate. Trigger over editing the generic engine to keep PO coupling in the integration layer (mirrors 20260714101236). NOT handled: REJECTED leaves the PO at SUBMITTED (business decision owed — back to DRAFT vs CANCELLED). Client note: purchasing list/detail refresh on next load, not live (no cross-feature provider invalidation). Owed: supabase db push + verify approve-in-tab moves PO.

2026-07-24 -- Product image UPLOAD added (was URL-paste only). New public storage bucket 'product-images' (migration 20260724170000) — public because catalog images aren't sensitive, giving stable CDN URLs with no signed-URL churn in lists/POS grid; writes tenant-scoped by path '<tenant_id>/<product_id>/<ts>.<ext>' + gated on inventory:update (mirrors signatures bucket). Flow: file_picker (already installed, no new dep — image pick works on desktop file-dialog AND mobile gallery on all platforms) -> datasource.uploadProductImage(bytes) -> storage.uploadBinary + getPublicUrl -> reuses existing addImage() to insert the product_images row. First image on a product auto-set primary. Form 'Add image' now opens a chooser: Upload from device | Paste image URL (old URL path kept). No camera-live-capture / no image downscaling (image: pkg present) — YAGNI; add if raw phone-photo sizes bite. analyze 8/0. NOT verified on device; REQUIRES supabase db push (bucket won't exist until then -> upload 400s; nothing else breaks, additive).

2026-07-25 -- Inventory top-level detail routes now show the desktop nav rail (fix: they rendered as bare full screens, no rail/fade). The 5 routes (/inventory/stock/:productId detail, /inventory/products/:productId edit, /inventory/stock/movement, /inventory/adjustments/create, /inventory/labels) MUST stay top-level (outside the StatefulShellRoute) because they are pushed from ABOVE the shell (dashboard drilldown, notifications, global search, sync centre) where a shell descendant crashes on a duplicate page key (!keyReservation.contains(key)). So instead of moving them into the shell, wrapped each in new RailDetailScaffold (lib/core/widgets/bottom_nav_shell.dart): on >=900px it renders the same _NavRail + Expanded(child); below, the child alone (keeps its own back nav). Rail navigates with context.go (not goBranch) via _branchRoot map since these pages live above the shell. Extracted _branchMapFor() shared by BottomNavShell + RailDetailScaffold (no drift). Also switched product-detail + product-edit from builder: to pageBuilder:_fadePage (they lacked the fade the other 3 already had). Chose wrapper over moving-into-shell to avoid reintroducing the push-from-above crash + changing app-wide push->go semantics. analyze 8/0. NOT verified on device.

2026-07-25 -- Product detail page (product_stock_detail_page.dart) redesigned premium within the Lumina system (no new tokens): larger image hero (120/92px), mono SKU chip, hairline-divided spec strip (Cost/Margin/On hand/Reorder) replacing 4 grey boxes, right-sized action row (was 4 full-width slabs), section headers with leading icon + rule. Added a "Stock trend" card: cumulative-on-hand sparkline from the ledger via CustomPainter (NO chart dep — ponytail; fl_chart avoided) with a dashed reorder reference line; shows "not enough history" under 2 ledger points. Responsive via LayoutBuilder (wide >=560 inside the card). Presentation-only. analyze 8/0.

2026-07-25 -- Fix: POS and Products page cross-filtered each other's product lists. Root cause: POS online path watched the inventory AsyncNotifier productsProvider AND drove it via productsProvider.notifier.search(query) — the same stateful provider the Products catalog page owns, so a search in one mutated the other. Offline POS was already decoupled (offlineProductSearchProvider family by query). Fix: added posProductSearchProvider (FutureProvider.family<List<Product>,String> in sales/presentation/controllers/pos_products_controller.dart) mirroring the offline family — POS online now watches posProductSearchProvider(_query), never touches productsProvider. Both POS paths driven by _query alone; removed the productsProvider.notifier.search() call + its import. Stock still from stockLevelsProvider (unchanged). Note: empty-query POS default now returns searchProducts('') = 50 rows (was productsProvider's 200-page); POS is search/scan-driven so acceptable. analyze 8/0.

2026-07-25 -- POS grid scroll-to-load-more. Upgraded the POS-own online provider from a flat FutureProvider.family (searchProducts, 50 rows, no paging) to posProductsProvider = a SECOND independent instance of the inventory ProductsController (AsyncNotifierProvider, same class → separate state). POS reuses its pagination (200/page + loadMore), search and stock-merge while staying decoupled from the catalog page's productsProvider. pos_terminal adds a grid ScrollController: near-bottom (maxScrollExtent-400) + online + notifier.hasMore → loadMore. Online search now drives posProductsProvider.notifier.search(); offline still reads offlineProductSearchProvider(_query) family (cache, no paging). ProductGrid gained an optional scrollController wired to its GridView. analyze 8/0.

2026-07-25 -- Fix: duplicate products in the POS grid (and inventory list when scrolled past 200). Root cause: loadProducts paginated with .order('created_at' desc).range(from,to) — created_at is NOT unique (bulk_import_products inserts a batch sharing one timestamp), so Postgres' order among equal keys differs between page queries and range() returns the same row on multiple pages while skipping others. loadMore appended [...current, ...merged] with no de-dup, so a row reappearing on page 1/2/3 showed 2/3/4x (the observed 4/2/1 pattern). Surfaced now because POS scroll-to-load-more was added today; page 0 alone was always unique. Fix: (1) total order — added .order('id') tiebreaker so range() pages are disjoint/deterministic (inventory_remote_datasource.loadProducts; fixes POS + Products list, both share it); (2) defence-in-depth — loadMore now filters out ids already in state via a seen-set before appending. searchProducts unaffected (no pagination, limit 50). analyze 8/0.

2026-07-25 -- AI Companion (in-app chat assistant) — Phases 1–5 CODE COMPLETE, deploy owed. New feature: a read-only conversational assistant that explains the app + finds features (from a baked-in route/feature knowledge base in the system prompt) and answers questions about the user's OWN business data by calling the existing report RPCs. Architecture decision: the Google Gemini API key (`@google/genai`) lives ONLY in a new Supabase Edge Function `supabase/functions/llm-proxy/` (Deno.env, set via `supabase secrets set GEMINI_API_KEY`) — NEVER in the app (which ships only the anon key). (Originally scoped on Anthropic Claude; switched to Gemini per owner before deploy — SSE contract unchanged, so zero Flutter changes.) The function mirrors the existing user-JWT-gated `list-sessions` pattern (withSupabase({auth:["publishable","secret"]}) + getUser 401 + resolve tenant). SECURITY INVARIANT: all data tools run on `ctx.supabase` (the caller's RLS-scoped client), never service-role, so cross-tenant leakage is structurally impossible and the model cannot read another tenant's data even if prompted. Tool whitelist (`tools.ts`) is READ-ONLY by construction — 15 tools mapping to read RPCs (dashboard_summary, receivables/payables_aging, customer/supplier_ledger + find_ resolvers, profit_loss/trial_balance/balance_sheet, report_daily_sales/inventory_valuation/product_performance, drilldown_low_stock, search_products); runTool rejects any unregistered name; NO mutation RPC is ever registered. Tools emitted as Gemini `functionDeclarations` (JSON-schema → uppercase Type via a small converter). Model gemini-2.5-flash (swap to gemini-2.5-pro for max reasoning — one string), streaming SSE (event: delta/tool/done/error) via a manual function-call loop over `generateContentStream` (append model turn's text+functionCall parts, respond with functionResponse parts; loop ends when a turn emits no calls). Stable system-instruction prefix (app KB) + appended per-user permission block so "you can/can't reach X" is honest. Persistence: migration 20260725120000 adds chat_conversations + chat_messages (tenant + user-owned RLS `tenant_id=auth_tenant_id() and user_id=auth.uid()`, realtime-published). Per-user rate limit (20 msgs/60s → 429) + token usage logged to chat_messages.usage for cost attribution. Flutter `lib/features/assistant/` mirrors notifications clean-arch: entities/model/datasource (raw http SSE stream — functions.invoke buffers, so POST to the function URL with the JWT and parse SSE) / repository + a Notifier<AssistantState> controller (optimistic user+assistant bubbles, stream deltas into the last bubble). Chat UI net-new (ChatBubble, ChatComposer with native STT mic reusing VoiceInputService, AssistantPage bespoke Scaffold — AppDetailScaffold is scroll-centered, wrong for a pinned composer). Top-level route /assistant (sibling GoRoute, no redirect/branch-index changes); AssistantLauncher (sparkles, copy of NotificationBell) mounted in module_scaffold + dashboard_app_bar → present on every screen. Voice v1 = native only (iOS/Android/macOS via voiceSearchNativeSupported); Windows/Linux Voxa desktop STT deferred. analyze 8/0 (0 new). DEPLOY OWED (owner, hits prod + Gemini billing): supabase db push; supabase secrets set GEMINI_API_KEY=...; supabase functions deploy llm-proxy; then on-device verify. DATA-POLICY GATE: chat content (balances, salaries) is sent to Google Gemini — owner must sign off before real rollout. DEFERRED: token-level streaming already done; conversation list/rename/delete UI, realtime multi-device reconciliation in the controller, desktop Voxa voice.

2026-07-25 -- AI Companion bring-up: provider + auth corrections (supersede the entry above). (1) AUTH: `withSupabase({auth:["publishable","secret"]})` from @supabase/server REJECTED this project's legacy anon JWT with `{"code":"INVALID_CREDENTIALS"}` (it wants the new sb_publishable_ key format). Replaced with the classic pattern — `Deno.serve` + `createClient(SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_ANON_KEY, {global.headers.Authorization: caller JWT})` + `getUser()`; deploy `--no-verify-jwt` (gateway open, auth in-handler). Still RLS-scoped as the caller → tools stay tenant-safe. (2) PROVIDER: Anthropic→Gemini→**OpenAI**. Gemini rejected: `gemini-2.5-flash` 404 "no longer available to new users", then `gemini-2.0-flash` 429 `limit:0` (free tier unavailable in region/PKR without billing). Owner supplied an OpenAI key. Now `npm:openai`, `chat.completions` streaming + tool-call loop (accumulate streamed tool_calls fragments by index → run → role:tool results → loop), model `OPENAI_MODEL` env default `gpt-4o-mini` (cheapest reliable tool-caller; gpt-4.1-nano/gpt-5-nano cheaper). Tools = standard OpenAI function schema (our input_schema as-is, no converter). `cleanError()` unwraps nested SDK error JSON for readable chat errors. Debug logs added both sides ([llm-proxy] server console + [assistant] Flutter, no secrets — lengths/booleans only). VERIFIED working end-to-end on macOS (status 200, streaming, tool loop, persistence); only external dependency is the OpenAI key having quota. SSE contract unchanged throughout all provider swaps → ZERO Flutter changes across Anthropic/Gemini/OpenAI.

2026-07-25 -- AI Companion system-prompt hardened (prompt.ts, edge-fn only — exports unchanged, ZERO Flutter change). Rewrote SYSTEM_STABLE for a small non-reasoning model (gpt-4o-mini): (1) SCOPE hard-boundary — explicit refuse list (general knowledge/news/math, code/content writing, other software/companies, other tenants' data, off-app advice medical/legal/investment/personal/political, roleplay/persona) + one-line refusal template + redirect; greetings & "what can you do" kept in-scope. Reason: cheap model would otherwise answer arbitrary off-topic prompts, wasting the OpenAI budget and misrepresenting the product. (2) ANSWER PROCEDURE baked in as fixed rules (not left to the model's weak reasoning): classify request (out-of-scope→refuse / feature-or-concept→answer from KB, no tool / data→tool); one tool per intent; resolver-first chaining (find_customer|find_supplier|search_products → ledger/detail, never pass a name where an id is required); multi-tool chaining allowed; dates — model does NOT know today, omit dates for optional-date tools (server defaults to current), and for range-REQUIRED reports (profit_loss) ASK the user rather than guess; summarize large results, don't dump rows. (3) OUTPUT STYLE — lead with the answer, terse chat bubble, bold key figure, tables for multi-row/aging, tenant currency as-returned. (4) Anti-hallucination + no prompt/tool/internal-id disclosure reinforced. Kept app-area KB + glossary verbatim (DRY — per-tool triggers already live in tools.ts descriptions). Fixed stale "Gemini systemInstruction" comment → OpenAI. flutter analyze unaffected (Deno file, not in Flutter tree). DEPLOY OWED to take effect: supabase functions deploy llm-proxy --no-verify-jwt.

2026-07-25 -- AI Companion launcher promoted to a nav-shell branch (user-approved router edit). The sparkles AssistantLauncher lived in the dashboard + module headers and pushed /assistant as a top-level route. Moved into the nav bar exactly like the module promotions (reporting/accounting/approvals/hr): /assistant is now StatefulShellBranch index 11 (appended LAST so no earlier branch index shifts), single-screen branch (no sub-routes → no sub-rail, plain builder like dashboard). bottom_nav_shell.dart: _allItems += Assistant (LucideIcons.sparkles, index 11), _kAssistantBranch=11, _branchMapFor inserts it UNGATED just before the pinned Settings (no assistant:read permission exists — available to every signed-in user), _branchRoot += '/assistant'. No sub-rail/switch case (assistant has no in-branch destinations). AssistantPage lost its header back button + onBack param (a branch root has nothing to pop to; the rail/bottom bar is the exit now). analyze 8/0 (baseline 8, 0 new). Flutter-only, no redeploy.
