2026-07-25 -- AI Companion chat now renders GFM pipe tables (closes the deferred "richer markdown / tables" item). The hardened system prompt tells the model to use tables for multi-row data, so replies started emitting `| a | b |` blocks that the zero-dependency AssistantMarkdown renderer showed as raw pipes. Extended the SAME renderer (no new package — ponytail): the line loop now detects a run of `|`-rows whose 2nd row is the `|---|` divider and renders it as a real Flutter Table (hairline TableBorder, surface2 bold header row, ClipRRect sm radius, FlexColumnWidth so cells wrap to bubble width instead of overflowing). Cells reuse _spans so inline **bold**/`code` still render inside a cell. Ragged rows are normalised to the header's column count (pad/truncate) so a malformed LLM row can't trip Table's equal-length requirement. A lone `| x` line with no divider falls through to plain text (unchanged). Flutter-only, no redeploy, hot-reload. analyze 8/0 (baseline 8, 0 new).

2026-07-25 -- AI Companion: conversation history + data-freshness fix (two user-reported bugs). BUG 1 "doesn't show previous data queries": AssistantController.build() returned an empty state and NEVER called the datasource's loadHistory/listConversations (both were dead code), so every (re)build started a brand-new conversation and prior threads were invisible. Fix (Flutter, hot-reload): controller now (a) resumes the most recent thread on open via _resumeLast() — listConversations() → loadHistory(newest) → populate state, with a guard so it won't clobber a chat the user already started; (b) holds conversations + activeConversationId in AssistantState; (c) gained openConversation/renameConversation/deleteConversation/startNewChat + _refreshConversations; send() now reads/sets state.activeConversationId (replacing the private _conversationId) and refreshes the list after a new thread is created. Datasource gained renameConversation (update title) + deleteConversation (delete; chat_messages cascade) — RLS policy is `for all to authenticated` scoped tenant+user, so UPDATE/DELETE were already permitted, NO migration needed. UI: assistant header gained a history button (messagesSquare) opening a _HistorySheet (showAppSheet — modal wide / bottom sheet narrow) that watches the controller and lists threads newest-first with tap-to-open, rename (nested showAppSheet + AppTextField), delete (showAppConfirm + showAppToast), and a New button; the old back button was already removed when /assistant became a nav branch, and reset() was renamed startNewChat (keeps the history list). BUG 2 "recent sale not reflected until restart": the assistant queries live each message, so restart only "fixed" it by resetting to a NEW conversation — within one thread the model reused its earlier answer/number instead of re-calling the tool, and it couldn't scope "today" (no current date in the prompt). Fix (edge fn, REDEPLOY): index.ts injects `new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)` into buildSystem(permissionKeys, today) → a "Today is <date> (UTC)" block; prompt.ts date rule rewritten (use the injected date to resolve today/yesterday/this-month; only ask when genuinely ambiguous) + a new "always fetch fresh — CALL the tool this turn, never reuse an earlier figure, data changes as sales/payments/stock move" rule. ponytail: reused the existing datasource/design-system widgets, no new deps, no migration. Realtime multi-device sync (item #4) intentionally NOT added — user chose "recent sale" not "new messages need reload". analyze 8/0 (baseline 8, 0 new). DEPLOY OWED for BUG 2: supabase functions deploy llm-proxy --no-verify-jwt (BUG 1 is Flutter-only).

2026-07-25 -- Products page (inventory) now orders products WITH a photo first, then the rest. Presentation-only, frontend boundary respected: a stable partition of the already-loaded list in _buildBody (`imageUrl` non-empty first, imageless after) feeds the existing card GridView — no provider/datasource/pagination change (POS + catalog share loadProducts, left untouched). Stable within each group so the server created_at+id order is otherwise preserved; re-partitions on every rebuild so newly paged-in image products bubble up. No section headers added (not asked). analyze 8/0.

2026-07-26 -- Dashboard hover states. Nothing in the app had a hover effect (grep: zero MouseRegion/onHover outside bottom_nav_shell), and the existing InkWells could not provide one — their ink paints on the Material *behind* an opaque ClayContainer, so it never shows. Rather than plumbing a hovered flag + a second colour through every card, added ONE shared primitive `core/design/widgets/app_hover.dart`: `AppHover` = MouseRegion → Stack(child, Positioned.fill(IgnorePointer(AnimatedOpacity(tint)))). Overlay approach chosen deliberately: it works identically over flat fills, gradients and clay shadows, and leaves every existing colour/size/shadow byte-identical ("keep it alike"), whereas swapping surface tokens per widget would have meant a bespoke hover colour for each of the 9 surfaces. Tint = accent @ 7% light / 12% dark, AppMotion.fast + AppMotion.curve; `enabled: false` returns the child untouched (locked quick-launch tiles, disabled KPI move buttons). Wrapped in the dashboard module only: KpiTile grid entries, KpiEditFooter buttons, HeaderIconButton, quick-launch tiles, WelcomeCard, DashboardSectionCard (covers trend/payments/recent), _SaleRow, drilldown back button + rows. 'View all' TextButton left alone — Material's own hover overlay already applies (theme sets no overlayColor). Recent-sales rows needed a ClipRRect on the row Column so the last row's square tint doesn't poke past the card's rounded bottom. Presentation-only, frontend boundary respected: no controller/router/data edits, no new deps. test/design/app_hover_test.dart asserts opacity 0 → 1 → 0 across a mouse enter/exit and that enabled:false is a pass-through; both pass. analyze 8/0 (baseline 8, 0 new).

2026-07-26 -- AppHover fixes after screenshot review (2 user-reported bugs, both in the new primitive, no call-site changes). BUG 1 "hover getting out of many cards": Stack defaults to StackFit.loose, which LOOSENS the incoming constraints for non-positioned children. Inside a GridView cell (tight) the wrapped KPI/quick-launch tile therefore shrank to its content height while the Stack itself stayed the full cell, so the Positioned.fill overlay painted past the card's bottom edge — and the cards silently shrank too, breaking "keep it alike". Fix: `fit: StackFit.passthrough` (one word) — non-positioned children get the incoming constraints unmodified, so the child is exactly the size it was before AppHover existed and the overlay is exactly the child. Regression test asserts a min-sized Column still fills a tight 200x200 parent (fails on loose). BUG 2 "colour scheme not consistent with the sidebar": the tint was an accent-blue wash; the nav rail hovers with `hoverColor: lum.g100` — a neutral, and its BLUE is reserved for the *active* item. A blue hover therefore read as "selected" and clashed. Retinted neutral: light = `lum.ink` @ 5.5% (white -> ~#F1F1F1, i.e. g100's own step), dark = white @ 5%. Accent is left to mean selection only. analyze 8/0 (baseline 8, 0 new); 3/3 hover tests pass.

2026-07-26 -- Scan result is now a confirm popup, not an immediate action. All three scan entry points (dashboard header, POS header, products page) diverged on what an exact barcode/SKU match did: dashboard + products jumped to /inventory/stock/<id>, POS silently added a cart line. Unified on one shared widget, features/sales/presentation/widgets/scanned_product_dialog.dart: showScannedProductDialog() = clay Dialog (same shell as showAppConfirm) with photo/name/SKU/price/stock pill/type/barcode + "Add to Cart" and "Back". Placed under sales (not core) because it writes to posCartProvider — core must not depend on a feature. The tax-inclusive price split that lived inline in PosTerminalPage._addLine moved into addProductToCart(ref, product) in the same file and _addLine now delegates, so the POS grid tap and the popup cannot drift apart. Add button wrapped in PermissionGate(sales:create) so inventory-only staff get details + Back only. Presentation-only: no controller/usecase/repo/router/schema change; the stock-detail route is untouched, just no longer auto-navigated to on scan. analyze 8/0 (baseline 8, 0 new).

2026-07-26 -- Dashboard drilldown date is now UTC (fixes "Today's sales / Today's profit open an empty list"). Not a regression from the hover work — the KPI tap handler was untouched. Root cause: the client sent `DateTime.now().toIso8601String()` = the user's LOCAL date, while both ends of the DB disagree with that — `dashboard_summary` buckets today as `date_trunc('day', now())` and `drilldown_sales` filters `date(created_at) = p_date`, each evaluated in the PostgREST session timezone (UTC). At UTC+5 (PKT) the local date runs a day ahead of UTC between 05:00 and midnight local, so the tile counted Saturday's invoices (visible in the trend chart, whose last bar was Sat while the client's date was Sun) and the drilldown then asked for Sunday's — none existed → "Nothing to show". Fix: `.toUtc()` at the two drilldown call sites (kpi_grid.dart `_GridEntry`, dashboard_page.dart `_viewAllSales`), so the client, the summary and the drilldown all bucket the same day. Presentation-only, no SQL/migration — the DB is already internally consistent, it was only the client that disagreed. Longer-term alternative (NOT taken, needs a tenant-timezone concept + migration): make the RPCs bucket by a tenant-configured timezone instead of UTC, so "today" means the store's day rather than UTC's. Same latent mismatch exists for any other caller that passes a local date to a date(created_at) RPC. analyze 8/0.

2026-07-26 -- Products-page scan reverted to the stock-detail jump; the cart action moved onto that page instead. The popup stays for the dashboard + POS header scans only (those have no natural destination). ProductStockDetailPage._HeaderCard gained an onAddToCart callback -> addProductToCart(ref, product) + snackbar, rendered as a tinted "Add to cart" button wrapped in PermissionGate(sales:create); the gate's own gap sits inside its child Padding so hiding it leaves no orphan spacing (left:10 wide, top:10 narrow/full-width). Same shared helper as POS and the popup, so the tax-inclusive price split has exactly one implementation. analyze 8/0 (baseline 8, 0 new).

2026-07-26 -- Product stock-detail action row overflowed 66px once "Add to cart" made it five buttons (and would already have overflowed between the 560px `wide` breakpoint and ~890px with the previous four). Fixed at the root, not by trimming labels: the wide branch is now a Wrap (spacing/runSpacing 8) instead of a fixed Row with a Spacer, and wide buttons drop to AppButtonSize.sm (38px, 13.5pt, 14 gutter) — ~130px reclaimed, so all five sit on one line at normal desktop widths and flow to a second line below instead of painting the overflow stripes. Cost: the secondary pair (Set opening stock / Print label) is no longer right-aligned; they read as the tail of one action group. Narrow layout untouched (md, stacked). analyze 8/0.

2026-07-26 -- Required-field marking is a shared primitive, not a per-form string. Marking mandatory fields with a red '*' first looked like a one-liner (append ' *' to each label), but the label is rendered in four places (AppTextField's own Column, the PO page's _PickerField and _labeled, the product page's _fieldLabel) each with its own padding, so a string suffix could never be coloured and the four would drift. Added core/design/widgets/app_field.dart with (a) AppFieldLabel — Text.rich(label + ' *' in lum.dangerText, semanticsLabel '<label>, required'), now used by AppTextField (new `isRequired` flag, default false so no existing call site changes) and by both forms' dropdown/picker labels, deleting the three duplicate label builders; and (b) AppFieldRow — LayoutBuilder that lays N fields across one Row and stacks them into a Column when maxWidth < minFieldWidth * N (default 190). AppFieldRow replaces the hardcoded 2-col Rows because the density pass wanted 3-4 fields per line, which a fixed Row would have crushed on phones/narrow desktop windows. Layout: both forms lead with the required fields and end in an "Extra info" card holding everything optional (PO: expected date, exch. rate, freight, insurance, custom duty, order discount, notes / product: description, min price, wholesale, weight, tags). Product form's cost + selling price are now genuinely enforced via _requiredFieldError(), shared by Save and Save & close — a red asterisk on an unvalidated field is a lie, and previously only Name was checked. UoM deliberately NOT marked: _buildCoreData already defaults it to 'PCS'. Presentation-only; no controller/usecase/router/schema change. analyze 8/0 (baseline 8, 0 new).

2026-07-26 -- Suppliers are creatable from inside the PO flow, without leaving it. The picker sheet was a dead end when the supplier did not exist yet (leave the draft PO, go to Suppliers, come back, re-enter every line). Added a "Create new supplier" tile at the top of showSupplierPicker's list opening a nested showAppSheet form; on success it pops the picker with the created Supplier, so the PO field is filled in one gesture. The quick form carries ONLY the fields the full supplier form validates (name, payment terms, currency, opening balance), each isRequired so AppFieldLabel paints the red '*', with the full form's exact validation copied rather than a looser variant -- a second, weaker validator would let the quick path create rows the real form would reject. status is forced ACTIVE (a supplier you are ordering from today is by construction active); everything else the full form offers is left out behind a footnote pointing at the Suppliers page. Wiring: reads createSupplierUseCaseProvider directly instead of SuppliersController.create, because create() discards the returned row and auto-selecting needs it -- same page-to-usecase shortcut supplier_form_page already uses for loadSupplier; suppliersProvider is refreshed after so the list behind stays correct. Presentation-only; no controller/usecase/repository/router/schema change. analyze clean on the changed file.

2026-07-26 -- GRN receipt invalidates the per-PO providers in the controller, not at the call site, and lands on the invoice screen. PurchaseOrdersController.receive() only did ref.invalidateSelf() (the PO *list*); purchaseOrderDetailProvider(poId) and poGrnsProvider(poId) were left cached, so popping back to the PO detail still showed "Approved", no GRN and a live Receive button. The detail page hid this for submit/approve/cancel because it invalidates the detail provider itself after each in-page action -- receive is the one action that leaves the page, so it had no such call site. Fixed inside receive() (mirrors SuppliersController.edit invalidating supplierLedgerProvider) so every current and future caller reloads, instead of adding a fourth manual invalidate. Navigation: on success the receive page now context.pushReplacement's /purchasing/orders/:id/invoice -- replacement, not push, because the receive form is spent and back should reach the PO. Valid for both RECEIVED and PARTIALLY_RECEIVED (the same two states that show the detail page's "Create invoice" action), and PurchaseInvoiceMatchPage watches both invalidated providers so it opens with the new GRN already in it. Presentation-only. analyze clean on the 3 touched files.

2026-07-26 -- Suppliers list got a back arrow that go's to /purchasing rather than popping. Suppliers is a rail destination inside the purchasing shell branch (not a pushed page), so Navigator.maybePop -- what the module's form/detail back buttons do -- is a no-op whenever the branch was entered on the rail; context.go('/purchasing') is the only thing that reliably lands somewhere. Rendered as ModuleScaffold's `leading` using the same 44px clay-soft tile the other module headers use (copied, not promoted to core/design: ~10 pages already carry a private copy of this tile and unifying them is its own slice, out of scope here). analyze clean.

2026-07-26 -- FIX: intermittent red-screen on logout ('framework.dart' line 6417 InheritedElement.notifyClients: "check that it really is our descendant" assertion). Root cause = AuthController.signOut() cleared the router's user-scoped gate flags BEFORE destroying the session: BranchRouterState.reset() + resetUserScopedState() each notifyListeners() synchronously, and WorkspaceInitState.reset() sets completed=false while supabase.auth.currentSession is still non-null -- so _redirect fell through the `!loggedIn` branch into the workspace gate and navigated the still-logged-in user to /workspace-init. That screen mounted, fired its post-frame profile load and 10s timeout timer, then was torn down a moment later when the awaited signOut() landed, SIGNED_OUT fired and the redirect resolved to /login. Two overlapping _fadePage transitions (300ms in / 220ms out) plus a half-built screen whose provider loads complete after unmount = the intermittent element-lifecycle assertion ("sometimes it appears and sometimes it doesn't" = whether the two redirects land in the same frame, which depends on the secure-storage delete's platform-channel timing). Fix: AuthController.signOut() now only logs the audit event and awaits the sign-out use case; the SIGNED_OUT handler in router.dart already does BranchRouterState.reset() + resetUserScopedState() and by then currentSession is null, so _redirect short-circuits to /login before it ever consults the pin/branch/workspace/mfa gates. Logout is now ONE route transition. resetUserScopedState() consequently has exactly one caller (the router's auth-lifecycle handler) = single source of truth, and AuthController matches every other sign-out site in the app (workspace-init / pin-lock / branch-select / mfa-challenge all just call supabase.auth.signOut() and let the listener reset). analyze: 8 pre-existing infos, 0 new.

2026-07-26 -- App-wide copy tone pass: user-facing strings only, no logic, 108 files. Four rules, applied because the app read as three teams' work rather than one product. (1) ONE failure vocabulary: `We couldn't X` / `Couldn't X` / `Could not X` coexisted across 49 files -- all now `Unable to X`. Three spellings of the identical error is the single most visible inconsistency a buyer hits. (2) Reassurance padding deleted: `Your data is safe` (20 files) and `Nothing is lost` -- an error screen cannot verify that claim, and asserting it after a failed *read* is both patronising and unfalsifiable; the body is now just the recovery step. (3) `Something went wrong` (24 uses) names nothing -- replaced per site with what actually failed. (4) Error title and body must not restate each other; four sites had become "Unable to load notifications" / "Unable to load notifications. Try again." after the bulk pass and were split into title=what-failed, body=what-to-do. Also removed dead UI rather than rewording it: trial balance's Export button only ever toasted "Export coming soon" (deleted with its four now-orphan imports -- ship it or hide it, an apology is not a feature), and preferences' "More themes are on the way." roadmap promise (the `description` param is required on _PrefRow, so it took a factual line, not an empty string). Brand tagline "One light. Every operation." is now splash + auth hero ONLY -- it was also sitting on the inventory and purchasing hub headers, where a tagline on a stock screen reads as unfinished; removing it orphaned `lum` in purchase_hub_page. "Counter mode" -> "Dark mode" in settings (title AND the toggle's semanticLabel -- invented jargon that a screen reader says out loud). Ambiguous single-word controls disambiguated: Move to -> Move to stage, How many? -> Quantity, Extra info -> Additional details (x2), Keep it -> Cancel (it was the dismiss button on a *destructive* void-invoice confirm, where the casual register is wrong), Type then add -> "Type the number, then tap Add". `No filter` -> `None selected` NOT the proposed "No categories selected": that string lives in a shared multi-select sheet driven by `widget.allLabel`, used for categories, brands and statuses alike, so the category-specific wording would have been wrong two times out of three. Receipt copy (screen + PDF + WhatsApp share text) -> "Thank you for your business." Bulk edits ran via .NET ReadAllText/WriteAllText with UTF8Encoding($false) rather than Set-Content, because PS 5.1 would have BOM'd every file and mangled the em-dashes and curly apostrophes already in the copy; verified 0 mojibake after. Deliberately NOT changed (proposed, then cut as cosmetic rather than unprofessional): the `Nothing ...` empty states, `will show up here` -> `will appear here`, the assistant's time-of-day greetings, `Smart insights`, and the invite/notes microcopy. analyze: 8 pre-existing infos, 0 new.

2026-07-26 -- FIX: report Export PDF/CSV silently did nothing on Windows. Root cause = both paths used mobile share primitives with no await and no catch. CSV called SharePlus with an XFile (share_plus's Windows plugin throws UnimplementedError for files on pre-RS5 and routes through the OS share sheet otherwise -- either way nothing lands on disk); PDF called Printing.sharePdf, whose Windows impl writes the bytes to %TEMP% and ShellExecutes them, returning false with no exception if no handler opens. Because `onSelected` was sync and discarded the future, every failure mode looked identical: menu closes, nothing happens. Replaced both with `FilePicker.saveFile` (file_picker already a dep, used for pickFiles in 3 places; its Windows impl runs the comdlg32 save dialog then writes the bytes) -- a save dialog is the right verb for "Export" on a desktop-first POS anyway, and it is the one call that works on Windows/macOS/Linux/mobile alike. Service now returns the saved path (null = cancelled); the button awaits it and toasts saved-path / failure, plus a warning when the report has no rows (previously an invisible early-return). CSV gained a UTF-8 BOM so Excel stops mangling non-ASCII product names. NOT touched: the other Printing.sharePdf callers (invoice_detail_page, receipt_pdf_service) -- same fragility, different flow, out of scope for this prompt. analyze clean on lib/features/reporting.

2026-07-26 -- FIX: receipt "Print & share" was a dead button. Root cause was NOT the share plumbing (the prior entry's suspicion) but PDF construction: `ReceiptPdfService.buildReceipt` used `pw.MultiPage` with `PdfPageFormat.roll80`, and roll80's height is `double.infinity`, which multi_page.dart:252 asserts against -- `doc.addPage` threw before any print or share call ran, and `onPressed` discarded the future, so the tap looked like a no-op. Switched to `pw.Page`, which is the widget that supports an infinite page height: it measures the content and rewrites the page format to the actual roll length (page.dart:156). Body wrapped in a Column (mainAxisSize.min + crossAxisAlignment.stretch) since Page takes one child, not a list -- no pagination is lost because a thermal roll is one continuous page by definition. Same service backs invoice_detail_page's Reprint, so both flows were broken and both are fixed by the one change; verified the assertion fires on the old shape via a throwaway MultiPage+roll80 test before committing to the diagnosis. Added `test/sales/receipt_pdf_test.dart` (builds a real receipt, asserts non-trivial bytes) -- it fails on the pre-fix shape. Also closed the silent-failure hole around it: `_printAndShare` now try/catches and toasts the error, and `shareReceipt` returns `Printing.sharePdf`'s bool (Windows returns false with no exception when nothing is registered for .pdf) so a print-succeeded-share-failed run says so instead of looking identical to success. NOT fixed (pre-existing, out of scope): `PdfGoogleFonts.openSansRegular()` downloads from gstatic on every build and silently falls back to Latin-1 Helvetica when offline -- observed live in the test run, so non-ASCII product names print blank; the fix is bundling the .ttf as an asset, and it affects label_pdf_service equally. analyze clean on lib/features/sales + test/sales; repo baseline 8 pre-existing infos unchanged.

2026-07-26 -- FIX: report Export PDF threw TooManyPagesException on the inventory report. Not a "too big widget" -- MultiPage's `sameCount` guard counts one strike per page a single spanning widget covers and only resets when that widget finishes, so ONE table spanning more than `maxPages` (default 20) pages trips it; the inventory valuation table is ~1000 rows ≈ 30 pages. Set `maxPages: 5000` on the MultiPage. Note the guard lives inside an `assert`, so this only ever threw in debug/profile -- release builds were rendering these already. Extracted `ReportExport.buildPdfBytes()` from `pdf()` (the save dialog is untestable) and added test/reporting/report_export_test.dart rendering 1200 rows.
2026-07-25 -- Mobile nav + top safe-area (frontend/presentation-only, desktop untouched). (1) Bottom bar overflow: the not-in-module branch of _LuminaBottomBar rendered EVERY permitted module (~11 icons) in one row — unreadable on a phone. Now caps at _kMaxBottomTabs=5: if modules ≤5 show all inline (unchanged for low-permission users); if >5, show the first _kPrimaryBottomTabs=4 (Dashboard, Inventory, Purchase, Sales per branchMap order) + a "More" tab (LucideIcons.moreHorizontal, three dots) that opens the EXISTING _showModulesSheet — ponytail: reused the in-module sheet, added one `showActive` flag so the overflow variant lists every module and lights the active one (the in-module Modules tab still hides the current module, behavior byte-identical). The More tab itself lights when the active module is folded (selected >= 4). Folded modules stay reachable via onSelect from the sheet — no route/branchMap/goBranch change. (2) Dashboard top overlapped the status bar: DashboardAppBar is a bare Container in Scaffold.appBar and, unlike ModuleHeader, never padded for the status bar. Scaffold already reserves preferredSize.height + MediaQuery.padding.top for the appBar slot, so the fix is Container height += topInset and padding.top = topInset — surface now fills under the status bar and the row clears the notch. Desktop (≥900 rail / 0 inset) unaffected on both counts. analyze clean (2 files, 0 issues).

2026-07-25 -- Mobile "More" modules sheet reworked to a boxy grid (fixes a 134px overflow). The list variant (Column of _RailItem rows, mainAxisSize.min, no scroll) overflowed once >~8 modules were listed. Now: ConstrainedBox(maxHeight 70% screen) → GridView.count(crossAxisCount 3, shrinkWrap, childAspectRatio ~1.0) of _ModuleGridTile (icon over label, rounded card, accentSoft+accent when it's the active module, g100 otherwise). Grid scrolls inside the bounded sheet, so any module count fits. Drag handle re-centred (Align, was stretched full-width). Presentation-only, no route/data change. analyze clean.

2026-07-25 -- App-wide top safe-area fix for the shared ModuleHeader (module_scaffold.dart), used by every ModuleScaffold/SalesScaffold/RepairScaffold page + the POS terminal. The header was a fixed-height Container (54 narrow / 60 wide) with its Row wrapped in an inner SafeArea(bottom:false) — the fixed height clamped the child, so the status-bar inset SafeArea tried to add had nowhere to go and the back button / title / notification bell rendered over the status bar (not vertically centred in the visible bar). Fix mirrors the DashboardAppBar/_DrilldownAppBar pattern: height = base + MediaQuery.paddingOf(context).top, padding.top = that inset, inner SafeArea dropped. Self-correcting against double-insets (if an ancestor already consumed the top padding, paddingOf returns 0). Desktop inset is 0 → wide layout unchanged. AppDetailScaffold (accounting/reporting detail pages) already wraps its body in a full SafeArea, and NoAccessScaffold uses a Material AppBar, so both were already correct. Presentation-only, no route/data change. analyze clean.

2026-07-25 -- Product detail page (product_stock_detail_page.dart) now shows Variants + Pricing-tiers sections. No new data layer: the ProductVariant/PricingTier entities, models, datasource (loadVariants/loadPricingTiers over product_variants / product_pricing_tiers), repo methods and usecases (loadVariantsUseCaseProvider, loadPricingTiersUseCaseProvider) already existed for the product FORM — the detail (read) page just reused them. The page's imperative _load() now also fetches variants + tiers for the product (secondary — their failure leaves them empty, doesn't block the page or set _error). Two new read-only cards (_VariantsCard: name · sku · attributes → selling price; _PricingTiersCard: tier name · qty range (minQty–maxQty / minQty+) + optional discount% → unit price, sorted ascending by minQty) mirror the existing _LedgerCard/_PerWarehouseCard style (AppCard + _sectionHeader + hairline rows, AppMoneyText). Rendered only when the product has ACTIVE variants/tiers (isActive filter) so the vast majority of products with neither stay uncluttered. Presentation + reuse only, no migration, no new deps. analyze 8/0.

2026-07-27 -- Smart insights page paginates client-side: 10 cards, then a "Load more (N left)" button reveals 10 more. pendingRecommendationsProvider already returns every pending recommendation in one call, so this is a slice of the loaded list (_RecommendationList StatefulWidget holding _visible), NOT a server range query — no controller/usecase/datasource change and no extra round trip. The count stays in the widget's State, so accepting/dismissing a card (which shrinks the provider list) keeps the reveal depth instead of collapsing back to 10; _visible is clamped to the list length. Presentation-only. Server-side paging is the upgrade if the pending set ever grows past a few hundred, at which point the RPC needs limit/offset. analyze clean on lib/features/reporting.

2026-07-27 -- HR ↔ Staff hiring flow simplified in 4 presentation-only phases; no data/domain/migration change and both employee RPC payloads byte-identical (verified: the only changed param line in the whole diff is `'p_branch_id': branch.id` → `branchId`). (1) LOGIN BADGE: new `employeeLoginUi(String? userId)` record mapper in hr_ui.dart (mirrors staff_ui.dart's inviteStatusUi shape) → AppPill, lumen not success because the employee-status pill next to it is already green. Rendered on employee_card's SUBTITLE line, not the name row: at 360px the card's left column is ~109px and both the mono employee code and AppPill are unshrinkable, so a pill there collapses Flexible(name) to 0 and throws RenderFlex overflowed — the subtitle already has maxLines:1+ellipsis so it degrades instead. Positive-only on the list (a grey "No login" on every row of a mostly-no-login list is noise); BOTH states on the profile, where "Create login" makes the negative actionable. Deliberately NOT wrapped in PermissionGate — Employee.userId is already authorised by the RLS-scoped query, every existing gate in this repo wraps an action not information, and gating would mount a permissionMatrixProvider watch per row; consequence is that roles lacking users:create can now SEE who has a login while still being unable to create one, which is the actual fix (previously the gated button's absence was the only signal). Zero backend: user_id was already in _empCols → employee_model → entity, read by exactly one widget. KNOWN LIMIT, not fixed: employees.user_id is written by consume_staff_invite at REDEMPTION, so a sent-but-unredeemed invite still reads "No login", and there is a window where the row is linked while the account is AWAITING_CONFIRMATION (unusable); surfacing invite-pending needs staff_invites joined onto the employee list = backend, out of scope. (2) FORM RESTRUCTURE: employee_form_page 5 cards → 3 (Basic info / Role & pay / Additional details), local `_grid` + `_LabelledField` (~50 lines) deleted for the shared AppFieldRow/AppFieldLabel + the 6-line `_labeled` helper the product form already uses; hardcoded `'Full name *'`/`'Base salary *'` label strings → `isRequired: true` (also fixes a live a11y defect — screen readers announced "Full name star"), employee code gained the asterisk it always enforced. Joining date + salary type deliberately NOT asterisked, per the 2026-07-26 rule that an asterisk on an unvalidated field is a lie — both default silently (today / MONTHLY) and neither is checked. New `_requiredFieldError()` keeps BOTH the emptiness and the double.tryParse range checks: degrading it to the product form's isEmpty-only shape would let 'abc' through to coalesce(p_base_salary, 0) and create an employee on zero pay with a success toast. Branch resolution hoisted above the `_saving = true` flip so a missing branch no longer flashes the spinner. Error banner deliberately LEFT at the bottom (the product form puts it on top only because it has a sticky bottomBar; this form's save button is an inline Row directly under the banner, and AppDetailScaffold exposes no scroll controller to reach a top banner). Employee code / CNIC / joining date stay create-only — update_employee has no parameter for any of them, so an editable field would discard the edit and still toast success. (3) BRANCH PREFILL: /staff/invite extra + router destructure gained `branchId` (backward compatible — the other two callers pass no extra); seeded in initState, NOT in the branch list's `data:` builder, which re-runs on every setState (role tap, expiry change) and would silently re-tick a branch the user just unticked. `valid` and the submit payload now intersect _branchIds with the ids the picker is actually SHOWING: listBranches() returns every tenant branch while create_staff_invite's GUARD 4 requires the caller be assigned to each, and employees are readable tenant-wide — so a Branch-A manager opening a Branch-B employee would otherwise get an enabled button with nothing ticked and a raw "branch not assigned". (4) INLINE LOGIN: create-mode-only "Also create an app login" AppCheckbox outside the section cards (it changes what Save does, it isn't part of the record), PermissionGate(users:create) with the spacing inside the gate so ungated roles get no orphan gap, plus a footnote when the employee has no email (invites without one still work via QR). On success: capture GoRouter, then go(profile) then push(invite) — pushReplacement would strand Back on the employees list, a bare push leaves the filled form alive underneath (change the code, re-tap Create, second employee), and go(invite) leaves a dead back button since AppDetailScaffold pops. NOT DONE, deliberately: the "Staff" → "App access" label rename (user chose to keep Staff), and merging features/staff into features/hr — the audit proved zero cross-imports either way, so the split costs nothing and a merge is 21 files of churn for identical behaviour. Two PRE-EXISTING bugs found and left alone: update_employee COALESCEs every param so clearing an optional field in edit mode is a silent no-op (needs a SQL sentinel), and /hr/employees/:id/edit with a null `extra` renders a CREATE form (currently unreachable — the splash redirect discards deep links). analyze 8/0 (baseline 8, 0 new, 0 in any touched file); flutter test 13 pass / 2 fail, both pre-existing Supabase-bootstrap failures.

2026-07-27 -- TOOLING GOTCHA: do NOT run `dart format` on this repo. The codebase is written in the pre-3.7 "short" dart style; the installed formatter is the 3.7+ "tall" style, so `dart format` rewrites 459 of 836 files. Formatting a single touched file therefore buries the real change in unrelated churn -- caught on this task when a ONE-LINE router.dart edit came out as a 146-line diff (and employee_profile_page as 196 lines for 10 real ones). Files were reverted and the edits re-applied by hand in the repo's existing style; diff went 1611 -> ~470 lines. Match surrounding style manually. Reformatting the whole repo to tall style is a legitimate future task, but it must be its own commit touching nothing else.

2026-07-27 -- FIX (SECURITY): a CASHIER saw every module in the desktop rail. NOT a desktop/mobile divergence -- verified the rail and the bottom bar consume the same permission-filtered list (`_branchMapFor` is the only filter, and `_allItems` is referenced in exactly 3 places: its declaration plus two identical `[for (final b in branchMap) _allItems[b]]` lines). NOT bad data either -- the tenant's CASHIER role holds the seeded 6 permissions, and every module migration's permission seeder correctly guards `if NEW.name = 'ADMIN'` / `where r.name = 'ADMIN'`. ROOT CAUSE = a stale in-memory permission matrix surviving an account switch. `permissionMatrixProvider` is a plain (non-autoDispose) NotifierProvider, and `resetUserScopedState()` (app_flow_state.dart) resets only WorkspaceInitState/PinLockState/MfaState/RecoveryState + the stored branch id -- it cannot reach Riverpod, and there was no `ref.invalidate(permissionMatrixProvider)` anywhere in lib/. Sequence: admin session loads 36 keys -> sign out leaves them in memory -> sign in as cashier -> router sends to /workspace-init -> the screen registers an EDGE-triggered `ref.listen(profileControllerProvider)` -> but `permissionsReadyProvider` is just `!isLoading && !hasError`, which is already true on the stale AsyncData, so `_complete()` fires post-frame, the router releases to /dashboard and the screen unmounts BEFORE the cashier's profile ever emits -> `load(cashierRoleId)` is never called and the admin matrix persists for the whole session. `userBranchesProvider` had the identical shape (same edge-triggered load, same non-autoDispose survival), so the previous user's branch list and `currentBranchProvider` survived too -- every `create_*` RPC would then have been called with a branch the new user may not be assigned to (server-side `auth_has_branch` was the only thing catching it). FIX, presentation/state layer only, no schema change: (1) new `core/state/reset_on_user_change.dart` -- `resetOnUserChange(ref, cb)`, deliberately shaped like the existing `subscribeReload(ref, table, cb)` helper; it watches `supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange` and fires ONLY on a real uid change, so token refreshes and `initialSession` (uid unchanged) never clear a live session. (2) PermissionController and UserBranchesController each clear themselves through it (matrix -> AsyncLoading + `_roleId = null`; branches -> AsyncLoading + `_userId = null` + `currentBranchProvider` set to null). (3) Both gained a LEVEL-triggered `ensureLoadedFor(id)` called from workspace_init_screen's build alongside the existing listener -- this is REQUIRED, not belt-and-braces: with (2) alone, a profile that resolves before the screen mounts would leave the matrix stuck on AsyncLoading forever and hang the app at /workspace-init, i.e. the fix would have converted a wrong-rail bug into a boot hang. `ensureLoadedFor` no-ops when the id is already loaded or in flight (load() sets the id synchronously) and retries on error. ALSO HARDENED, same pass: inventory / purchasing / accounting hub pages were `StatelessWidget`s with hardcoded tile lists and zero permission awareness, reachable by deep link (the inventory one from every cashier, since branch 1 is intentionally ungated) -- each now wraps its scroll view in the same page-level `PermissionGate` + fallback copy that reports_hub_page already used (`inventory:read` / `purchase:read` / `accounting:read`; ADMIN holds all three so nothing changes for them). NOT CHANGED, deliberately: the 4 hardcoded nav branches (Dashboard, Inventory, Assistant, Settings) are documented choices; `_ModuleDest` sub-rail entries stay ungated because inventing keys (e.g. gating Returns on `sales:delete`) would hide destinations from users who legitimately have them today; and `_ModuleRail`'s `selected == -1` case renders every entry of an ALREADY-FILTERED list, so it is cosmetic, not a leak. STILL OPEN, needs its own reviewed migration -- read RLS is tenant-scoped only, never permission-scoped (verified: `create policy "accounts tenant read" on public.accounts for select to authenticated using (tenant_id = public.auth_tenant_id())`, no `auth_has_permission`), while writes ARE gated; so a deep-linked cashier can still READ a module's data, and every read gate in the app is client-side only. Route-level authorisation is likewise absent (`_redirect` has no permission logic; 3 of ~150 routes gate their builder). analyze 8/0 (baseline 8, 0 new); flutter test 13 pass / 2 fail, both pre-existing. VERIFY OWED (needs two accounts): sign in as ADMIN, sign out, sign in as CASHIER WITHOUT quitting -> rail must show 7 items (Dashboard, Inventory, Sales, Customers, Reports, Assistant, Settings), and must not hang on /workspace-init.

2026-07-27 -- FIX (SECURITY, follow-up to the stale-matrix entry above): after the RBAC fix the nav was correct, but the DASHBOARD still showed the signed-out user's figures until a manual refresh. Same root disease, wider blast radius: ~80 controllers are plain (non-autoDispose) Notifier/AsyncNotifier providers whose `build()` runs once and caches for the process lifetime, so EVERY one of them serves the previous session's data to the next user on first view -- dashboard totals, POS cart lines, customer lists, invoices, communication logs. Within one tenant that is a stale screen; across two tenants it is a cross-tenant disclosure. REJECTED FIXES, with reasons: (a) invalidating a hand-maintained provider list (the `tab_reload.dart` pattern) -- 82 providers today, two of them duplicate names (`bankAccountsProvider` in accounting + settings, `customersProvider` in customers + sales) needing import aliases, and it silently rots the day someone adds the 83rd controller; (b) per-controller `resetOnUserChange` -- same incompleteness, and it only fires for providers that happen to be ALIVE at the switch; (c) keying the root `ProviderScope` -- that rebuilds `MaterialApp.router`, and GoRouter holds an internal navigator GlobalKey, which is precisely the duplicate-key crash this project has already hit once; (d) a nested `ProviderScope` -- does not isolate, because without overrides providers still initialise in the root container. CHOSEN: `core/state/session_scope.dart` -- `SessionScope` owns a `ProviderContainer` and hands it to an `UncontrolledProviderScope`; on a real auth uid change it swaps in a fresh container and disposes the old one in a post-frame callback. That discards every cached provider at once (and closes their realtime channels via the existing `ref.onDispose` hooks) while the widget tree stays MOUNTED -- dependents merely re-read from the new container, so `MaterialApp.router` is never torn down and the GlobalKey hazard never arises. `main.dart` now runs `SessionScope(child: App())` instead of `ProviderScope(child: App())`. Guards on a real uid change only, so token refreshes and `initialSession` leave a live session untouched. CONSEQUENCE: the `resetOnUserChange` helper added earlier today is strictly weaker than this (same stream, same condition, but it required the provider to be alive) so it and all 7 of its call sites were DELETED rather than left as a second, redundant mechanism -- one reset path, not two. The `ensureLoadedFor` additions to PermissionController/UserBranchesController STAY: they fix a different bug (workspace-init's edge-triggered `ref.listen` missing an emission that happened before it mounted) and they are what re-populates the now-empty container. VERIFIED: `flutter build macos --debug` clean, app launched via Finder and stayed up on both the pre- and post-simplification builds; analyze 8/0; flutter test 13 pass / 2 fail (both pre-existing). VERIFY OWED (needs two accounts): sign in as A, sign out, sign in as B without quitting -- B's dashboard, POS cart and lists must be B's on first paint, with no manual refresh.
